# Lort Phase 1B: Web App Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans (recommended) or superpowers:subagent-driven-development (can be usage intensive) to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `web/`, the Next.js app for Lort's public board (feedback list, request pages, accounts, Suggest something) and its dashboard (sign-in, invites, requests table, request page with triage and merge, and Settings → Branding, Boards, Tags and Team), plus the few `/api/lort` endpoints the pages need.

**Architecture:** Next.js 16 (App Router, `output: "standalone"`) runs behind the Go binary from Phase 1A, which proxies every page request to it on `127.0.0.1:3000` and adds an `X-Lort-Config` header with the brand. Server components only read that header (to apply the owner's colour and name before first paint); all data loads in the browser through the PocketBase JS SDK and TanStack Query, against the same origin, so every page shows its own skeleton while it loads. Business rules stay in Go. The UI is shadcn/ui (Base UI flavour, added with the CLI and then restyled) plus Lort's own components in `components/lort`, all styled with Tailwind v4 utilities from the design tokens in `app/globals.css`; Motion handles the sticker slap, the sliding tab pill and toasts.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27 + PocketBase v0.40.5 (server additions); Next.js 16.4, React 19.3, TypeScript 5, Tailwind CSS 4.3, shadcn CLI 4.21 (`base-nova`: Base UI 1.9), `cn` 0.4, Tabler icons (`@tabler/icons-react`), Motion 14, TanStack Query 5, PocketBase JS SDK 0.28, dnd-kit (`@dnd-kit/core` 6 + `@dnd-kit/sortable` 10), Sonner 2, next-themes; Vitest 5 + Testing Library + jsdom; Playwright 1.64 + `@axe-core/playwright`; pnpm.

**Spec:** `.superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-lort-design.md`, sections 3 (data), 4 (triage, merge), 5.1–5.4 and 5.6–5.7 (public site), 6.1, 6.2 and 6.6 Branding/Boards/Tags/Team (dashboard), 11 (errors), 14–16 (visual system and page layouts). Design tokens and rules: `DESIGN.md`. Approved page designs: `design/canvas/*.dc.html` (README explains the markup) and the canvas artifact https://claude.ai/artifact/EYjbK9CRgK2XvR1gFQov1v. Component reference: `design/components/src/*.tsx` and `design/components/lort.css`.

This is plan 2 of the Phase 1 trio: 1A server (shipped), **1B web (this)**, 1C packaging (Dockerfile, setup scripts, CI).

## How to use the designs

The canvases and `design/components` are a designer's hand-off, like a Figma file: match them visually, don't copy their code. They are plain HTML with inline styles and `lt-` classes because the canvas can't run component libraries or Motion. In this app:

- Every control is a shadcn component added with `pnpm dlx shadcn@latest add …` and then restyled in `components/ui/*` to the tokens. Never write a shadcn component from memory, and never hand-roll an `<input className="my-field">` with its own CSS.
- Lort-specific pieces (status icons, the vote sticker, chips, the composer, the bars) live in `components/lort/*` and are built from shadcn parts plus Tailwind utilities.
- Styles are Tailwind utilities that read the tokens (`bg-surface-2`, `text-status-planned`, `shadow-float`, `inset-shadow-sm`). Use `@layer components` with `@apply` only for a class that repeats in many files and can't be a component; this plan needs none.
- Read the canvas for layout, spacing, sizes and copy. When the spec and a canvas disagree on layout, the canvas wins; on behaviour, the spec wins.

## Global Constraints

- Working directory for web tasks: `web/` (a sibling of `server/`). Package manager: pnpm. Node 20+.
- Scaffold, exactly: `pnpm dlx shadcn@latest init -t next -n web -b base -p nova --no-monorepo -y` (verified with shadcn 4.21.5; `-p base-nova` is not a valid preset).
- `components.json` has `"iconLibrary": "tabler"` before any component is added. Icons: Tabler outline, 2px stroke (`@tabler/icons-react`); `lucide-react` is removed. Statuses and stickers use Lort's own SVG components. No Unicode glyphs or emoji as icons.
- shadcn components only come from the CLI. If `pnpm dlx shadcn@latest add` fails (it needs `ui.shadcn.com`), stop and report it. Never write the component by hand.
- After every `shadcn add`, run `pnpm ui:fix` (Task 4) so generated components import `cn` from `@/lib/utils`, never from `"cn"`.
- Base UI, not Radix: triggers compose through `render={<Button … />}`, not `asChild`; menu items use `onClick`, not `onSelect` (the one exception is `CommandItem`, which is cmdk, not Base UI, and does take `onSelect`); `Tabs onValueChange(value, details)`; `ToggleGroup value: string[]`. Check a generated component's props in `components/ui/` before using it.
- Merge classes only with `cn()`. Never build a class string with a template literal: Prettier's Tailwind plugin trims whitespace inside className strings, so `` `a${x ? ' b' : ''}` `` becomes `"ab"`.
- Statuses, exactly: `pending`, `reviewing`, `planned`, `progress`, `completed`, `closed`; labels Pending, Reviewing, Planned, In progress, Completed, Closed. Open statuses (default filter): the first four.
- Status Label Rule: a status is icon + label in ink, or label text in its status colour. Never both, never neither. An icon on its own (the requests table) has a tooltip and an `aria-label`.
- Owner's Colour Rule: `primary` only for live states (voted, selected, primary action). One primary button per view. No filled red buttons.
- Copy is sentence case, friendly and short; public pages speak for "the team"; "Lort" appears on public pages only in the footer and the optional card. Errors name the problem and the fix.
- Features switched off are absent, never disabled. In 1B email, voice and Turnstile are always off (`config.features`), so there is no verify step, no reset-by-email, no notification switches and no mic.
- Accessibility: WCAG 2.2 AA in both themes; full keyboard use; visible 3px focus ring with 3px offset (the global `:focus-visible` rule in `globals.css`; don't add `outline-none` to focusable elements); `prefers-reduced-motion` respected (`MotionConfig reducedMotion="user"` plus the global CSS rule).
- Theme follows the visitor's system setting; there is no theme switch and no keyboard shortcut for it.
- Images: at most 3 per request or comment, 5 MB each, PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF. Protected request and comment images need a PocketBase file token when the request isn't public.
- Rate-limited actions answer 429; show "Slow down a little. Try again in a minute." in a toast.
- `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint` and `pnpm test` stay clean after every task. The scaffold's ESLint has React 19's hook rules: don't call `setState` synchronously inside an effect, and don't read refs during render.
- Go: `gofmt` every file, `go vet ./...` clean, no secrets in logs.

## Decisions this plan makes (to record in the spec in the docs commit)

1. **Data loads in the browser.** Next never calls PocketBase from the server. The only server-side input is `X-Lort-Config` (brand, public boards, tags, features, version), which the Go proxy always overwrites. That keeps PocketBase auth in the browser (where the SDK keeps it) and lets every page render its real shell with its own skeleton.
2. **Two PocketBase clients.** `pb` holds the board user's session (`localStorage` key `lort_user`); `pbAdmin` holds the superuser's (`lort_admin`). When an admin opens the dashboard, `GET /api/lort/team/me` returns a token for their linked board profile, which is saved into `pb`: on the dashboard and the public board they comment, vote and suggest as that profile, with the Team badge.
3. **Auth happens in dialogs**, not pages: the account dialog (sign up / sign in) opens from the top bar, from a sticker click, and inside the Suggest modal. The only auth pages are `/reset-password` (the admin's "Copy password reset link") and the dashboard's `/dashboard/login` and `/dashboard/invite`.
4. **Navigation shows only pages that exist.** The public nav shows Feedback; Roadmap and Changelog join in Phase 2. The dashboard sidebar shows Requests and Settings; Roadmap and Changelog join in Phase 2, Docs in Phase 6. Settings shows only Branding, Boards, Tags and Team.
5. **"Get your own board like this"** links to `https://github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort` until `/lort-landing` exists (Phase 6). It sits bottom-left, as on the canvas (the spec said bottom-right; the canvas wins on layout).
6. **New server endpoints** (Tasks 1–3): `GET /api/lort/requests/{id}` (the whole request page; the comment list is `thread`), `POST /api/lort/requests/{id}/board`, `POST /api/lort/requests/{id}/author` (post on behalf of an email), `GET /api/lort/queue` (Skip and "3 of 12"), `GET /api/lort/stats`, `POST /api/lort/order/{boards|tags}`, `DELETE /api/lort/boards/{id}?moveTo=`. When the team makes a request visible, it stops awaiting approval.
7. **Status chip menu** offers the same moves as the triage bar (minus Merge and Skip), so the server's transition rules are the only rules.
8. **The Skip queue** is the requests table's current filters, carried in the request page's URL.
9. **Primary-soft** is mixed in CSS from `--primary` (`color-mix`), so only `--primary`, `--primary-foreground` and `--primary-text` are injected.

## Known traps (each one cost a previous attempt real time)

- **PocketBase auth in React.** `LocalAuthStore` returns a new record object on every read, so `useSyncExternalStore(…, () => authStore.record)` loops forever. The snapshot is `JSON.stringify(record)`, parsed with `useMemo` (Task 8, with a test that signs in, renames and signs out).
- **`cn` drops Lort's text sizes** unless configured: `cn("text-label", "text-primary-foreground")` treats `text-label` as a colour and keeps only the colour. `lib/utils.ts` extends `cn` with Lort's text sizes and shadows, and a test fails if any component imports `cn` from `"cn"` (the shadcn CLI writes that import; `pnpm ui:fix` rewrites it).
- **Admin session renewal.** The dashboard fetches `/api/lort/team/me` on load. Sign out only when it answers 401 or 403. An aborted request (status 0, the admin navigated away) or a network blip must not end the session.
- **Stale lists.** Realtime refetches are debounced by 300 ms and cancelled on navigation, so after creating or changing anything, invalidate the affected TanStack queries yourself; after a sign-in or sign-out invalidate everything (votes and visibility depend on who is signed in).
- **PocketBase SDK auto-cancellation** aborts parallel identical requests. Both clients turn it off; TanStack Query dedupes instead.
- **Rate limits in e2e.** Sign-up is limited to 3 per minute per IP. Playwright's global setup signs in as the superuser and PATCHes `/api/settings` with `{"rateLimits":{"enabled":false}}`, then seeds boards and tags.
- **Playwright URLs.** After a click that navigates, `await page.waitForURL(…)` before reading `page.url()`. Run the suite with `--repeat-each=2`.
- **Duplicate JSON keys in Go.** `requests.Detail` embeds `Item`, whose `comments` is the count; the comment list is `thread`. Go silently drops one of two same-named fields.
- **Supervisor log.** Log the restart backoff as `backoff.String()`; PocketBase's log store can't encode a `time.Duration` and drops the line.
- **Template-literal classes.** Prettier's Tailwind plugin trims inside className strings. Always `cn()`.
- **Base UI APIs.** `render`, not `asChild`; `onClick`, not `onSelect`.
- **Protected images.** Images on hidden requests need `pb.files.getToken()` (or `pbAdmin`'s) before building their URLs.
- **`outline-none` hides the focus ring.** The generated components use `outline-none` plus a `ring` for focus; the restyle removes both from focusable controls so the global 3px outline shows.
- **Coral is nudged.** Of the 8 colour presets, Coral `#d4553a` reaches 4.5:1 with neither white nor ink, so `deriveBrand` moves its lightness slightly. That's the spec's rule working, not a bug.
- **Windows.** `server/internal/web/web_test.go` built its `sh -c` command with a backslash path; Task 3 uses `filepath.ToSlash`, so the Go suite passes on Windows (Git Bash) too.

```
server/                                   (Phase 1A, extended)
  internal/requests/list.go               List/Queue share filter() and order()
  internal/requests/detail.go             Detail: one request page (thread, activity, mergedInto)

  internal/web/web.go                     proxy sets X-Lort-Config; backoff logged as a string
  internal/api/api.go                     + detail, board, author, queue, stats, order, board delete
web/
  app/
    globals.css                           tokens → shadcn variables and Tailwind theme

    lort/                                 design-system components

    request/                              the shared request view
    dashboard/                            dashboard pieces (settings/ inside)
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Give test users a name, like the sign-up hook does**

In `server/internal/testutil/testutil.go`, add `"strings"` to the imports and change `User`:

```go
// User creates a verified user named like the sign-up hook would: the part before the @.
func User(t testing.TB, app core.App, email string) *core.Record {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := app.FindCollectionByNameOrId("users")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r := core.NewRecord(c)
	r.SetEmail(email)
	r.SetPassword("correcthorse")
	r.SetVerified(true)
	r.Set("name", strings.Split(email, "@")[0])
	if err := app.Save(r); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return r
}
```

```go
package requests_test

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"

	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/requests"
	"github.com/hacker4ofakind/lort/server/internal/testutil"

func comment(t *testing.T, app core.App, req, author *core.Record, body, parent string, internal bool) *core.Record {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := app.FindCollectionByNameOrId("comments")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r := core.NewRecord(c)
	r.Set("request", req.Id)
	r.Set("author", author.Id)
	r.Set("body", body)
	r.Set("parent", parent)
	r.Set("internal", internal)
	if err := app.Save(r); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return r
}

func TestDetailThreadsCommentsAndHidesInternalNotes(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	r := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Dark mode", "reviewing", 3)
	devon := testutil.User(t, app, "devon@example.com")
	top := comment(t, app, r, devon, "Same on iOS", "", false)
	comment(t, app, r, mara, "Thanks devon", top.Id, false)
	comment(t, app, r, mara, "Reproduced it", "", true)

	pub, err := svc.Detail(r.Id, nil, false)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !pub.Public || pub.Votes != 3 || len(pub.Thread) != 1 || len(pub.Thread[0].Replies) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("public detail = %+v", pub)
	}
	if pub.Comments != 2 || pub.Thread[0].Author.Name != "devon" {
		t.Errorf("comments = %d, author = %q", pub.Comments, pub.Thread[0].Author.Name)
	}
	team, err := svc.Detail(r.Id, nil, true)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(team.Thread) != 2 || !team.Thread[1].Internal || team.Comments != 3 {
		t.Errorf("team thread = %+v, comments = %d", team.Thread, team.Comments)
	}
}

func TestDetailVisibility(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	devon := testutil.User(t, app, "devon@example.com")
	r := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Waiting one", "pending", 1)
	r.Set("visible", false)
	if err := app.Save(r); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Detail(r.Id, nil, false); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("anonymous err = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Detail(r.Id, devon, false); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("other user err = %v", err)
	}
	if d, err := svc.Detail(r.Id, mara, false); err != nil || d.Public {
		t.Errorf("author detail = %+v, %v", d, err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Detail(r.Id, nil, true); err != nil {
		t.Errorf("team err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestDetailMergedIntoAndTimeline(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	private := testutil.Board(t, app, "Internal", false, false)
	src := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Night theme", "pending", 0)
	dst := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Dark mode", "planned", 0)
	secret := testutil.Request(t, app, private, mara, "Secret", "pending", 0)
	other := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Dusk theme", "pending", 0)
	if err := svc.Merge(src.Id, dst.Id, mara); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := svc.Merge(other.Id, secret.Id, mara); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	d, err := svc.Detail(src.Id, nil, false)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if d.MergedInto == nil || d.MergedInto.Name != "Dark mode" {
		t.Fatalf("mergedInto = %+v", d.MergedInto)
	}
	if len(d.Activity) != 1 || d.Activity[0].Kind != "merged_in" || d.Activity[0].Ref == nil ||
		d.Activity[0].Ref.ID != dst.Id || d.Activity[0].Actor.Name != "mara" {
		t.Errorf("activity = %+v", d.Activity)
	}
	o, err := svc.Detail(other.Id, nil, false)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if o.MergedInto != nil || o.Activity[0].Ref != nil {
		t.Errorf("a private target must not leak: %+v %+v", o.MergedInto, o.Activity[0].Ref)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Share the list filter**

In `server/internal/requests/list.go`, replace the body of `List` between the paging defaults and the `total` count (the `where := …` block and `base := …` line) with `base := s.filter(q, viewer, team)`, delete the `order := map…` block, and use `OrderBy(order(q.Sort)...)`. `List` becomes:

```go
func (s *Service) List(q Query, viewer *core.Record, team bool) (Page, error) {
	if q.PerPage <= 0 || q.PerPage > 100 {
		q.PerPage = 20
	}
	if q.Page < 1 {
		q.Page = 1
	}
	base := s.filter(q, viewer, team)

	var total int
	if err := base.Select("count(*)").Row(&total); err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}

	var ids []string
	err := base.Select("r.id").OrderBy(order(q.Sort)...).Limit(int64(q.PerPage)).Offset(int64((q.Page - 1) * q.PerPage)).Column(&ids)
	if err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	items, err := s.items(ids, viewer, team)
	if err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	return Page{Items: items, Page: q.Page, PerPage: q.PerPage, TotalItems: total}, nil
}

// filter is the FROM and WHERE shared by List and Queue.
func (s *Service) filter(q Query, viewer *core.Record, team bool) *dbx.SelectQuery {
	where := []dbx.Expression{dbx.HashExp{"r.mergedInto": ""}}
	if !team {
		public := dbx.And(dbx.HashExp{"r.visible": true}, dbx.NewExp("b.isPublic = 1"))
		if viewer != nil {
			public = dbx.Or(public, dbx.HashExp{"r.author": viewer.Id})
		}
		where = append(where, public)
	} else {
		switch q.Visibility {
		case "public":
			where = append(where, dbx.HashExp{"r.visible": true})
		case "hidden":
			where = append(where, dbx.HashExp{"r.visible": false})
		}
	}
	if len(q.Statuses) > 0 {
		where = append(where, dbx.In("r.status", anys(q.Statuses)...))
	}
	if len(q.Boards) > 0 {
		where = append(where, dbx.In("r.board", anys(q.Boards)...))
	}
	if len(q.Tags) > 0 {
		ors := make([]dbx.Expression, 0, len(q.Tags))
		for i, tag := range q.Tags {
			p := "tag" + string(rune('a'+i))
			ors = append(ors, dbx.NewExp("EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM json_each(r.tags) WHERE value = {:"+p+"})", dbx.Params{p: tag}))
		}
		where = append(where, dbx.Or(ors...))
	}
	if m := ftsQuery(q.Search); m != "" {
		where = append(where, dbx.NewExp("r.rowid IN (SELECT rowid FROM requests_fts WHERE requests_fts MATCH {:fts})", dbx.Params{"fts": m}))
	}
	return s.App.DB().Select().From("requests r").InnerJoin("boards b", dbx.NewExp("b.id = r.board")).Where(dbx.And(where...))
}

// order is the ORDER BY for a sort name; trending is the default.
func order(sort string) []string {
	switch sort {
	case "newest":
		return []string{"r.created DESC"}
	case "oldest":
		return []string{"r.created ASC"}
	case "top":
		return []string{"r.upvoteCount DESC", "r.created DESC"}
	case "updated":
		return []string{"r.updated DESC"}
	}
	return []string{"r.trendScore DESC", "r.created DESC"}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `Detail`**

Create `server/internal/requests/detail.go`:

```go
package requests

import (
	"time"

	"github.com/pocketbase/dbx"
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
)

// Comment is one comment on a request page, with its replies (one level deep).
type Comment struct {
	ID       string    `json:"id"`
	Body     string    `json:"body"`
	Images   []string  `json:"images"`
	Internal bool      `json:"internal"`
	Author   Person    `json:"author"`
	Created  time.Time `json:"created"`
	Replies  []Comment `json:"replies"`
}

// Event is one timeline entry: created, status_changed, merged_in, merged_from or board_changed.
type Event struct {
	ID      string    `json:"id"`
	Kind    string    `json:"kind"`
	Actor   Person    `json:"actor"`
	From    string    `json:"from"`
	To      string    `json:"to"`
	Ref     *Ref      `json:"ref"` // the other request of a merge, when the viewer may see it
	Created time.Time `json:"created"`
}

// Detail is everything a request page shows, in one response. The comment list is called
// thread because Item already has a comments field (the count); Go's JSON encoder silently
// drops one of two fields with the same name.
type Detail struct {
	Item
	Images     []string  `json:"images"`
	Public     bool      `json:"public"` // visible on a public board, so its images load without a file token
	MergedInto *Ref      `json:"mergedInto"`
	Thread     []Comment `json:"thread"`
	Activity   []Event   `json:"activity"`
}

// Detail loads a request page. viewer may be nil. team is the dashboard's view: any request,
// internal comments included. Anyone else gets ErrNotFound for a request they may not see.
func (s *Service) Detail(id string, viewer *core.Record, team bool) (Detail, error) {
	r, err := s.find(s.App, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Detail{}, err
	}
	public, err := isPublic(s.App, r)
	if err != nil {
		return Detail{}, err
	}
	if !team && !public {
		if viewer == nil {
			return Detail{}, ErrNotFound
		}
		if ok, err := canSee(s.App, r, viewer.Id); err != nil || !ok {
			return Detail{}, ErrNotFound
		}
	}
	items, err := s.items([]string{r.Id}, viewer, team)
	if err != nil || len(items) == 0 {
		return Detail{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	d := Detail{Item: items[0], Images: r.GetStringSlice("images"), Public: public}
	if target := r.GetString("mergedInto"); target != "" {
		d.MergedInto = s.visibleRef(target, viewer, team)
	}
	if d.Thread, err = s.thread(r.Id, team); err != nil {
		return Detail{}, err
	}
	if d.Activity, err = s.activity(r.Id, viewer, team); err != nil {
		return Detail{}, err
	}
	return d, nil
}

// isPublic reports whether anyone may see r: visible and on a public board.
func isPublic(app core.App, r *core.Record) (bool, error) {
	if !r.GetBool("visible") {
		return false, nil
	}
	b, err := app.FindRecordById("boards", r.GetString("board"))
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	return b.GetBool("isPublic"), nil
}

// visibleRef returns the id and title of request id, or nil if the viewer may not see it.
func (s *Service) visibleRef(id string, viewer *core.Record, team bool) *Ref {
	r, err := s.find(s.App, id)
	if err != nil {
		return nil
	}
	if !team {
		public, err := isPublic(s.App, r)
		if err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		if !public {
			if viewer == nil {
				return nil
			}
			if ok, err := canSee(s.App, r, viewer.Id); err != nil || !ok {
				return nil
			}
		}
	}
	return &Ref{ID: r.Id, Name: r.GetString("title")}
}

func person(r *core.Record) Person {
	if r == nil {
		return Person{}
	}
	return Person{ID: r.Id, Name: r.GetString("name"), Avatar: r.GetString("avatar"), Team: r.GetBool("isAdmin")}
}

// thread returns the request's comments oldest first, replies nested under their parent.
func (s *Service) thread(requestID string, team bool) ([]Comment, error) {
	filter := "request = {:r}"
	if !team {
		filter += " && internal = false"
	}
	recs, err := s.App.FindRecordsByFilter("comments", filter, "created", 0, 0, dbx.Params{"r": requestID})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if errs := s.App.ExpandRecords(recs, []string{"author"}, nil); len(errs) > 0 {
		for _, e := range errs {
			return nil, e
		}
	}
	top := []Comment{}
	replies := map[string][]Comment{}
	for _, c := range recs {
		cm := Comment{
			ID: c.Id, Body: c.GetString("body"), Images: c.GetStringSlice("images"), Internal: c.GetBool("internal"),
			Author: person(c.ExpandedOne("author")), Created: c.GetDateTime("created").Time(), Replies: []Comment{},
		}
		if p := c.GetString("parent"); p != "" {
			replies[p] = append(replies[p], cm)
		} else {
			top = append(top, cm)
		}
	}
	for i := range top {
		if rs, ok := replies[top[i].ID]; ok {
			top[i].Replies = rs
		}
	}
	return top, nil
}

// activity returns the request's timeline entries oldest first.
func (s *Service) activity(requestID string, viewer *core.Record, team bool) ([]Event, error) {
	recs, err := s.App.FindRecordsByFilter("activity", "request = {:r}", "created", 0, 0, dbx.Params{"r": requestID})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if errs := s.App.ExpandRecords(recs, []string{"actor"}, nil); len(errs) > 0 {
		for _, e := range errs {
			return nil, e
		}
	}
	out := make([]Event, 0, len(recs))
	for _, a := range recs {
		ev := Event{
			ID: a.Id, Kind: a.GetString("kind"), From: a.GetString("from"), To: a.GetString("to"),
			Actor: person(a.ExpandedOne("actor")), Created: a.GetDateTime("created").Time(),
		}
		if ev.Actor.Name == "" {
			ev.Actor.Name = a.GetString("actorName")
		}
		switch ev.Kind {
		case "merged_in":
			ev.Ref = s.visibleRef(ev.To, viewer, team)
		case "merged_from":
			ev.Ref = s.visibleRef(ev.From, viewer, team)
		}
		out = append(out, ev)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```go
// viewer is who is looking at a request page: a board user, or a superuser's team profile
// (team = true, the dashboard's view).
func viewer(e *core.RequestEvent) (*core.Record, bool, error) {
	switch {
	case e.Auth == nil:
		return nil, false, nil
	case e.HasSuperuserAuth():
		p, err := actor(e)
		return p, true, err
	case e.Auth.Collection().Name == "users":
		return e.Auth, false, nil
	}
	return nil, false, nil
}

func (d Deps) detail(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	who, team, err := viewer(e)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	det, err := d.Svc.Detail(e.Request.PathValue("id"), who, team)
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, det)
}
```

```go
func TestMoveBoardLogsTheChange(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	bugs := testutil.Board(t, app, "Bugs", true, false)
	r := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Crash on paste", "pending", 0)
	if err := svc.MoveBoard(r.Id, bugs.Id, mara); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r, _ = app.FindRecordById("requests", r.Id)
	if r.GetString("board") != bugs.Id {
		t.Fatalf("board = %s", r.GetString("board"))
	}
	a, err := app.FindFirstRecordByFilter("activity", "request = {:r} && kind = 'board_changed'", dbx.Params{"r": r.Id})
	if err != nil || a.GetString("from") != "Features" || a.GetString("to") != "Bugs" {
		t.Fatalf("activity = %v, %v", a, err)
	}
	if err := svc.MoveBoard(r.Id, "missing", mara); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrNoBoard) {
		t.Errorf("unknown board err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestDeleteBoardMovesRequestsFirst(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	bugs := testutil.Board(t, app, "Bugs", true, false)
	r := testutil.Request(t, app, bugs, mara, "Crash on paste", "pending", 0)
	if err := svc.DeleteBoard(bugs.Id, "", mara); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrBoardNotEmpty) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}

		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := app.FindRecordById("boards", bugs.Id); err == nil {
		t.Error("board still exists")
	}
	r, _ = app.FindRecordById("requests", r.Id)
	if r.GetString("board") != board.Id {
		t.Errorf("request board = %s", r.GetString("board"))
	}
	if err := svc.DeleteBoard(board.Id, "", mara); !errors.Is(err, requests.ErrLastBoard) {
		t.Errorf("last board err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestStatsAndQueue(t *testing.T) {
	svc, app, board, mara := setup(t)
	a := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Alpha", "pending", 9)
	b := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Bravo", "reviewing", 5)
	c := testutil.Request(t, app, board, mara, "Charlie", "planned", 1)
	if err := svc.MarkSeen(c.Id); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	st, err := svc.Stats()
	if err != nil || st != (requests.Stats{Total: 3, Waiting: 2, New: 2}) {
		t.Fatalf("stats = %+v, %v", st, err)
	}

	q := requests.Query{Sort: "top", Statuses: []string{"pending", "reviewing"}}
	info, err := svc.Queue(q, a.Id)
	if err != nil || info != (requests.QueueInfo{Position: 1, Total: 2, Next: b.Id}) {
		t.Fatalf("queue at a = %+v, %v", info, err)
	}
	if info, _ = svc.Queue(q, b.Id); info.Position != 2 || info.Next != "" {
		t.Errorf("queue at b = %+v", info)
	}
	if info, _ = svc.Queue(q, c.Id); info.Position != 0 || info.Next != a.Id {
		t.Errorf("queue from outside = %+v", info)
	}
}
```

```go
// The JS SDK sends the non-file fields of a multipart create as JSON in "@jsonPayload", so an
// admin's internal flag arrives as a real boolean even with an image attached.
func TestAdminInternalRequestWithImage(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture(t)
	body := &bytes.Buffer{}
	mp := multipart.NewWriter(body)
	_ = mp.WriteField("@jsonPayload", `{"board":"`+f.features.Id+`","title":"Roadmap notes","internal":true}`)
	w, err := mp.CreateFormFile("images", "notes.png")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, _ = w.Write(png)
	if err := mp.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.scenario(t, tests.ApiScenario{Method: http.MethodPost, URL: "/api/collections/requests/records", Body: body,
		Headers:        map[string]string{"Authorization": testutil.Token(t, f.admin), "Content-Type": mp.FormDataContentType()},
		ExpectedStatus: 200, ExpectedContent: []string{`"visible":false`, `"awaitingApproval":false`, `"images":["`}}, nil)
}
```

Append to `server/internal/team/team.go` (and add `"net/mail"` to its imports):

```go
// ErrBadEmail is returned for an address that can't be an email.
var ErrBadEmail = errors.New("enter an email address like name@example.com")

// UserByEmail returns the board user with this email, creating an unverified account with a
// random password when there is none. The team uses it to post a request for someone; that
// person can claim the account later through a password reset link.
func UserByEmail(app core.App, email string) (*core.Record, error) {
	addr, err := mail.ParseAddress(strings.TrimSpace(email))
	if err != nil || addr.Name != "" {
		return nil, ErrBadEmail
	}
	if u, err := app.FindAuthRecordByEmail("users", addr.Address); err == nil {
		return u, nil
	}
	c, err := app.FindCollectionByNameOrId("users")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	u := core.NewRecord(c)
	u.SetEmail(addr.Address)
	u.SetRandomPassword()
	u.Set("name", NameFromEmail(addr.Address))
	if err := app.Save(u); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return u, nil
}
```

Create `server/internal/catalog/catalog.go`:

```go
// Package catalog keeps the team's ordered lists: boards and tags.
package catalog

import (
	"errors"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
)

// ErrNotOrderable is returned for a collection without a team-managed order.
var ErrNotOrderable = errors.New("only boards and tags can be reordered")

// SetOrder stores each record's position in ids (0-based) in one transaction. Records of the
// collection that ids leaves out keep their order after the listed ones.
func SetOrder(app core.App, collection string, ids []string) error {
	if collection != "boards" && collection != "tags" {
		return ErrNotOrderable
	}
	return app.RunInTransaction(func(tx core.App) error {
		recs, err := tx.FindRecordsByFilter(collection, "", "order", 0, 0)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		pos := make(map[string]int, len(ids))
		for i, id := range ids {
			pos[id] = i
		}
		next := len(ids)
		for _, r := range recs {
			p, ok := pos[r.Id]
			if !ok {
				p = next
				next++
			}
			if r.GetInt("order") == p {
				continue
			}
			r.Set("order", p)
			if err := tx.Save(r); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
}
```

```go
	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
)

var (
	ErrNoBoard       = errors.New("pick a board")
	ErrLastBoard     = errors.New("a board needs somewhere to live, so keep at least one")
	ErrBoardNotEmpty = errors.New("move this board's requests to another board first")
)

// MoveBoard moves a request to another board and notes it on the timeline.
func (s *Service) MoveBoard(requestID, boardID string, actor *core.Record) error {
	return s.App.RunInTransaction(func(tx core.App) error {
		r, err := s.find(tx, requestID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return moveBoard(tx, r, boardID, actor)
	})
}

func moveBoard(app core.App, r *core.Record, boardID string, actor *core.Record) error {
	to, err := app.FindRecordById("boards", boardID)
	if err != nil {
		return ErrNoBoard
	}
	fromID := r.GetString("board")
	if fromID == to.Id {
		return nil
	}
	fromName := ""
	if from, err := app.FindRecordById("boards", fromID); err == nil {
		fromName = from.GetString("name")
	}
	r.Set("board", to.Id)
	if err := app.Save(r); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return logActivity(app, r.Id, actor, "board_changed", fromName, to.GetString("name"))
}

// DeleteBoard deletes a board. A board with requests needs moveTo, the board that takes them.
func (s *Service) DeleteBoard(boardID, moveTo string, actor *core.Record) error {
	return s.App.RunInTransaction(func(tx core.App) error {
		b, err := tx.FindRecordById("boards", boardID)
		if err != nil {
			return ErrNoBoard
		}
		if n, err := tx.CountRecords("boards"); err != nil || n <= 1 {
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return ErrLastBoard
		}
		recs, err := tx.FindAllRecords("requests", dbx.HashExp{"board": b.Id})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if len(recs) > 0 && (moveTo == "" || moveTo == b.Id) {
			return ErrBoardNotEmpty
		}
		for _, r := range recs {
			if err := moveBoard(tx, r, moveTo, actor); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return tx.Delete(b)
	})
}

// Reassign makes author the author of a request that a team member posted for them: the
// request, its "posted this" timeline entry and its automatic vote move to the new author.
func (s *Service) Reassign(requestID string, author *core.Record) error {
	return s.App.RunInTransaction(func(tx core.App) error {
		r, err := s.find(tx, requestID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		old := r.GetString("author")
		if old == author.Id {
			return nil
		}
		if old != "" {
			v, _ := tx.FindFirstRecordByFilter("votes", "user = {:u} && request = {:r}", dbx.Params{"u": old, "r": r.Id})
			if v != nil {
				if err := tx.Delete(v); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		}
		dup, _ := tx.FindFirstRecordByFilter("votes", "user = {:u} && request = {:r}", dbx.Params{"u": author.Id, "r": r.Id})
		if dup == nil {
			if err := addVote(tx, author.Id, r.Id); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		r.Set("author", author.Id)
		if _, err := s.recount(tx, r); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		created, _ := tx.FindFirstRecordByFilter("activity", "request = {:r} && kind = 'created'", dbx.Params{"r": r.Id})
		if created != nil {
			created.Set("actor", author.Id)
			created.Set("actorName", author.GetString("name"))
			return tx.Save(created)
		}
		return nil
	})
}

// Stats are the dashboard's counts. Merged requests are left out.
type Stats struct {
	Total   int `json:"total"`
	Waiting int `json:"waiting"` // pending or reviewing: waiting for the team
	New     int `json:"new"`     // not opened by the team yet
}

func (s *Service) Stats() (Stats, error) {
	var st Stats
	err := s.App.DB().NewQuery(`SELECT
		count(*) AS total,
		coalesce(sum(status IN ('pending', 'reviewing')), 0) AS waiting,
		coalesce(sum(teamSeenAt = ''), 0) AS new
		FROM requests WHERE mergedInto = ''`).Row(&st.Total, &st.Waiting, &st.New)
	return st, err
}
```

Add to `server/internal/requests/list.go`, after `order`:

```go
// QueueInfo is where a request sits in the dashboard's current filtered list.
type QueueInfo struct {
	Position int    `json:"position"` // 1-based; 0 when the request is not in the list
	Total    int    `json:"total"`
	Next     string `json:"next"` // the request Skip opens; "" at the end of the list
}

// queueMax caps how many ids Queue reads; Skip past it starts again from the top.
const queueMax = 1000

// Queue finds current in the team's list for q and the request after it. When current has
// left the list (its status changed), Next is the first request of the list.
func (s *Service) Queue(q Query, current string) (QueueInfo, error) {
	base := s.filter(q, nil, true)
	var info QueueInfo
	if err := base.Select("count(*)").Row(&info.Total); err != nil {
		return info, err
	}
	var ids []string
	if err := base.Select("r.id").OrderBy(order(q.Sort)...).Limit(queueMax).Column(&ids); err != nil {
		return info, err
	}
	for i, id := range ids {
		if id == current {
			info.Position = i + 1
			if i+1 < len(ids) {
				info.Next = ids[i+1]
			}
			return info, nil
		}
	}
	if len(ids) > 0 && ids[0] != current {
		info.Next = ids[0]
	}
	return info, nil
}
```

```go
// onRequestUpdate rejects banned authors, and when the team puts a request on the board it is
```

```go
// query reads the list filters shared by /requests and /queue.
func query(e *core.RequestEvent) requests.Query {
	q := e.Request.URL.Query()
	page, _ := strconv.Atoi(q.Get("page"))
	perPage, _ := strconv.Atoi(q.Get("perPage"))
```

```go
func (d Deps) moveBoard(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		Board string `json:"board"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil || body.Board == "" {
		return e.BadRequestError("Pick a board.", err)
	}
	who, err := actor(e)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := d.Svc.MoveBoard(e.Request.PathValue("id"), body.Board, who); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) reassign(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		Email string `json:"email"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil {
		return e.BadRequestError("Enter an email address.", err)
	}
	u, err := team.UserByEmail(e.App, body.Email)
	if err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	if err := d.Svc.Reassign(e.Request.PathValue("id"), u); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) queue(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	info, err := d.Svc.Queue(query(e), e.Request.URL.Query().Get("current"))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, info)
}

func (d Deps) stats(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	st, err := d.Svc.Stats()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return e.JSON(http.StatusOK, st)
}

func (d Deps) order(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	var body struct {
		IDs []string `json:"ids"`
	}
	if err := e.BindBody(&body); err != nil {
		return e.BadRequestError("Send the ids in their new order.", err)
	}
	if err := catalog.SetOrder(e.App, e.Request.PathValue("collection"), body.IDs); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (d Deps) deleteBoard(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
	who, err := actor(e)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := d.Svc.DeleteBoard(e.Request.PathValue("id"), e.Request.URL.Query().Get("moveTo"), who); err != nil {
		return fail(e, err)
	}
	return e.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the site package**

Create `server/internal/site/site.go`:

```go
// Package site builds the public configuration of the board (brand, boards, tags, features)
// and keeps it cached between changes.
package site

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"sync"

	"github.com/pocketbase/pocketbase/core"
)

// Version is Lort's version, set at build time with -ldflags "-X .../site.Version=1.0.0".
var Version = "dev"

type Ref struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

// Features are the optional services that are switched on. The web app hides everything
// that belongs to a feature that is off.
type Features struct {
	Email     bool `json:"email"`
	Voice     bool `json:"voice"`
	Turnstile bool `json:"turnstile"`
}

// Config is what every visitor may know about the board.
type Config struct {
	AppName         string   `json:"appName"`
	Logo            string   `json:"logo"`
	PrimaryColor    string   `json:"primaryColor"`
	ShowLandingCard bool     `json:"showLandingCard"`
	RoadmapPublic   bool     `json:"roadmapPublic"`
	Boards          []Ref    `json:"boards"` // public boards, in order
	Tags            []Ref    `json:"tags"`
	Features        Features `json:"features"`
	Version         string   `json:"version"`
}

// Load reads the config from the database.
func Load(app core.App) (Config, error) {
	settings, err := app.FindFirstRecordByFilter("settings", "")
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	logo := ""
	if f := settings.GetString("logo"); f != "" {
		logo = "/api/files/settings/" + settings.Id + "/" + f
	}
	boards, err := app.FindRecordsByFilter("boards", "isPublic = true", "order", 0, 0)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	tags, err := app.FindRecordsByFilter("tags", "", "order", 0, 0)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	return Config{
		AppName:         settings.GetString("appName"),
		Logo:            logo,
		PrimaryColor:    settings.GetString("primaryColor"),
		ShowLandingCard: settings.GetBool("showLandingCard"),
		RoadmapPublic:   settings.GetBool("roadmapPublic"),
		Boards:          refs(boards),
		Tags:            refs(tags),
		Version:         Version,
	}, nil
}

func refs(rs []*core.Record) []Ref {
	out := make([]Ref, len(rs))
	for i, r := range rs {
		out[i] = Ref{r.Id, r.GetString("name")}
	}
	return out
}

// Cache holds the config and its header encoding, and drops them whenever settings, boards
// or tags change (after the change commits).
type Cache struct {
	app    core.App
	mu     sync.Mutex
	cfg    *Config
	header string
}

// NewCache returns a cache bound to app's record hooks.
func NewCache(app core.App) *Cache {
	c := &Cache{app: app}
	drop := func(e *core.RecordEvent) error {
		c.Invalidate()
		return e.Next()
	}
	app.OnRecordAfterCreateSuccess("settings", "boards", "tags").BindFunc(drop)
	app.OnRecordAfterUpdateSuccess("settings", "boards", "tags").BindFunc(drop)
	app.OnRecordAfterDeleteSuccess("settings", "boards", "tags").BindFunc(drop)
	return c
}

func (c *Cache) Invalidate() {
	c.mu.Lock()
	c.cfg, c.header = nil, ""
	c.mu.Unlock()
}

// Get returns the config, loading it when the cache is empty.
func (c *Cache) Get() (Config, error) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	if c.cfg != nil {
		return *c.cfg, nil
	}
	cfg, err := Load(c.app)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	b, err := json.Marshal(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	c.cfg, c.header = &cfg, base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b)
	return cfg, nil
}

// Header is the config as unpadded base64url JSON, for the X-Lort-Config request header
// (header values must be ASCII; app names may not be). It is "" if the config can't load.
func (c *Cache) Header() string {
	if _, err := c.Get(); err != nil {
		return ""
	}
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.header
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Send it with every page request, and fix the backoff log**

In `server/internal/web/web.go`, replace `Proxy`'s doc comment and signature, and set the header before forwarding:

```go
// ConfigHeader carries the board's public config to Next.js. The proxy always overwrites it,
// so a visitor can't send their own.
const ConfigHeader = "X-Lort-Config"

// Proxy returns a handler that forwards everything to target, with config() in ConfigHeader.
func Proxy(target *url.URL, config func() string) func(*core.RequestEvent) error {
	p := httputil.NewSingleHostReverseProxy(target)
	p.ErrorHandler = func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request, _ error) {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
		w.Header().Set("Retry-After", "5")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadGateway)
		_, _ = w.Write([]byte(unavailable))
	}
	return func(e *core.RequestEvent) error {
		e.Request.Header.Del(ConfigHeader)
		if v := config(); v != "" {
			e.Request.Header.Set(ConfigHeader, v)
		}
		p.ServeHTTP(e.Response, e.Request)
		return nil
	}
}
```

and in `Supervise`:

```go
		// A string, not a time.Duration: PocketBase's log store can't encode a Duration and
		// would drop the whole line.
		log.Warn("web server exited, restarting", "err", err, "in", backoff.String())
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Make `cn` Lort-aware and point components at it**

Replace `web/lib/utils.ts`:

```ts
import { createCn } from "cn/config"

/**
 * Class merging (clsx + tailwind-merge semantics) that knows Lort's theme. Without the
 * extension, cn reads an unknown text-* as a colour, so cn("text-label", "text-foreground")
 * would drop the font size. Every component imports cn from here, never from "cn".
 */
export const cn = createCn({
  extend: {
    theme: {
      text: ["display-xl", "display-l", "title", "count", "body-lg", "body", "label", "caption", "micro"],
      shadow: ["sticker", "float", "card-float"],
    },
  },
})
```

Create `web/scripts/ui-fix.mjs`:

```js
// Points shadcn components at Lort's configured cn (lib/utils.ts). The shadcn CLI writes
// `import { cn } from "cn"`, which does not know Lort's text sizes and shadows.
// Run after every `pnpm dlx shadcn@latest add`: `pnpm ui:fix`.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { join } from "node:path"

const dir = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "components", "ui")
for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
  const file = join(dir, name)
  const src = readFileSync(file, "utf8")
  const out = src.replace(/from ["']cn["']/g, 'from "@/lib/utils"')
  if (out !== src) writeFileSync(file, out)
}
```

Run: `cd web && pnpm ui:fix && pnpm test`
Expected: 3 tests pass.

```tsx
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { ThemeProvider as NextThemesProvider } from "next-themes"

// The scaffold's "D toggles the theme" hotkey is gone on purpose: visitors on a public board
// would trigger it by accident, and the design has no theme switch. The theme follows the
// visitor's system setting.
function ThemeProvider({ children, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<typeof NextThemesProvider>) {
  return (
    <NextThemesProvider attribute="class" defaultTheme="system" enableSystem disableTransitionOnChange {...props}>
      {children}
    </NextThemesProvider>
  )
}

export { ThemeProvider }
```

```css
@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *));

/*
 * Lort's tokens (DESIGN.md). The raw tokens are plain custom properties on :root (light) and

 * Naming: Lort's "muted" text colour is shadcn's muted-foreground (text-muted-foreground);
 * bg-muted is surface-2. Lort's "surface" is shadcn's card (bg-card, also bg-surface).

  @keyframes countdown {
    from {

    }
    to {

  /* A request that just arrived over realtime starts tinted and fades to its normal surface. */
  @keyframes fresh {

    }
  }
  @keyframes bone {
    0%,
    100% {
      opacity: 1;
    }
    50% {

    }
  }
}

@layer base {
  * {
    @apply border-border;
  }
  html {
    @apply bg-background font-sans text-foreground antialiased;
  }
  body {
    @apply min-h-dvh bg-background;
  }
  /* The design's focus ring: 3px solid, 3px away, on everything focusable. */
  :focus-visible {
    @apply outline-3 outline-offset-3 outline-focus;
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *,
    ::before,
    ::after {
      animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
      animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
      transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;

  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { brandCss, contrast, deriveBrand, GROUNDS, MIN_CONTRAST } from "@/lib/colors"

const PRESETS = ["#cc3a6d", "#d4553a", "#b7791f", "#2f855a", "#0f8a8a", "#2563eb", "#7c4dcc", "#4b5563"]

describe("deriveBrand", () => {
  it("finds readable text for every preset, changing the colour only when it must", () => {
    for (const hex of PRESETS) {
      const b = deriveBrand(hex)
      expect(contrast(b.primary, b.onPrimary)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_CONTRAST)
    }
    expect(deriveBrand("#cc3a6d").primary).toBe("#cc3a6d")
    expect(deriveBrand("#2563eb").primary).toBe("#2563eb")
    // Coral reaches 4.5:1 with neither white nor ink, so its lightness moves a little.
    expect(deriveBrand("#d4553a").primary).not.toBe("#d4553a")
  })

  it("uses white on Lort Pink and ink on Amber", () => {
    expect(deriveBrand("#cc3a6d").onPrimary).toBe("#ffffff")
    expect(deriveBrand("#b7791f").onPrimary).toBe("#1e1a18")
  })

  it("nudges a colour that neither white nor ink can sit on", () => {
    const b = deriveBrand("#777777")
    expect(b.primary).not.toBe("#777777")
    expect(contrast(b.primary, b.onPrimary)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_CONTRAST)
  })

  it("makes primary-text readable on both grounds of each theme", () => {
    for (const hex of [...PRESETS, "#ffee00", "#101010"]) {
      const { primaryText } = deriveBrand(hex)
      for (const g of GROUNDS.light) expect(contrast(primaryText.light, g)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_CONTRAST)
      for (const g of GROUNDS.dark) expect(contrast(primaryText.dark, g)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_CONTRAST)
    }
  })

  it("falls back to Lort Pink for garbage", () => {
    expect(deriveBrand("red").primary).toBe("#cc3a6d")
  })
})

describe("brandCss", () => {
  it("writes both themes", () => {
    expect(brandCss("#2563eb")).toMatch(
      /^:root\{--primary:#2563eb;--primary-foreground:#ffffff;--primary-text:#[0-9a-f]{6}\}\.dark\{/
    )
  })
})
```

Create `web/lib/config.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { DEFAULT_CONFIG, parseConfig } from "@/lib/config"

// Same encoding as the Go proxy: unpadded base64url of the JSON's UTF-8 bytes.
function encode(v: unknown): string {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(v))
  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes))
    .replace(/\+/g, "-")
    .replace(/\//g, "_")
    .replace(/=+$/, "")
}

describe("parseConfig", () => {
  it("decodes the proxy header, including non-ASCII names", () => {
    const c = parseConfig(
      encode({ appName: "Driftnote ✨", primaryColor: "#2563eb", boards: [{ id: "b1", name: "Features" }] })
    )
    expect(c.appName).toBe("Driftnote ✨")
    expect(c.primaryColor).toBe("#2563eb")
    expect(c.boards).toEqual([{ id: "b1", name: "Features" }])
    expect(c.features).toEqual(DEFAULT_CONFIG.features)
  })

  it("falls back to the defaults without a usable header", () => {
    expect(parseConfig(null)).toBe(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
    expect(parseConfig("%%%")).toBe(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the colour derivation**

Create `web/lib/colors.ts`:

```ts
// Derives the tokens that depend on the owner's primary colour, keeping WCAG AA contrast.
// primary-soft is not here: globals.css mixes it from --primary with color-mix().

type RGB = [number, number, number] // sRGB channels, 0–1
type OKLCH = [number, number, number] // lightness 0–1, chroma, hue in degrees

export const INK = "#1e1a18"
export const WHITE = "#ffffff"
/** The grounds primary-text must stay readable on, per theme: ground and card surface. */
export const GROUNDS = { light: ["#f3f1ef", "#ffffff"], dark: ["#1a1716", "#24201e"] } as const
export const MIN_CONTRAST = 4.5

export function parseHex(hex: string): RGB | null {
  const m = /^#?([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(hex.trim())
  if (!m) return null
  const n = parseInt(m[1], 16)
  return [((n >> 16) & 255) / 255, ((n >> 8) & 255) / 255, (n & 255) / 255]
}

export function toHex([r, g, b]: RGB): string {
  const c = (v: number) =>
    Math.round(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v)) * 255)
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(2, "0")
  return `#${c(r)}${c(g)}${c(b)}`
}

const linear = (v: number) => (v <= 0.04045 ? v / 12.92 : ((v + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4)
const gamma = (v: number) => (v <= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 * v : 1.055 * v ** (1 / 2.4) - 0.055)

function luminance(rgb: RGB): number {
  const [r, g, b] = rgb.map(linear)
  return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b
}

/** WCAG contrast ratio between two hex colours (1–21). */
export function contrast(a: string, b: string): number {
  const la = luminance(parseHex(a) ?? [0, 0, 0])
  const lb = luminance(parseHex(b) ?? [0, 0, 0])
  return (Math.max(la, lb) + 0.05) / (Math.min(la, lb) + 0.05)
}

function toOklch(rgb: RGB): OKLCH {
  const [r, g, b] = rgb.map(linear)
  const l = Math.cbrt(0.4122214708 * r + 0.5363325363 * g + 0.0514459929 * b)
  const m = Math.cbrt(0.2119034982 * r + 0.6806995451 * g + 0.1073969566 * b)
  const s = Math.cbrt(0.0883024619 * r + 0.2817188376 * g + 0.6299787005 * b)
  const L = 0.2104542553 * l + 0.793617785 * m - 0.0040720468 * s
  const A = 1.9779984951 * l - 2.428592205 * m + 0.4505937099 * s
  const B = 0.0259040371 * l + 0.7827717662 * m - 0.808675766 * s
  return [L, Math.hypot(A, B), ((Math.atan2(B, A) * 180) / Math.PI + 360) % 360]
}

function fromOklch([L, C, h]: OKLCH): RGB {
  const A = C * Math.cos((h * Math.PI) / 180)
  const B = C * Math.sin((h * Math.PI) / 180)
  const l = (L + 0.3963377774 * A + 0.2158037573 * B) ** 3
  const m = (L - 0.1055613458 * A - 0.0638541728 * B) ** 3
  const s = (L - 0.0894841775 * A - 1.291485548 * B) ** 3
  const rgb: RGB = [
    4.0767416621 * l - 3.3077115913 * m + 0.2309699292 * s,
    -1.2684380046 * l + 2.6097574011 * m - 0.3413193965 * s,
    -0.0041960863 * l - 0.7034186147 * m + 1.707614701 * s,
  ]
  return rgb.map((v) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, gamma(v)))) as RGB
}

/** Moves hex's OKLCH lightness in steps of 0.01 (up or down) until ok() holds. */
function nudge(hex: string, direction: 1 | -1, ok: (candidate: string) => boolean): string {
  const [L, C, h] = toOklch(parseHex(hex)!)
  for (let i = 0; i <= 100; i++) {
    const candidate = toHex(fromOklch([Math.min(1, Math.max(0, L + direction * i * 0.01)), C, h]))
    if (ok(candidate)) return candidate
  }
  return direction > 0 ? WHITE : INK
}

const readableOn = (grounds: readonly string[]) => (c: string) =>
  grounds.every((g) => contrast(c, g) >= MIN_CONTRAST)

export interface Brand {
  /** The primary fill. Equal to the owner's colour unless neither white nor ink could sit on it. */
  primary: string
  /** Text and icons on a primary fill. */
  onPrimary: string
  /** Primary as text or an outline, per theme. */
  primaryText: { light: string; dark: string }
}

/** Builds the brand tokens for an owner's colour. Invalid input falls back to Lort Pink. */
export function deriveBrand(input: string): Brand {
  let primary = parseHex(input) ? toHex(parseHex(input)!) : "#cc3a6d"
  const best = () => (contrast(primary, WHITE) >= contrast(primary, INK) ? WHITE : INK)
  let onPrimary = best()
  if (contrast(primary, onPrimary) < MIN_CONTRAST) {
    // Darken toward white text or lighten toward ink text, whichever is nearer.
    const dir = onPrimary === WHITE ? -1 : 1
    primary = nudge(primary, dir, (c) => contrast(c, onPrimary) >= MIN_CONTRAST)
    onPrimary = best()
  }
  return {
    primary,
    onPrimary,
    primaryText: {
      light: nudge(primary, -1, readableOn(GROUNDS.light)),
      dark: nudge(primary, 1, readableOn(GROUNDS.dark)),
    },
  }
}

/** The inline stylesheet the root layout injects to apply the owner's colour in both themes. */
export function brandCss(input: string): string {
  const b = deriveBrand(input)
  const shared = `--primary:${b.primary};--primary-foreground:${b.onPrimary};`
  return `:root{${shared}--primary-text:${b.primaryText.light}}.dark{${shared}--primary-text:${b.primaryText.dark}}`
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the config reader**

Create `web/lib/config.ts`:

```ts
// The board's public config. The Go proxy sends it with every page request in the
// X-Lort-Config header (unpadded base64url JSON), so server components never fetch it.

export interface Ref {
  id: string
  name: string
}

export interface SiteConfig {
  appName: string
  logo: string
  primaryColor: string
  showLandingCard: boolean
  roadmapPublic: boolean
  /** Public boards, in order. The first is the default for new suggestions. */
  boards: Ref[]
  tags: Ref[]
  features: { email: boolean; voice: boolean; turnstile: boolean }
  version: string
}

export const CONFIG_HEADER = "x-lort-config"

export const DEFAULT_CONFIG: SiteConfig = {
  appName: "My app",
  logo: "",
  primaryColor: "#cc3a6d",
  showLandingCard: true,
  roadmapPublic: true,
  boards: [],
  tags: [],
  features: { email: false, voice: false, turnstile: false },
  version: "dev",
}

/** Decodes the header. A missing or broken header (Next run without the Go server) gives the defaults. */
export function parseConfig(header: string | null | undefined): SiteConfig {
  if (!header) return DEFAULT_CONFIG
  try {
    const b64 = header.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/")
    const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0))
    const raw = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as Partial<SiteConfig>
    return {
      ...DEFAULT_CONFIG,
      ...raw,
      appName: raw.appName || DEFAULT_CONFIG.appName,
      boards: raw.boards ?? [],
      tags: raw.tags ?? [],
      features: { ...DEFAULT_CONFIG.features, ...raw.features },
    }
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_CONFIG
  }
}
```

Expected: the colour and config tests pass.

```tsx
"use client"

import { useTheme } from "next-themes"
import { Toaster as Sonner, type ToasterProps } from "sonner"

// Lort: toasts sit bottom-centre above the floating bars, at most three at once, drawn as the
// design's pill-ish surface-2 card (inset-sm + float). No icons: the text says what happened.
const Toaster = ({ ...props }: ToasterProps) => {
  const { theme = "system" } = useTheme()

  return (
    <Sonner
      theme={theme as ToasterProps["theme"]}
      className="toaster group"
      position="bottom-center"
      visibleToasts={3}
      offset={{ bottom: 104 }}
      mobileOffset={{ bottom: 96 }}
      toastOptions={{
        unstyled: true,
        classNames: {

            "relative flex min-h-12 w-(--width) items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-border-strong bg-surface-2 py-2 pr-2 pl-[18px] text-label font-medium text-foreground inset-shadow-sm shadow-float",
          title: "flex-1",

          actionButton: "h-8 rounded-full px-3 font-bold text-primary-text hover:bg-surface-3",
          cancelButton: "h-8 rounded-full px-3 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-surface-3",
        },
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Toaster }
```

Create `web/lib/query.ts` (Task 8 adds the cache helpers to this file):

```ts
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"

export function makeQueryClient() {
  return new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: { staleTime: 30_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: false, retry: 1 },
    },
  })
}
```

Create `web/app/providers.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { createContext, useContext, useState } from "react"
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { MotionConfig } from "motion/react"
import { ThemeProvider } from "@/components/theme-provider"
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner"
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip"
import type { SiteConfig } from "@/lib/config"
import { makeQueryClient } from "@/lib/query"

const ConfigContext = createContext<SiteConfig | null>(null)

/** The board's public config (brand, boards, tags, features), from the X-Lort-Config header. */
export function useConfig(): SiteConfig {
  const c = useContext(ConfigContext)
  if (!c) throw new Error("useConfig must be used inside <Providers>")
  return c
}

export function Providers({ config, children }: { config: SiteConfig; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [queryClient] = useState(makeQueryClient)
  return (
    <ConfigContext.Provider value={config}>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <ThemeProvider>
          <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
            <TooltipProvider>
              {children}
              <Toaster />
            </TooltipProvider>
          </MotionConfig>
        </ThemeProvider>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </ConfigContext.Provider>
  )
}
```

Replace `web/app/layout.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next"
import { Bricolage_Grotesque, Figtree } from "next/font/google"
import { headers } from "next/headers"

import "./globals.css"
import { Providers } from "@/app/providers"
import { brandCss } from "@/lib/colors"
import { CONFIG_HEADER, parseConfig } from "@/lib/config"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// Self-hosted at build time by next/font.
const figtree = Figtree({ subsets: ["latin"], variable: "--font-figtree", weight: ["400", "500", "600", "700"] })
const bricolage = Bricolage_Grotesque({ subsets: ["latin"], variable: "--font-bricolage", weight: ["700", "800"] })

export async function generateMetadata(): Promise<Metadata> {
  const config = parseConfig((await headers()).get(CONFIG_HEADER))
  return {
    title: { default: config.appName, template: `%s · ${config.appName}` },
    icons: config.logo ? { icon: config.logo } : undefined,
  }
}

export default async function RootLayout({ children }: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>) {
  const config = parseConfig((await headers()).get(CONFIG_HEADER))
  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning className={cn(figtree.variable, bricolage.variable)}>
      <head>
        {/* The owner's primary colour, in both themes (lib/colors.ts keeps it AA). */}
        <style id="lort-brand">{brandCss(config.primaryColor)}</style>
      </head>
      <body>
        <Providers config={config}>{children}</Providers>
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}
```

```tsx
function DropdownMenuCheckboxItem({
  className,
  children,
  checked,
  inset,
  ...props
}: MenuPrimitive.CheckboxItem.Props & {
  inset?: boolean
}) {
  // Lort: a square checkbox on the left (primary when checked), not a tick on the right.
  return (
    <MenuPrimitive.CheckboxItem
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-checkbox-item"
      data-inset={inset}
      className={cn(
        "group/checkbox-item relative flex h-10 cursor-default items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl px-2.5 text-label font-medium outline-hidden select-none focus:bg-accent focus:text-accent-foreground focus:[--si-ground:var(--surface-2)] data-disabled:pointer-events-none data-disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0",
        className

      checked={checked}
      {...props}
    >
      <span
        data-slot="dropdown-menu-checkbox-item-indicator"
        className="pointer-events-none grid size-[18px] place-items-center rounded-[6px] border-2 border-dot-empty group-data-checked/checkbox-item:border-primary group-data-checked/checkbox-item:bg-primary group-data-checked/checkbox-item:text-primary-foreground"
      >
        <MenuPrimitive.CheckboxItemIndicator>
          <IconCheck className="size-3" stroke={3} />
        </MenuPrimitive.CheckboxItemIndicator>
      </span>
      {children}
    </MenuPrimitive.CheckboxItem>
  )
}
```

```tsx
"use client"

import { Tabs as TabsPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/tabs"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// Lort: a pill track pressed into the page (inset-sm) holding pill tabs; the selected tab
// inverts to an ink pill. components/lort/sort-tabs.tsx swaps that fill for a Motion pill
// that slides between tabs.

function Tabs({
  className,
  orientation = "horizontal",
  ...props
}: TabsPrimitive.Root.Props) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.Root
      data-slot="tabs"
      data-orientation={orientation}
      className={cn("group/tabs flex gap-2 data-horizontal:flex-col", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const tabsListVariants = cva(
  "group/tabs-list relative inline-flex w-fit items-center rounded-full border border-border bg-background p-1 text-muted-foreground inset-shadow-sm",
  {
    variants: {
      size: {
        default: "h-[46px]",
        sm: "h-10",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function TabsList({
  className,
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: TabsPrimitive.List.Props & VariantProps<typeof tabsListVariants>) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.List
      data-slot="tabs-list"
      data-size={size}
      className={cn(tabsListVariants({ size }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TabsTrigger({ className, ...props }: TabsPrimitive.Tab.Props) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.Tab
      data-slot="tabs-trigger"
      className={cn(
        "relative isolate inline-flex h-9 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-[18px] text-label whitespace-nowrap text-muted-foreground transition-colors duration-200 hover:text-foreground disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 data-active:bg-foreground data-active:text-background group-data-[size=sm]/tabs-list:h-8 group-data-[size=sm]/tabs-list:px-[13px] group-data-[size=sm]/tabs-list:text-caption group-data-[size=sm]/tabs-list:font-semibold [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function TabsContent({ className, ...props }: TabsPrimitive.Panel.Props) {
  return (
    <TabsPrimitive.Panel
      data-slot="tabs-content"
      className={cn("flex-1 text-body outline-none", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger, TabsContent, tabsListVariants }
```

```tsx
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// Lort: "default" is the tag pill of the Suggest modal (primary-soft with a primary-text ring
// when pressed); "segment" is an option in a segmented track (components/lort/segmented.tsx),
// whose pressed pill is drawn by Motion.
const toggleVariants = cva(
  "group/toggle relative isolate inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap transition-colors disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "h-8 bg-surface-2 px-[13px] text-caption font-semibold text-ink-2 hover:bg-surface-3 data-pressed:bg-primary-soft data-pressed:text-primary-text data-pressed:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1.5px_var(--primary-text)]",
        segment: "h-[34px] px-4 text-label text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground data-pressed:text-background",
      },
      // Kept so the generated toggle-group.tsx compiles unchanged; Lort has one size.
      size: {
        default: "",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Toggle({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: TogglePrimitive.Props & VariantProps<typeof toggleVariants>) {
  return <TogglePrimitive data-slot="toggle" className={cn(toggleVariants({ variant, size, className }))} {...props} />
}

export { Toggle, toggleVariants }
```

- `badge.tsx`: in the base class list replace `h-5 … rounded-4xl … px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium` with `h-auto … rounded-full … px-2 py-0.5 text-micro`; the `secondary` variant becomes `"bg-surface-3 text-ink-2 [a]:hover:bg-surface-3/80"`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the statuses**

Create `web/lib/status.ts`:

```ts
// Statuses and triage actions. Mirrors server/internal/status; the server is the authority.

export type Status = "pending" | "reviewing" | "planned" | "progress" | "completed" | "closed"

export const STATUSES: Status[] = ["pending", "reviewing", "planned", "progress", "completed", "closed"]

/** Still in play: the default status filter on the board and in the dashboard. */
export const OPEN_STATUSES: Status[] = ["pending", "reviewing", "planned", "progress"]

/** Label and fill level (0–1) of each status. The fill rises from the bottom of the circle. */
export const STATUS_META: Record<Status, { label: string; fill: number }> = {
  pending: { label: "Pending", fill: 0 },
  reviewing: { label: "Reviewing", fill: 0.28 },
  planned: { label: "Planned", fill: 0.5 },
  progress: { label: "In progress", fill: 0.8 },
  completed: { label: "Completed", fill: 1 },
  closed: { label: "Closed", fill: 1 },
}

export function isStatus(v: string): v is Status {
  return (STATUSES as string[]).includes(v)
}

/** Server action ids (POST /api/lort/requests/{id}/status), plus merge and skip, which aren't status changes. */
export type TriageActionId =
  | "close"
  | "merge"
  | "skip"
  | "accept"
  | "roadmap"
  | "rereview"
  | "start"
  | "complete"
  | "reopen"

export interface TriageAction {
  id: TriageActionId
  label: string
  icon?: "x" | "merge" | "skip" | "rotate"
  tone: "danger" | "neutral" | "quiet" | "primary"
  /** The status it moves to; undefined for merge (opens a picker) and skip (opens the next request). */
  next?: Status
}

const CLOSE: TriageAction = { id: "close", label: "Close", icon: "x", tone: "danger", next: "closed" }
const MERGE: TriageAction = { id: "merge", label: "Merge", icon: "merge", tone: "neutral" }
const SKIP: TriageAction = { id: "skip", label: "Skip", icon: "skip", tone: "quiet" }

const ACTIONS: Record<Status, TriageAction[]> = {
  pending: [CLOSE, MERGE, SKIP, { id: "accept", label: "Accept", tone: "primary", next: "reviewing" }],
  reviewing: [CLOSE, MERGE, SKIP, { id: "roadmap", label: "Add to roadmap", tone: "primary", next: "planned" }],
  planned: [
    { id: "rereview", label: "Re-review", icon: "rotate", tone: "neutral", next: "reviewing" },
    { id: "start", label: "Start working", tone: "primary", next: "progress" },
  ],
  progress: [{ id: "complete", label: "Complete", tone: "primary", next: "completed" }],
  completed: [{ id: "reopen", label: "Reopen", tone: "primary", next: "progress" }],
  closed: [{ id: "reopen", label: "Reopen", tone: "primary", next: "pending" }],
}

/** The triage bar's actions for a status, left to right; the last is the primary. */
export function triageActions(status: Status): TriageAction[] {
  return ACTIONS[status]
}

/** The status changes on offer from the dashboard's status chip: the triage moves without merge and skip. */
export function statusMoves(status: Status): TriageAction[] {
  return ACTIONS[status].filter((a) => a.next)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the status icon and the vote sticker**

Create `web/components/lort/status-icon.tsx`:

```tsx
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { STATUS_META, type Status } from "@/lib/status"

// Static class names (Tailwind only sees whole strings).
const ICON_COLOR: Record<Status, string> = {
  pending: "text-status-pending",
  reviewing: "text-status-reviewing",
  planned: "text-status-planned",
  progress: "text-status-progress",
  completed: "text-status-completed",
  closed: "text-status-closed",
}

const TEXT_COLOR: Record<Status, string> = {
  pending: "text-status-pending-text",
  reviewing: "text-status-reviewing-text",
  planned: "text-status-planned-text",
  progress: "text-status-progress-text",
  completed: "text-status-completed-text",
  closed: "text-status-closed-text",
}

/** SVG path of a circle of radius r (centred in a 16×16 box) filled from the bottom up to fraction f. */
export function fillPath(f: number, r: number): string {
  if (f <= 0) return ""
  if (f >= 1) return `M${8 - r} 8A${r} ${r} 0 1 0 ${8 + r} 8A${r} ${r} 0 1 0 ${8 - r} 8Z`
  const y = 8 + r - 2 * r * f
  const dx = Math.sqrt(r * r - (y - 8) ** 2)
  const n = (v: number) => +v.toFixed(2)
  return `M${n(8 - dx)} ${n(y)}A${r} ${r} 0 ${f > 0.5 ? 1 : 0} 0 ${n(8 + dx)} ${n(y)}Z`
}

export interface StatusIconProps {
  status: Status
  /** Pixel size, 14–24 (38 on the landing page). */
  size?: number
  /** The sticker keyline used on public cards and timelines. */
  keyline?: boolean
  /** Decorative when a visible label sits next to it; otherwise it is an image named after the status. */
  decorative?: boolean
  className?: string
}

/**
 * The circle that shows where a request is, filling from the bottom toward Completed.
 * Its inner ground follows --si-ground (default: the card surface) so it matches whatever
 * it sits on: set [--si-ground:var(--surface-2)] on a hovered row, for example.
 */
export function StatusIcon({ status, size = 16, keyline = false, decorative = false, className }: StatusIconProps) {
  const { label, fill } = STATUS_META[status]
  const ring = keyline ? 6.2 : 7
  const inner = keyline ? 4.5 : 5.1
  const ground = "fill-[var(--si-ground,var(--surface))]"
  return (
    <svg
      width={size}
      height={size}
      viewBox="0 0 16 16"
      className={cn("block shrink-0", ICON_COLOR[status], className)}
      role={decorative ? undefined : "img"}
      aria-label={decorative ? undefined : label}
      aria-hidden={decorative || undefined}
    >
      {!decorative && <title>{label}</title>}
      {keyline && <circle cx="8" cy="8" r="7.6" className="fill-keyline" />}
      <circle cx="8" cy="8" r={ring} className={ground} />
      <circle cx="8" cy="8" r={ring} className="fill-current opacity-30" />
      <circle cx="8" cy="8" r={ring} className="fill-none stroke-current" strokeWidth={keyline ? 1.3 : 1.5} />
      {fill > 0 && <path d={fillPath(fill, inner)} className="fill-current" />}
      {status === "completed" && (
        <path
          d="M5.3 8.2l1.8 1.8l3.6-3.7"
          className="fill-none stroke-[var(--si-ground,var(--surface))]"
          strokeWidth={1.8}
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
        />
      )}
      {status === "closed" && (
        <path
          d="M6 6l4 4M10 6l-4 4"
          className="fill-none stroke-[var(--si-ground,var(--surface))]"
          strokeWidth={1.8}
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
      )}
    </svg>
  )
}

/** Icon + label in ink: one of the two forms the Status Label Rule allows. */
export function StatusLabel({
  status,
  size = 16,
  keyline,
  className,
}: Omit<StatusIconProps, "decorative">) {
  return (
    <span className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-[7px] font-semibold text-foreground", className)}>
      <StatusIcon status={status} size={size} keyline={keyline} decorative />
      {STATUS_META[status].label}
    </span>
  )
}

/** The label in its status colour, no icon: the rule's other form. */
export function StatusText({ status, className }: { status: Status; className?: string }) {
  return <span className={cn("font-semibold", TEXT_COLOR[status], className)}>{STATUS_META[status].label}</span>
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/vote-sticker.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { motion } from "motion/react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface VoteStickerProps {
  /** Votes, including the viewer's when voted. */
  count: number
  voted: boolean
  onToggle?: () => void
  size?: "md" | "sm"
  /** Public roadmap: shows the count, isn't a control. Closed and merged requests too. */
  readOnly?: boolean
  /** The request title, for the accessible name. */
  subject?: string
  className?: string
}

const SLAP = { scale: [1.15, 1], rotate: [0, -7] }
const PEEL = { scale: [1, 1.06, 1], rotate: [-7, -14, 0] }
const SLAP_T = { type: "spring" as const, stiffness: 520, damping: 18, mass: 0.7 }
const PEEL_T = { duration: 0.16, ease: "easeOut" as const }

/**
 * The upvote: an empty dashed spot that becomes a sticker slapped onto the card (scale 1.15 → 1,
 * rotate to −7°) and peels off again. MotionConfig reducedMotion="user" in the providers makes
 * both instant for visitors who ask for less motion.
 */
export function VoteSticker({ count, voted, onToggle, size = "md", readOnly, subject, className }: VoteStickerProps) {
  const classes = cn(
    "relative inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full font-display text-count tabular-nums",
    size === "md" ? "size-[58px]" : "size-10 text-sm",
    voted
      ? "border-3 border-keyline bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-sticker"
      : "border-2 border-dashed border-dot-empty text-foreground",
    !readOnly && !voted && "transition-colors hover:border-muted-foreground",
    className
  )

  const votes = count === 1 ? "1 vote" : `${count} votes`

  if (readOnly) {
    return (
      <span className={cn(classes, voted && "-rotate-7")} aria-label={votes}>
        {count}
      </span>
    )
  }

  const about = subject ? ` ${subject}` : " this"
  return (
    <motion.button
      type="button"
      className={classes}
      aria-pressed={voted}
      aria-label={voted ? `Remove your upvote from${about}, ${votes}` : `Upvote${about}, ${votes}`}
      onClick={onToggle}
      initial={false}
      animate={voted ? SLAP : PEEL}
      transition={voted ? SLAP_T : PEEL_T}
    >
      {count}
    </motion.button>
  )
}
```
