Create `web/lib/files.ts`:

```ts
interface FileOptions {
  thumb?: string // "100x100", see PocketBase thumb formats
  token?: string // a file token, needed for protected files the visitor can't see anonymously
}

/** URL of a file on a record. */
export function fileUrl(collection: string, recordId: string, filename: string, opts: FileOptions = {}): string {
  const q = new URLSearchParams()
  if (opts.thumb) q.set("thumb", opts.thumb)
  if (opts.token) q.set("token", opts.token)
  const s = q.toString()
  return `/api/files/${collection}/${recordId}/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}${s ? `?${s}` : ""}`
}

/** A person's avatar thumbnail, or undefined for the initial fallback. */
export function avatarUrl(p: { id: string; avatar?: string }, size = 100): string | undefined {
  return p.id && p.avatar ? fileUrl("users", p.id, p.avatar, { thumb: `${size}x${size}` }) : undefined
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/person-avatar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from "@/components/ui/avatar"
import { avatarUrl } from "@/lib/files"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// Decorative fills for initials, never status colours; the dark initial reads on all of them.
const FILLS = ["bg-[#3ba3a0]", "bg-[#e0a84b]", "bg-[#8db4f2]", "bg-[#c99ae8]", "bg-[#7fcb8f]", "bg-[#e88f7f]"]

export function fillFor(name: string): string {
  let h = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < name.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + name.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0
  return FILLS[h % FILLS.length]
}

export interface PersonAvatarProps {
  person: { id: string; name: string; avatar?: string; team?: boolean }
  size?: number
  /** The sticker keyline (public pages). */
  keyline?: boolean
  className?: string
}

/** A person's picture, or their initial on a warm fill; team members get a primary ring. */
export function PersonAvatar({ person, size = 24, keyline = false, className }: PersonAvatarProps) {
  const name = person.name || "Someone"
  const src = person.avatar ? avatarUrl({ id: person.id, avatar: person.avatar }, size > 48 ? 200 : 100) : undefined
  return (
    <Avatar
      role="img"
      aria-label={person.team ? `${name} (team)` : name}
      style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: Math.round(size * 0.46) }}
      className={cn(
        "after:hidden",
        keyline && "border-2 border-keyline",
        person.team && "ring-2 ring-primary ring-offset-2 ring-offset-background",
        className
      )}
    >
      {src && <AvatarImage src={src} alt="" />}
      <AvatarFallback className={cn("font-extrabold text-[#1a1716]", fillFor(name))} aria-hidden>
        {name.trim()[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? "?"}
      </AvatarFallback>
    </Avatar>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/logo-mark.tsx`:

```tsx
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface LogoMarkProps {
  appName: string
  /** The owner's uploaded logo URL; without one, the first letter on primary. */
  logo?: string
  size?: number
  className?: string
}

/** The app's logo mark, tilted −4° like a sticker on the wall. Decorative: the app name sits next to it. */
export function LogoMark({ appName, logo, size = 32, className }: LogoMarkProps) {
  const style = { width: size, height: size, fontSize: Math.round(size * 0.5) }
  if (logo) {
    return (
      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- owner-uploaded file served by PocketBase
      <img src={logo} alt="" style={style} className={cn("-rotate-4 rounded-sm object-cover shadow-sticker", className)} />
    )
  }
  return (
    <span
      aria-hidden
      style={style}
      className={cn(
        "grid -rotate-4 place-items-center rounded-sm bg-primary font-display font-extrabold text-primary-foreground shadow-sticker",
        className
      )}
    >
      {(appName.trim()[0] ?? "L").toUpperCase()}
    </span>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/chip.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ComponentProps } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** Chip look: 34px pills on surface-2 for status, board, tags, add-tag and the visibility switch. */
export const chipClass =
  "inline-flex h-[34px] items-center gap-[7px] rounded-full bg-surface-2 px-3.5 text-caption font-semibold text-foreground [--si-ground:var(--surface-2)]"

/** The interactive version adds hover; use it as a menu or popover trigger (render={<ChipButton />}). */
export const chipButtonClass = cn(chipClass, "cursor-pointer hover:bg-surface-3 hover:[--si-ground:var(--surface-3)] aria-expanded:bg-surface-3")

/** A read-only chip (public request page). */
export function Chip({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return <span className={cn(chipClass, className)} {...props} />
}

/** A chip that opens an editor in the dashboard. Forwards every prop, so Base UI triggers can render it. */
export function ChipButton({ className, type = "button", ...props }: ComponentProps<"button">) {
  return <button type={type} className={cn(chipButtonClass, className)} {...props} />
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/bone.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { CSSProperties } from "react"
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/**
 * One skeleton bone: a surface-2 pill that pulses (static under reduced motion). Title bones
 * pass className="rounded-[12px]". Page skeletons set aria-busy="true" on their container.
 */
export function Bone({ w, h, className, style }: { w: number | string; h: number; className?: string; style?: CSSProperties }) {
  return <Skeleton aria-hidden style={{ width: w, height: h, ...style }} className={cn("shrink-0", className)} />
}
```

```tsx
export interface InternalNoteProps {

  /** Relative time, e.g. "1 hour ago". */
  time: string

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-caption text-foreground">

        </span>
        <span className="text-muted-foreground">· {time}</span>
        {actions && <span className="ml-auto">{actions}</span>}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1.5 text-body whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink-2">{children}</div>
    </div>
```

### Task 8: Data layer: clients, sessions, API calls, filters and freshness

**Files:**
- Create: `web/lib/pocketbase.ts`, `web/lib/auth.ts`, `web/lib/api.ts`, `web/lib/errors.ts`, `web/lib/time.ts`, `web/lib/filters.ts`, `web/lib/timeline.ts`, `web/lib/realtime.ts`, `web/lib/use-cooldown.ts`, and tests `web/lib/{auth.test.tsx,filters.test.ts,query.test.ts,timeline.test.ts,time.test.ts,errors.test.ts,use-cooldown.test.ts}`
- Modify: `web/lib/query.ts` (full version)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `lib/config.ts` (`Ref`), `lib/status.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `pb`, `pbAdmin`, `type Client` from `@/lib/pocketbase`.
  - `useUser(): User | null`, `useAdmin(): Admin | null`, `useAuthRecord<T>(client)`; `User{id,email,name,avatar,verified,isAdmin,banned}`, `Admin{id,email}` from `@/lib/auth`.
  - From `@/lib/api`: types `Person`, `RequestItem`, `Page<T>`, `Comment`, `EventKind`, `TimelineEvent`, `RequestDetail`, `StatusChange`, `QueueInfo`, `Stats`, `TeamMember`, `UserSummary`, `Ref`; `PAGE_SIZE = 20`; `listRequests(c, params, page?)`, `getRequest(c, id)`, `similar(c, q)`, `vote(c, id)`, `setStatus(c, id, action)`, `undoComplete(c, id)`, `merge(c, id, target)`, `markSeen(c, id)`, `moveBoard(c, id, board)`, `reassign(c, id, email)`, `queue(c, params, current)`, `stats(c)`, `teamList(c)`, `teamMe(c)`, `invite(c, email)`, `removeAdmin(c, id)`, `userSummary(c, id)`, `setBanned(c, id, banned)`, `resetLink(c, id)`, `setOrder(c, collection, ids)`, `deleteBoard(c, id, moveTo?)`.
  - `errorMessage(err, fallback?)`, `isAbort(err)`, `isAuthError(err)` from `@/lib/errors`.
  - `shortDate(iso, now?)`, `relativeTime(iso, now?)` from `@/lib/time`.
  - From `@/lib/filters`: `BoardSort`, `TableSort`, `Visibility`, `BoardFilters`, `TableFilters`, `parseBoardFilters(params)`, `boardSearch(f)`, `parseTableFilters(params)`, `tableSearch(f)`, `apiParams(f)`.
  - From `@/lib/query`: `keys`, `makeQueryClient()`, `patchRequest(qc, id, patch)`, `toggledVote(r)`.
  - `buildTimeline(detail): TimelineEntry[]`, `TimelineEntry` from `@/lib/timeline`.
  - `useLiveRefresh(client, collection, keys, onCreate?) → { live }` from `@/lib/realtime`.
  - `useCooldown(ms) → [ready, start]` from `@/lib/use-cooldown`.

Two rules this task bakes in, from the known traps: the auth snapshot is a JSON string (never the record object), and both PocketBase clients turn auto-cancellation off.

```tsx
import { act, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { useUser } from "@/lib/auth"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

// A token PocketBase's isValid accepts: a JWT whose exp is an hour away.
function token() {
  const part = (v: object) => btoa(JSON.stringify(v)).replace(/=+$/, "")
  return `${part({ alg: "HS256" })}.${part({ exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600 })}.sig`
}

let renders = 0
function Probe() {
  renders++
  const user = useUser()
  return <p>{user ? user.name : "signed out"}</p>
}

describe("useUser", () => {
  it("follows sign-in, rename and sign-out without a render loop", () => {
    renders = 0
    render(<Probe />)
    expect(screen.getByText("signed out")).toBeInTheDocument()

    act(() => pb.authStore.save(token(), { id: "u1", collectionName: "users", name: "Mara" } as never))
    expect(screen.getByText("Mara")).toBeInTheDocument()

    act(() => pb.authStore.save(pb.authStore.token, { id: "u1", collectionName: "users", name: "Mara K." } as never))
    expect(screen.getByText("Mara K.")).toBeInTheDocument()

    act(() => pb.authStore.clear())
    expect(screen.getByText("signed out")).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(renders).toBeLessThan(10)
  })
})
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { apiParams, boardSearch, parseBoardFilters, parseTableFilters, tableSearch } from "@/lib/filters"
import { OPEN_STATUSES } from "@/lib/status"

const params = (s: string) => new URLSearchParams(s)

describe("board filters", () => {
  it("defaults to trending open ideas with a clean URL", () => {
    const f = parseBoardFilters(params(""))
    expect(f).toEqual({ sort: "trending", status: OPEN_STATUSES, board: [], q: "" })
    expect(boardSearch(f)).toBe("")
  })

  it("round-trips every field", () => {
    const s = "?sort=newest&status=completed%2Cclosed&board=b1%2Cb2&q=dark+mode"
    expect(boardSearch(parseBoardFilters(params(s)))).toBe(s)
  })

  it("keeps a cleared status filter apart from the default", () => {
    const f = parseBoardFilters(params("status=all"))
    expect(f.status).toEqual([])
    expect(boardSearch(f)).toBe("?status=all")
    expect(apiParams(f).status).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it("drops unknown values", () => {
    expect(parseBoardFilters(params("sort=weird&status=pending,nope")).status).toEqual(["pending"])
  })
})

describe("table filters", () => {
  it("round-trips and builds API params", () => {
    const s = "?sort=top&board=b1&tag=t1%2Ct2&visibility=hidden&q=crash"
    const f = parseTableFilters(params(s))
    expect(tableSearch(f)).toBe(s)
    expect(apiParams(f)).toEqual({
      sort: "top",
      status: OPEN_STATUSES.join(","),
      board: "b1",
      tag: "t1,t2",
      visibility: "hidden",
      q: "crash",
    })
  })
})
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import type { InfiniteData } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { Page, RequestDetail, RequestItem } from "@/lib/api"
import { keys, makeQueryClient, patchRequest, toggledVote } from "@/lib/query"

const item = (id: string, votes: number, voted = false) => ({ id, votes, voted }) as RequestItem

describe("patchRequest", () => {
  it("updates the request in lists, similar results and its detail page", () => {
    const qc = makeQueryClient()
    const page: Page<RequestItem> = { items: [item("a", 3), item("b", 1)], page: 1, perPage: 20, totalItems: 2 }
    qc.setQueryData(keys.requests("board", {}), { pages: [page], pageParams: [1] })
    qc.setQueryData(keys.similar("dark"), [item("a", 3)])
    qc.setQueryData(keys.request("board", "a"), { ...item("a", 3), thread: [] } as unknown as RequestDetail)

    patchRequest(qc, "a", toggledVote)

    const list = qc.getQueryData<InfiniteData<Page<RequestItem>>>(keys.requests("board", {}))!
    expect(list.pages[0].items.map((r) => [r.votes, r.voted])).toEqual([
      [4, true],
      [1, false],
    ])
    expect(qc.getQueryData<RequestItem[]>(keys.similar("dark"))![0].votes).toBe(4)
    expect(qc.getQueryData<RequestDetail>(keys.request("board", "a"))!.voted).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

Create `web/lib/errors.test.ts`:

```ts
import { ClientResponseError } from "pocketbase"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { errorMessage, isAbort, isAuthError } from "@/lib/errors"

const err = (status: number, response: object = {}, isAbort = false) =>
  new ClientResponseError({ status, response, isAbort })

describe("errorMessage", () => {
  it("is friendly for rate limits and lost connections", () => {
    expect(errorMessage(err(429))).toBe("Slow down a little. Try again in a minute.")
    expect(errorMessage(err(0))).toBe("Couldn't reach the board. Check your connection and try again.")
  })

  it("names the field problem, then the server's message", () => {
    expect(
      errorMessage(err(400, { message: "Failed.", data: { title: { message: "Must be at least 3 characters." } } }))
    ).toBe("Must be at least 3 characters.")
    expect(errorMessage(err(403, { message: "Your account can't vote on this board." }))).toBe(
      "Your account can't vote on this board."
    )
    expect(errorMessage(new Error("Plain"))).toBe("Plain")
    expect(errorMessage("weird", "Fallback.")).toBe("Fallback.")
  })
})

describe("error kinds", () => {
  it("tells aborts and auth failures apart from blips", () => {
    expect(isAbort(err(0, {}, true))).toBe(true)
    expect(isAbort(err(0))).toBe(false)
    expect(isAuthError(err(401))).toBe(true)
    expect(isAuthError(err(403))).toBe(true)
    expect(isAuthError(err(0, {}, true))).toBe(false)
    expect(isAuthError(err(500))).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

```ts
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useCooldown(10_000))
    expect(result.current[0]).toBe(true)

    expect(result.current[0]).toBe(false)
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(9_999))
    expect(result.current[0]).toBe(false)
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(1))
    expect(result.current[0]).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

Create `web/lib/pocketbase.ts`:

```ts
// Two PocketBase clients on the same origin, each with its own saved session:
// - pb: board users (the public site). When an admin opens the dashboard, their linked board
//   profile is signed in here too, so they comment, vote and suggest as that profile.
// - pbAdmin: superusers (the dashboard).
// Auto-cancellation is off: TanStack Query dedupes and cancels requests itself, and the SDK's
// own cancelling would abort legitimate parallel calls.
import PocketBase, { LocalAuthStore } from "pocketbase"

function client(storageKey: string) {
  const c = new PocketBase("/", new LocalAuthStore(storageKey))
  c.autoCancellation(false)
  return c
}

export const pb = client("lort_user")
export const pbAdmin = client("lort_admin")

export type Client = PocketBase
```

Create `web/lib/auth.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

// Reading the signed-in record from PocketBase in React.
//
// LocalAuthStore returns a new record object on every read, so handing authStore.record to
// useSyncExternalStore makes React see a change on every render and loop forever. The
// snapshot is the record as a JSON string instead (equal between reads), parsed with useMemo.
import { useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from "react"
import { pb, pbAdmin, type Client } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

export interface User {
  id: string
  email: string
  name: string
  avatar: string
  verified: boolean
  isAdmin: boolean
  banned: boolean
}

export interface Admin {
  id: string
  email: string
}

function snapshot(c: Client): string {
  return c.authStore.isValid ? JSON.stringify(c.authStore.record) : "null"
}

const subscribers = new Map<Client, (cb: () => void) => () => void>()

function subscribe(c: Client) {
  let fn = subscribers.get(c)
  if (!fn) {
    fn = (cb) => c.authStore.onChange(() => cb())
    subscribers.set(c, fn)
  }
  return fn
}

export function useAuthRecord<T>(c: Client): T | null {
  const snap = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe(c), () => snapshot(c), () => "null")
  return useMemo(() => JSON.parse(snap) as T | null, [snap])
}

/** The signed-in board user, or null. */
export function useUser() {
  return useAuthRecord<User>(pb)
}

/** The signed-in dashboard admin (a superuser), or null. */
export function useAdmin() {
  return useAuthRecord<Admin>(pbAdmin)
}
```

Create `web/lib/api.ts`:

```ts
// Typed calls to Lort's /api/lort endpoints. Each takes the client to call with: pb for the
// board user (and an admin's linked profile), pbAdmin for team endpoints.
import type { Client } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import type { Ref } from "@/lib/config"
import type { Status, TriageActionId } from "@/lib/status"

export type { Ref }

export interface Person {
  id: string // "" when the account was deleted
  name: string
  avatar: string // file name on the users record, "" for none
  team: boolean
}

export interface RequestItem {
  id: string
  title: string
  body: string
  status: Status
  board: Ref
  author: Person
  tags: Ref[]
  comments: number
  votes: number
  voted: boolean
  visible: boolean
  awaitingApproval: boolean
  new: boolean // team view only: not opened by the team yet
  created: string
}

export interface Page<T> {
  items: T[]
  page: number
  perPage: number
  totalItems: number
}

export interface Comment {
  id: string
  body: string
  images: string[]
  internal: boolean
  author: Person
  created: string
  replies: Comment[]
}

export type EventKind = "created" | "status_changed" | "merged_in" | "merged_from" | "board_changed"

export interface TimelineEvent {
  id: string
  kind: EventKind
  actor: Person
  from: string
  to: string
  ref: Ref | null
  created: string
}

export interface RequestDetail extends RequestItem {
  images: string[]
  public: boolean // on a public board and visible: its images load without a file token
  mergedInto: Ref | null
  thread: Comment[] // called thread because comments is the count
  activity: TimelineEvent[]
}

export interface StatusChange {
  from: Status
  to: Status
  roadmap: "added" | "removed" | ""
}

export interface QueueInfo {
  position: number
  total: number
  next: string
}

export interface Stats {
  total: number
  waiting: number
  new: number
}

export interface TeamMember {
  id: string
  email: string
  name: string
  created: string
}

export interface UserSummary {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  joined: string
  requests: number
  votes: number
  verified: boolean
  banned: boolean
  team: boolean
}

const json = (body: unknown) => ({ method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(body), headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } })

export const PAGE_SIZE = 20

export function listRequests(c: Client, params: Record<string, string>, page = 1): Promise<Page<RequestItem>> {
  return c.send("/api/lort/requests", { query: { ...params, page, perPage: PAGE_SIZE } })
}

export function getRequest(c: Client, id: string): Promise<RequestDetail> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}`, {})
}

export async function similar(c: Client, q: string): Promise<RequestItem[]> {
  const res = await c.send<{ items: RequestItem[] }>("/api/lort/similar", { query: { q } })
  return res.items
}

export function vote(c: Client, id: string): Promise<{ voted: boolean; votes: number }> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/vote`, { method: "POST" })
}

export function setStatus(c: Client, id: string, action: TriageActionId): Promise<StatusChange> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/status`, json({ action }))
}

export function undoComplete(c: Client, id: string): Promise<StatusChange> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/undo`, { method: "POST" })
}

export function merge(c: Client, id: string, target: string): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/merge`, json({ target }))
}

export function markSeen(c: Client, id: string): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/seen`, { method: "POST" })
}

export function moveBoard(c: Client, id: string, board: string): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/board`, json({ board }))
}

export function reassign(c: Client, id: string, email: string): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/requests/${id}/author`, json({ email }))
}

export function queue(c: Client, params: Record<string, string>, current: string): Promise<QueueInfo> {
  return c.send("/api/lort/queue", { query: { ...params, current } })
}

export function stats(c: Client): Promise<Stats> {
  return c.send("/api/lort/stats", {})
}

export async function teamList(c: Client): Promise<{ items: TeamMember[]; you: string }> {
  return c.send("/api/lort/team", {})
}

export function teamMe(c: Client): Promise<{ profile: Record<string, unknown>; token: string }> {
  return c.send("/api/lort/team/me", {})
}

export async function invite(c: Client, email: string): Promise<string> {
  const res = await c.send<{ link: string }>("/api/lort/team/invite", json({ email }))
  return res.link
}

export function removeAdmin(c: Client, id: string): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/team/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" })
}

export function userSummary(c: Client, id: string): Promise<UserSummary> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/users/${id}`, {})
}

export function setBanned(c: Client, id: string, banned: boolean): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/users/${id}/ban`, json({ banned }))
}

export async function resetLink(c: Client, id: string): Promise<string> {
  const res = await c.send<{ link: string }>(`/api/lort/users/${id}/reset-link`, { method: "POST" })
  return res.link
}

export function setOrder(c: Client, collection: "boards" | "tags", ids: string[]): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/order/${collection}`, json({ ids }))
}

export function deleteBoard(c: Client, id: string, moveTo = ""): Promise<void> {
  return c.send(`/api/lort/boards/${id}`, { method: "DELETE", query: moveTo ? { moveTo } : {} })
}
```

Create `web/lib/errors.ts`:

```ts
import { ClientResponseError } from "pocketbase"

const FALLBACK = "Something went wrong. Please try again."

/** A friendly, sentence-case message for any error from PocketBase or Lort. */
export function errorMessage(err: unknown, fallback = FALLBACK): string {
  if (!(err instanceof ClientResponseError)) return err instanceof Error && err.message ? err.message : fallback
  if (err.status === 429) return "Slow down a little. Try again in a minute."
  if (err.status === 0) return "Couldn't reach the board. Check your connection and try again."
  // Field errors ({ data: { title: { message } } }) name the actual problem.
  const fields = (err.response?.data ?? {}) as Record<string, { message?: string }>
  const first = Object.values(fields).find((f) => f?.message)?.message
  return first || err.response?.message || fallback
}

/** True for a request the client gave up on (navigation, unmount), which is not a failure. */
export function isAbort(err: unknown): boolean {
  return err instanceof ClientResponseError && err.isAbort
}

/** True when the server says the session is no longer valid. */
export function isAuthError(err: unknown): boolean {
  return err instanceof ClientResponseError && (err.status === 401 || err.status === 403)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write time, filters, timeline and the query helpers**

Create `web/lib/time.ts`:

```ts
const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"]

/** "14 Oct", or "14 Oct 2025" outside the current year. */
export function shortDate(iso: string, now = new Date()): string {
  const d = new Date(iso)
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")
  const base = `${day} ${MONTHS[d.getMonth()]}`
  return d.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() ? base : `${base} ${d.getFullYear()}`
}

/** "just now", "5 min ago", "2 hours ago", "yesterday", "3 days ago", then shortDate. */
export function relativeTime(iso: string, now = new Date()): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, (now.getTime() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 1000)
  if (s < 60) return "just now"
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
  if (m < 60) return `${m} min ago`
  const h = Math.floor(m / 60)
  if (h < 24) return h === 1 ? "1 hour ago" : `${h} hours ago`
  const d = Math.floor(h / 24)
  if (d === 1) return "yesterday"
  if (d < 7) return `${d} days ago`
  return shortDate(iso, now)
}
```

Create `web/lib/filters.ts`:

```ts
// Filters live in the URL so a refresh or a shared link keeps them. Defaults are left out of
// the URL. A cleared status filter ("every status") is written as status=all, because no
// status parameter means the default: the open statuses.
import { isStatus, OPEN_STATUSES, type Status } from "@/lib/status"

type Params = URLSearchParams | { get(name: string): string | null }

export type BoardSort = "trending" | "newest"
export type TableSort = "newest" | "oldest" | "top" | "updated"
export type Visibility = "" | "public" | "hidden"

export interface BoardFilters {
  sort: BoardSort
  status: Status[] // empty means every status
  board: string[]
  q: string
}

export interface TableFilters {
  sort: TableSort
  status: Status[]
  board: string[]
  tag: string[]
  visibility: Visibility
  q: string
}

function list(v: string | null): string[] {
  return v ? v.split(",").filter(Boolean) : []
}

function statuses(v: string | null): Status[] {
  if (v === null) return [...OPEN_STATUSES]
  if (v === "all") return []
  return list(v).filter(isStatus)
}

const sameSet = (a: string[], b: string[]) => a.length === b.length && a.every((x) => b.includes(x))

function writeStatus(p: URLSearchParams, s: Status[]) {
  if (s.length === 0) p.set("status", "all")
  else if (!sameSet(s, OPEN_STATUSES)) p.set("status", s.join(","))
}

export function parseBoardFilters(p: Params): BoardFilters {
  return {
    sort: p.get("sort") === "newest" ? "newest" : "trending",
    status: statuses(p.get("status")),
    board: list(p.get("board")),
    q: p.get("q") ?? "",
  }
}

export function boardSearch(f: BoardFilters): string {
  const p = new URLSearchParams()
  if (f.sort !== "trending") p.set("sort", f.sort)
  writeStatus(p, f.status)
  if (f.board.length) p.set("board", f.board.join(","))
  if (f.q) p.set("q", f.q)
  const s = p.toString()
  return s ? `?${s}` : ""
}

const TABLE_SORTS: TableSort[] = ["newest", "oldest", "top", "updated"]

export function parseTableFilters(p: Params): TableFilters {
  const sort = p.get("sort") as TableSort
  const vis = p.get("visibility")
  return {
    sort: TABLE_SORTS.includes(sort) ? sort : "newest",
    status: statuses(p.get("status")),
    board: list(p.get("board")),
    tag: list(p.get("tag")),
    visibility: vis === "public" || vis === "hidden" ? vis : "",
    q: p.get("q") ?? "",
  }
}

export function tableSearch(f: TableFilters): string {
  const p = new URLSearchParams()
  if (f.sort !== "newest") p.set("sort", f.sort)
  writeStatus(p, f.status)
  if (f.board.length) p.set("board", f.board.join(","))
  if (f.tag.length) p.set("tag", f.tag.join(","))
  if (f.visibility) p.set("visibility", f.visibility)
  if (f.q) p.set("q", f.q)
  const s = p.toString()
  return s ? `?${s}` : ""
}

/** Query parameters for GET /api/lort/requests and /api/lort/queue. */
export function apiParams(f: BoardFilters | TableFilters): Record<string, string> {
  const out: Record<string, string> = { sort: f.sort }
  if (f.status.length) out.status = f.status.join(",")
  if (f.board.length) out.board = f.board.join(",")
  if ("tag" in f && f.tag.length) out.tag = f.tag.join(",")
  if ("visibility" in f && f.visibility) out.visibility = f.visibility
  if (f.q) out.q = f.q
  return out
}
```

Create `web/lib/timeline.ts`:

```ts
import type { Comment, RequestDetail, TimelineEvent } from "@/lib/api"

export type TimelineEntry = { type: "event"; event: TimelineEvent } | { type: "comment"; comment: Comment }

/** Timeline entries and top-level comments together, newest first. Replies stay inside their comment, oldest first. */
export function buildTimeline(d: Pick<RequestDetail, "activity" | "thread">): TimelineEntry[] {
  const entries: Array<{ at: number; entry: TimelineEntry }> = [
    ...d.activity.map((event) => ({ at: Date.parse(event.created), entry: { type: "event" as const, event } })),
    ...d.thread.map((comment) => ({ at: Date.parse(comment.created), entry: { type: "comment" as const, comment } })),
  ]
  return entries.sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at).map((e) => e.entry)
}
```

Replace `web/lib/query.ts`:

```ts
import { QueryClient, type InfiniteData } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { Page, RequestDetail, RequestItem } from "@/lib/api"

/** Query keys. Every request list starts with "requests" so one invalidation refreshes them all. */
export const keys = {
  requests: (scope: "board" | "team", params?: Record<string, string>) => ["requests", scope, params ?? {}] as const,
  request: (scope: "board" | "team", id: string) => ["request", scope, id] as const,
  similar: (q: string) => ["requests", "similar", q] as const,
  queue: (params: Record<string, string>, id: string) => ["queue", params, id] as const,
  stats: ["stats"] as const,
  team: ["team"] as const,
  boards: ["boards"] as const,
  tags: ["tags"] as const,
  settings: ["settings"] as const,
  user: (id: string) => ["user", id] as const,
}

export function makeQueryClient() {
  return new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: { staleTime: 30_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: false, retry: 1 },
    },
  })
}

type Patch = (r: RequestItem) => RequestItem

/** Applies patch to request id wherever it is cached: board and team lists, similar results, detail pages. */
export function patchRequest(qc: QueryClient, id: string, patch: Patch) {
  const each = <T extends RequestItem>(r: T): T => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ...patch(r) } : r)
  qc.setQueriesData<InfiniteData<Page<RequestItem>> | RequestItem[]>({ queryKey: ["requests"] }, (data) => {
    if (!data) return data
    if (Array.isArray(data)) return data.map(each)
    return { ...data, pages: data.pages.map((p) => ({ ...p, items: p.items.map(each) })) }
  })
  qc.setQueriesData<RequestDetail>({ queryKey: ["request"] }, (d) => (d && d.id === id ? each(d) : d))
}

/** The optimistic side of a vote toggle. */
export function toggledVote(r: RequestItem): RequestItem {
  return { ...r, voted: !r.voted, votes: Math.max(0, r.votes + (r.voted ? -1 : 1)) }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the freshness hooks**

`useLiveRefresh` is the one place realtime is wired. Every list page calls it with the query keys it shows. Mutations still invalidate their own keys (see the known traps).

Create `web/lib/realtime.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { useQueryClient, type QueryKey } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { Client } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

const DEBOUNCE_MS = 300
const POLL_MS = 3 * 60_000
const STATUS_MS = 5_000

/**
 * Keeps lists fresh. Subscribes to a collection over PocketBase realtime (SSE) and invalidates
 * `keys` 300 ms after the last change. Leaving the page cancels a pending refetch, so code that
 * changes data must still invalidate its own queries. While the connection is down it polls
 * every 3 minutes, and it catches up once on reconnect.
 *
 * Returns `live` (realtime connected, for the dashboard's Live indicator). `onCreate` hears
 * about new records, for the "just arrived" tint.
 */
export function useLiveRefresh(
  client: Client,
  collection: string,
  keys: QueryKey[],
  onCreate?: (id: string) => void
): { live: boolean } {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [live, setLive] = useState(false)
  const keysRef = useRef(keys)
  const onCreateRef = useRef(onCreate)
  useEffect(() => {
    keysRef.current = keys
    onCreateRef.current = onCreate
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
    const unsubs: Array<() => Promise<void>> = []
    const keep = (u: () => Promise<void>) => {
      if (cancelled) void u().catch(() => {})
      else unsubs.push(u)
    }
    const refresh = () => keysRef.current.forEach((k) => void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: k }))
    const schedule = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer)
      timer = setTimeout(refresh, DEBOUNCE_MS)
    }

    client
      .collection(collection)
      .subscribe("*", (e) => {
        if (e.action === "create") onCreateRef.current?.(e.record.id)
        schedule()
      })
      .then((u) => {
        keep(u)
        if (!cancelled) setLive(true)
      })
      .catch(() => setLive(false))

    let connectedBefore = false
    client.realtime
      .subscribe("PB_CONNECT", () => {
        if (connectedBefore) schedule() // a reconnect: catch up on what was missed
        connectedBefore = true
      })
      .then(keep)
      .catch(() => {})

    const status = setInterval(() => setLive(client.realtime.isConnected), STATUS_MS)
    const poll = setInterval(() => {
      if (!client.realtime.isConnected) refresh()
    }, POLL_MS)

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
      clearTimeout(timer)
      clearInterval(status)
      clearInterval(poll)
      unsubs.forEach((u) => void u().catch(() => {}))
    }
  }, [client, collection, qc])

  return { live }
}
```

Create `web/lib/use-cooldown.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"

/** [ready, start]: after start(), ready is false for ms. The refresh buttons use 10 seconds. */
export function useCooldown(ms: number): [boolean, () => void] {
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(true)
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined)
  useEffect(() => () => clearTimeout(timer.current), [])
  const start = useCallback(() => {
    setReady(false)
    clearTimeout(timer.current)
    timer.current = setTimeout(() => setReady(true), ms)
  }, [ms])
  return [ready, start]
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react"
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event"
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest"
import { addImages, Composer, MAX_IMAGE_BYTES } from "@/components/lort/composer"
import { filterSummary } from "@/components/lort/filter-menu"
import { FloatingBar } from "@/components/lort/floating-bar"

import { OPEN_STATUSES } from "@/lib/status"

const file = (name: string, size: number, type = "image/png") => {
  const f = new File(["x"], name, { type })
  Object.defineProperty(f, "size", { value: size })
  return f
}

describe("filterSummary", () => {
  const opts = [...OPEN_STATUSES, "completed", "closed"].map((v) => ({ value: v, label: v }))
  const p = { allLabel: "Every status", noun: "statuses", options: opts }
  it("summarises the selection", () => {

    expect(filterSummary(p, ["closed"])).toBe("closed")
    expect(filterSummary(p, ["closed", "completed"])).toBe("2 statuses")
    expect(filterSummary({ ...p, summarize: (v) => (v.length === 4 ? "Open ideas" : undefined) }, OPEN_STATUSES)).toBe(
      "Open ideas"
    )

})

describe("FloatingBar", () => {

    onSortChange: vi.fn(),
    selectedBoards: [],
    onSelectedBoardsChange: vi.fn(),

    expect(screen.getByText("Open ideas")).toBeInTheDocument()

      <FloatingBar
        {...props}
        boards={[

          { id: "b2", name: "Bugs" },
        ]}
      />

    expect(screen.getByText("All boards")).toBeInTheDocument()

})

describe("Composer", () => {
  it("limits images to three under 5 MB each", () => {
    const three = [file("a.png", 10), file("b.png", 10), file("c.png", 10)]
    expect(addImages([], [...three, file("d.png", 10)])).toEqual({
      images: three,
      error: "You can attach up to 3 images.",

    const onSubmit = vi
      .fn()

      .mockImplementationOnce(() => new Promise<void>((resolve) => (finish = resolve)))

    expect(field).toHaveValue("Same on iOS")

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Send" }))
    finish()
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(field).toHaveValue(""))
    expect(onSubmit).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ text: "Same on iOS", images: [], visibility: "public" })
  })

    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /Visibility/ })).toBeNull()

  })
})
```

```tsx
"use client"

import { useId } from "react"
import { motion } from "motion/react"
import { Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface SortOption<T extends string> {
  value: T
  label: string
}

export interface SortTabsProps<T extends string> {
  options: SortOption<T>[]
  value: T
  onValueChange: (value: T) => void
  "aria-label"?: string
  size?: "default" | "sm"
  className?: string
}

/** Segmented tabs whose ink pill slides to the selected option (Motion layoutId). */
export function SortTabs<T extends string>({
  options,
  value,
  onValueChange,
  "aria-label": ariaLabel = "Sort",
  size = "default",
  className,
}: SortTabsProps<T>) {
  const layoutId = useId()
  return (
    <Tabs value={value} onValueChange={(v) => onValueChange(v as T)} className={className}>
      <TabsList size={size} aria-label={ariaLabel}>
        {options.map((o) => (
          <TabsTrigger key={o.value} value={o.value} className="data-active:bg-transparent">
            {o.value === value && (
              <motion.span
                layoutId={layoutId}
                aria-hidden
                className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-full bg-foreground"
                transition={{ type: "spring", bounce: 0.18, duration: 0.35 }}
              />
            )}
            <span className={cn(o.value === value && "text-background")}>{o.label}</span>
          </TabsTrigger>
        ))}
      </TabsList>
    </Tabs>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/segmented.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useId } from "react"
import { motion } from "motion/react"
import { Toggle } from "@/components/ui/toggle"
import { ToggleGroup } from "@/components/ui/toggle-group"

export interface SegmentedProps {
  options: { value: string; label: string }[]
  value: string
  onValueChange: (value: string) => void
  "aria-label": string
}

/** Pick one of a few options (the Suggest modal's board): the SortTabs look, as toggle buttons. */
export function Segmented({ options, value, onValueChange, "aria-label": label }: SegmentedProps) {
  const layoutId = useId()
  return (
    <ToggleGroup
      aria-label={label}
      value={[value]}
      onValueChange={(v) => v[0] && onValueChange(v[0])}
      className="rounded-full border border-border bg-background p-1 inset-shadow-sm"
      spacing={0}
    >
      {options.map((o) => (
        <Toggle key={o.value} value={o.value} variant="segment">
          {o.value === value && (
            <motion.span
              layoutId={layoutId}
              aria-hidden
              className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-full bg-foreground"
              transition={{ type: "spring", bounce: 0.18, duration: 0.35 }}
            />
          )}
          {o.label}
        </Toggle>
      ))}
    </ToggleGroup>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/filter-menu.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { IconChevronDown, IconChevronUp } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuCheckboxItem,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"

export interface FilterOption {
  value: string
  label: string
  /** Shown before the label, e.g. a decorative StatusIcon. */
  icon?: ReactNode
}

export interface FilterMenuProps {
  /** Trigger text with nothing (or everything) selected: "All boards". */
  allLabel: string
  /** Plural noun for several: "statuses" makes "3 statuses". */
  noun: string
  options: FilterOption[]
  selected: string[]
  onSelectedChange: (values: string[]) => void
  /** Custom trigger text; return undefined for the default summary. */
  summarize?: (values: string[]) => string | undefined
  /** "up" for the floating bar at the bottom of the board. */
  side?: "top" | "bottom"
  className?: string
}

export function filterSummary(
  p: Pick<FilterMenuProps, "allLabel" | "noun" | "options" | "summarize">,
  values: string[]
): string {
  const custom = p.summarize?.(values)
  if (custom) return custom
  if (values.length === 0 || values.length === p.options.length) return p.allLabel
  if (values.length === 1) return p.options.find((o) => o.value === values[0])?.label ?? p.allLabel
  return `${values.length} ${p.noun}`
}

/** A multi-select filter behind a pill: checkbox items that keep the menu open, Clear at the bottom. */
export function FilterMenu(props: FilterMenuProps) {
  const { allLabel, options, selected, onSelectedChange, side = "bottom", className } = props
  const toggle = (v: string, on: boolean) =>
    onSelectedChange(on ? [...selected, v] : selected.filter((x) => x !== v))
  const Chevron = side === "top" ? IconChevronUp : IconChevronDown
  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<Button variant="secondary" className={className} />}>
        {filterSummary(props, selected)}
        <Chevron className="size-[15px]" />
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent side={side} sideOffset={10} className="min-w-[220px]" aria-label={allLabel}>
        {options.map((o) => (
          <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem
            key={o.value}
            checked={selected.includes(o.value)}
            onCheckedChange={(on) => toggle(o.value, on)}
          >
            {o.icon}
            {o.label}
          </DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>
        ))}
        <DropdownMenuSeparator />
        <DropdownMenuItem
          disabled={selected.length === 0}
          onClick={() => onSelectedChange([])}
          className="text-caption font-semibold text-muted-foreground"
        >
          Clear
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/floating-bar.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { IconFilter, IconPlus } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { FilterMenu } from "@/components/lort/filter-menu"
import { SortTabs } from "@/components/lort/sort-tabs"
import { StatusIcon } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import type { Ref } from "@/lib/config"
import type { BoardSort } from "@/lib/filters"
import { OPEN_STATUSES, STATUSES, STATUS_META, type Status } from "@/lib/status"

export const BOARD_SORTS: { value: BoardSort; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "trending", label: "Trending" },
  { value: "newest", label: "Newest" },
]

/** "Open ideas" when the selection is exactly the four open statuses. */
export function openIdeas(values: string[]): string | undefined {
  return values.length === OPEN_STATUSES.length && OPEN_STATUSES.every((s) => values.includes(s)) ? "Open ideas" : undefined
}

export const STATUS_OPTIONS = STATUSES.map((s) => ({
  value: s,
  label: STATUS_META[s].label,
  icon: <StatusIcon status={s} decorative />,
}))

export interface FloatingBarProps {
  /** Public boards; the Board filter is hidden when there is only one. */
  boards: Ref[]
  sort: BoardSort
  onSortChange: (sort: BoardSort) => void
  selectedBoards: string[]
  onSelectedBoardsChange: (ids: string[]) => void
  selectedStatuses: Status[]
  onSelectedStatusesChange: (statuses: Status[]) => void
  onSuggest: () => void
  /** Phones: opens the filters bottom sheet. */
  onOpenFilters: () => void
}

/** The pill bar at the bottom of /feedback. On phones: sort tabs, one filter button, a round "+". */
export function FloatingBar(p: FloatingBarProps) {
  return (
    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border-strong bg-surface-2 p-2 inset-shadow-sm shadow-float">
      <SortTabs options={BOARD_SORTS} value={p.sort} onValueChange={p.onSortChange} />
      <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 sm:flex">
        {p.boards.length > 1 && (
          <FilterMenu
            side="top"
            allLabel="All boards"
            noun="boards"
            options={p.boards.map((b) => ({ value: b.id, label: b.name }))}
            selected={p.selectedBoards}
            onSelectedChange={p.onSelectedBoardsChange}
          />
        )}
        <FilterMenu
          side="top"
          allLabel="Every status"
          noun="statuses"
          options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
          selected={p.selectedStatuses}
          onSelectedChange={(v) => p.onSelectedStatusesChange(v as Status[])}
          summarize={openIdeas}
        />
        <Button onClick={p.onSuggest}>
          <IconPlus stroke={2.4} />
          Suggest something
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:hidden">
        <Button variant="secondary" size="icon" aria-label="Filter" onClick={p.onOpenFilters}>
          <IconFilter />
        </Button>
        <Button size="icon-lg" aria-label="Suggest something" onClick={p.onSuggest}>
          <IconPlus stroke={2.4} />
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/composer.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { IconArrowUp, IconLock, IconPhoto, IconWorld, IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export type Visibility = "public" | "internal"

export interface ComposerValue {
  text: string
  images: File[]
  visibility: Visibility
}

export interface ComposerProps {
  placeholder?: string
  /** Admins can switch to an internal note. */
  canUseInternal?: boolean
  /**
   * Called with the draft. While the promise is pending the draft is locked; it clears when the
   * promise resolves and stays, with the error's message, when it rejects.
   */
  onSubmit: (value: ComposerValue) => Promise<void>
  /** The Suggest modal's look: surface-2 with a strong border so it stands off the modal. */
  raised?: boolean
  /** Label for the field (screen readers); defaults to "Comment". */
  label?: string
  autoFocus?: boolean
  className?: string
}

export const MAX_IMAGES = 3
export const MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024

/** Adds picked files to the current ones, applying the composer's limits. Pure, for tests. */
export function addImages(current: File[], picked: File[]): { images: File[]; error: string } {
  const images = picked.filter((f) => f.type.startsWith("image/"))
  let error = images.length < picked.length ? "Only images can be attached." : ""
  const tooBig = images.find((f) => f.size > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES)
  if (tooBig) error = `${tooBig.name} is over 5 MB. Try a smaller screenshot.`
  const ok = images.filter((f) => f.size <= MAX_IMAGE_BYTES)
  const room = MAX_IMAGES - current.length
  if (ok.length > room) error = `You can attach up to ${MAX_IMAGES} images.`
  return { images: [...current, ...ok.slice(0, Math.max(0, room))], error }
}

/** The prompt-box field for comments, notes and suggestion details. Voice dictation arrives in phase 5. */
export function Composer({
  placeholder = "Write a comment…",
  canUseInternal = false,
  onSubmit,
  raised = false,
  label = "Comment",
  autoFocus,
  className,
}: ComposerProps) {
  const [text, setText] = useState("")
  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState<Visibility>("public")
  const [images, setImages] = useState<File[]>([])
  const [error, setError] = useState("")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const fieldId = useId()
  const internal = visibility === "internal"

  const previews = useMemo(() => images.map((f) => URL.createObjectURL(f)), [images])
  useEffect(() => () => previews.forEach((u) => URL.revokeObjectURL(u)), [previews])

  const submit = async () => {
    if (!text.trim() || pending) return
    setPending(true)
    setError("")
    try {
      await onSubmit({ text: text.trim(), images, visibility })
      setText("")
      setImages([])
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err, "That didn't send. Your text is still here, so try again."))
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <form
      className={cn(
        "rounded-xl border bg-card px-4 pt-4 pb-2.5 transition-colors focus-within:border-border-strong",
        raised && "border-border-strong bg-surface-2 [--si-ground:var(--surface-2)]",
        internal && "border-2 border-dashed border-note-border bg-note-bg",
        className
      )}
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        void submit()
      }}
    >
      <label htmlFor={fieldId} className="sr-only">
        {internal ? "Internal note" : label}
      </label>
      {previews.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-2.5 flex gap-2">
          {previews.map((src, i) => (
            <span key={src} className="relative size-16 overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-surface-2">
              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- local object URL preview */}
              <img src={src} alt={images[i]?.name ?? ""} className="size-full object-cover" />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="absolute top-1 right-1 grid size-5 place-items-center rounded-full bg-foreground text-background"
                aria-label={`Remove ${images[i]?.name ?? "image"}`}
                disabled={pending}
                onClick={() => setImages(images.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
              >
                <IconX className="size-3" stroke={3} />
              </button>
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <Textarea
        id={fieldId}
        value={text}
        rows={2}
        readOnly={pending}
        aria-busy={pending}
        autoFocus={autoFocus}
        placeholder={internal ? "Leave a note only the team can see…" : placeholder}
        onChange={(e) => !pending && setText(e.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "Enter" && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey)) {
            e.preventDefault()
            void submit()
          }
        }}
        className="max-h-60 min-h-[52px] rounded-none border-0 bg-transparent p-0 text-body focus-visible:outline-none dark:bg-transparent"
      />
      {error && (
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-caption text-danger" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <input
          ref={fileRef}
          type="file"
          accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif"
          multiple
          hidden
          onChange={(e) => {
            const r = addImages(images, Array.from(e.target.files ?? []))
            setImages(r.images)
            setError(r.error)
            e.target.value = ""
          }}
        />
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          size="icon"
          className="size-10"
          aria-label={`Attach images (up to ${MAX_IMAGES})`}
          disabled={pending || images.length >= MAX_IMAGES}
          onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
        >
          <IconPhoto />
        </Button>
        {canUseInternal && (
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            disabled={pending}
            aria-label={`Visibility: ${internal ? "Internal" : "Public"}. Switch to ${internal ? "public" : "internal"}`}
            onClick={() => setVisibility(internal ? "public" : "internal")}
            className={cn(
              "text-ink-2",
              internal && "text-note-ink hover:bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--note-border)_24%,transparent)] hover:text-note-ink"
            )}
          >
            {internal ? <IconLock /> : <IconWorld />}
            {internal ? "Internal" : "Public"}
          </Button>
        )}
        <span className="flex-1" />
        <Button
          type="submit"
          size="icon"
          className="size-10"
          aria-label="Send"
          aria-busy={pending}
          disabled={!text.trim() || pending}
        >
          <IconArrowUp stroke={2.4} />
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}
```

The field uses `focus-visible:outline-none` because the whole composer is the visible field: its border strengthens on `focus-within`. That's the one place an outline is removed; the form keeps a visible focus state.

Create `web/components/lort/triage-bar.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { IconGitMerge, IconPlayerSkipForward, IconRotateClockwise, IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { triageActions, type Status, type TriageAction } from "@/lib/status"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const ICONS = {
  x: IconX,
  merge: IconGitMerge,
  skip: IconPlayerSkipForward,
  rotate: IconRotateClockwise,
}

const VARIANT = { primary: "default", danger: "destructive", neutral: "neutral", quiet: "ghost" } as const

export interface TriageBarProps {
  status: Status
  onAction: (action: TriageAction) => void
  /** The action whose request is in flight; every button is disabled meanwhile. */
  busy?: TriageAction["id"] | null
  className?: string
}

/** The floating bar on a dashboard request page that moves the request on; the forward action is last and primary. */
export function TriageBar({ status, onAction, busy, className }: TriageBarProps) {
  return (
    <div
      role="toolbar"
      aria-label="Triage"
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-border-strong bg-surface-2 p-1.5 inset-shadow-sm shadow-float",
        className
      )}
    >
      {triageActions(status).map((a) => {
        const Icon = a.icon ? ICONS[a.icon] : null
        return (
          <Button
            key={a.id}
            variant={VARIANT[a.tone]}
            className={cn(a.tone === "primary" && "ml-1")}
            disabled={!!busy}
            aria-busy={busy === a.id}
            onClick={() => onAction(a)}
          >
            {Icon && <Icon />}
            {a.label}
          </Button>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/lort/undo-toast.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

export interface UndoToastOptions {
  message: string
  /** Seconds the undo stays available; drawn as a countdown bar along the bottom. */
  seconds: number
  onUndo: () => void
}

/**
 * A toast with one Undo action and a countdown bar in primary. Sonner has no countdown, so this
 * is a toast.custom(). Sonner wraps custom content in the same styled <li> as every toast (the
 * Toaster's classNames.toast), so this renders only the content, not a second card. It closes
 * itself when the time is up; Undo closes it straight away.
 */
export function undoToast({ message, seconds, onUndo }: UndoToastOptions) {
  return toast.custom(
    (id) => (
      <>
        <span role="status" className="flex-1">
          {message}
        </span>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="h-8 rounded-full px-3 font-bold text-primary-text hover:bg-surface-3"
          onClick={() => {
            toast.dismiss(id)
            onUndo()
          }}
        >
          Undo
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Dismiss"
          className="grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-full text-muted-foreground hover:bg-surface-3 hover:text-foreground"
          onClick={() => toast.dismiss(id)}
        >
          <IconX className="size-3.5" />
        </button>
        <span
          aria-hidden
          className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-[3px] w-full origin-left animate-countdown bg-primary motion-reduce:hidden"
          style={{ animationDuration: `${seconds}s` }}
        />
      </>
    ),
    { duration: seconds * 1000 }
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/board/auth-form.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Field, FieldError, FieldGroup, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

export type AuthMode = "signup" | "signin"

export interface AuthErrors {
  email?: string
  password?: string
}

/** Inline validation, on blur and on submit. Pure, for tests. */
export function validateAuth(email: string, password: string, mode: AuthMode): AuthErrors {
  const errors: AuthErrors = {}
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())) errors.email = "Enter an email address like name@example.com."
  if (mode === "signup" && password.length < 8) errors.password = "Use at least 8 characters."
  if (mode === "signin" && !password) errors.password = "Enter your password."
  return errors
}

export interface AuthFormProps {
  /** Called once signed in. */
  onDone: () => void
  initialMode?: AuthMode
  /** Replaces the default heading, e.g. inside the Suggest modal. */
  heading?: string
}

/**
 * Sign up (email and password only; the name defaults to the part before the @) or sign in.
 * When email is on (Phase 3) a "Check your inbox" step follows sign-up; in 1B it doesn't exist.
 */
export function AuthForm({ onDone, initialMode = "signup", heading }: AuthFormProps) {
  const config = useConfig()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<AuthMode>(initialMode)
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("")
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("")
  const [website, setWebsite] = useState("") // honeypot: people never see it, bots fill it
  const [touched, setTouched] = useState<{ email?: boolean; password?: boolean }>({})
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState("")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)

  const errors = validateAuth(email, password, mode)
  const show = (f: keyof AuthErrors) => (submitted || touched[f] ? errors[f] : undefined)
  const signup = mode === "signup"

  const submit = async () => {
    setSubmitted(true)
    setServerError("")
    if (errors.email || errors.password) return
    setPending(true)
    try {
      const users = pb.collection("users")
      if (signup) await users.create({ email: email.trim(), password, passwordConfirm: password, website })
      await users.authWithPassword(email.trim(), password)
      // Votes and what's visible depend on who is signed in.
      await qc.invalidateQueries()
      onDone()
    } catch (err) {
      setServerError(
        errorMessage(err, signup ? "We couldn't create that account. Try again." : "That email and password don't match.")
      )
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      className="flex flex-col gap-[18px]"
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        void submit()
      }}
    >
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-[26px] leading-[31px] font-bold tracking-[-0.01em]">
          {heading ?? (signup ? `Join the ${config.appName} board` : "Welcome back")}
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-body text-ink-2">
          {signup
            ? `Suggest ideas and stick dots on the ones you want${config.features.email ? `, and hear when ${config.appName} adds them` : ""}.`
            : "Sign in to suggest ideas and vote."}
        </p>
      </div>
      <FieldGroup className="gap-[18px]">
        <Field data-invalid={!!show("email")}>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="auth-email">Email</FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id="auth-email"
            type="email"
            autoComplete="email"
            value={email}
            aria-invalid={!!show("email")}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            onBlur={() => setTouched((t) => ({ ...t, email: true }))}
          />
          <FieldError>{show("email")}</FieldError>
        </Field>
        <Field data-invalid={!!show("password")}>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="auth-password">Password</FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id="auth-password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete={signup ? "new-password" : "current-password"}
            value={password}
            aria-invalid={!!show("password")}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            onBlur={() => setTouched((t) => ({ ...t, password: true }))}
          />
          {show("password") ? (
            <FieldError>{show("password")}</FieldError>
          ) : (
            signup && <p className="ml-[18px] text-caption text-muted-foreground">At least 8 characters.</p>
          )}
        </Field>
      </FieldGroup>
      <input
        type="text"
        name="website"
        tabIndex={-1}
        autoComplete="off"
        aria-hidden
        value={website}
        onChange={(e) => setWebsite(e.target.value)}
        className="absolute -left-[9999px] size-px opacity-0"
      />
      {serverError && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-caption font-medium text-danger">
          {serverError}
        </p>
      )}
      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={pending} aria-busy={pending}>
        {signup ? "Create account" : "Sign in"}
      </Button>
      <p className="text-center text-label font-normal text-muted-foreground">
        {signup ? "Already have one? " : "New here? "}
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="link"
          onClick={() => {
            setMode(signup ? "signin" : "signup")
            setSubmitted(false)
            setServerError("")
          }}
        >
          {signup ? "Sign in" : "Create an account"}
        </Button>
      </p>
    </form>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/board/auth-gate.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { AuthForm } from "@/components/board/auth-form"
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

type RequireUser = (then?: () => void) => void

const AuthGateContext = createContext<RequireUser | null>(null)

/**
 * Hosts the account dialog for the public site. requireUser(then) runs `then` straight away
 * for a signed-in visitor, or opens the dialog and runs it after they sign in or sign up
 * (a sticker click becomes a vote once they're in).
 */
export function AuthGateProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const pending = useRef<(() => void) | undefined>(undefined)

  const requireUser = useCallback<RequireUser>((then) => {
    if (pb.authStore.isValid) {
      then?.()
      return
    }
    pending.current = then
    setOpen(true)
  }, [])

  return (
    <AuthGateContext.Provider value={requireUser}>
      {children}
      <Dialog
        open={open}
        onOpenChange={(o) => {
          setOpen(o)
          if (!o) pending.current = undefined
        }}
      >
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[420px]">
          <DialogTitle className="sr-only">Your account</DialogTitle>
          <AuthForm
            onDone={() => {
              const then = pending.current
              pending.current = undefined
              setOpen(false)
              then?.()
            }}
          />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </AuthGateContext.Provider>
  )
}

/** Outside the public site (the dashboard, where the admin is always signed in) it just runs `then`. */
export function useRequireUser(): RequireUser {
  return useContext(AuthGateContext) ?? runIfSignedIn
}

function runIfSignedIn(then?: () => void) {
  if (pb.authStore.isValid) then?.()
}
```

`useVote` is the one place votes happen on the public site. It updates every cached copy at once and rolls back with a toast; a signed-out visitor signs in first and their vote is then *added*, never toggled off.

Create `web/lib/use-debounced.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"

/** value, but only after it has stopped changing for ms. */
export function useDebounced<T>(value: T, ms: number): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value)
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), ms)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [value, ms])
  return debounced
}
```

Create `web/components/board/use-vote.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useMutation, useQueryClient, type QueryKey } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useRequireUser } from "@/components/board/auth-gate"
import { getRequest, vote } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { patchRequest, toggledVote } from "@/lib/query"

/**
 * Toggles the signed-in user's vote with an optimistic update everywhere the request is cached,
 * rolled back with a toast if the server says no. A signed-out visitor signs in first; their
 * vote is then added (not toggled: they may already have voted from another device).
 */
export function useVote() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const requireUser = useRequireUser()
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => vote(pb, id),
    onMutate: async (id: string) => {
      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })
      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["request"] })
      const snapshot: Array<[QueryKey, unknown]> = [
        ...qc.getQueriesData({ queryKey: ["requests"] }),
        ...qc.getQueriesData({ queryKey: ["request"] }),
      ]
      patchRequest(qc, id, toggledVote)
      return { snapshot }
    },
    onError: (err, _id, ctx) => {
      ctx?.snapshot.forEach(([key, data]) => qc.setQueryData(key, data))
      toast(errorMessage(err, "Your vote didn't stick. Try again."))
    },
    onSuccess: (res, id) => patchRequest(qc, id, (r) => ({ ...r, voted: res.voted, votes: res.votes })),
  })

  return (id: string) => {
    if (pb.authStore.isValid) {
      mutation.mutate(id)
      return
    }
    requireUser(async () => {
      try {
        const d = await getRequest(pb, id)
        if (!d.voted) mutation.mutate(id)
      } catch (err) {
        toast(errorMessage(err))
      }
    })
  }
}
```

Create `web/components/board/search-field.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
import { IconSearch } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { InputGroup, InputGroupAddon, InputGroupInput } from "@/components/ui/input-group"
import { boardSearch, parseBoardFilters } from "@/lib/filters"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const onBoard = (path: string) => path === "/" || path === "/feedback"

/**
 * The top bar's search. On the board it filters as you type (the q parameter, 300 ms after the
 * last key); anywhere else Enter opens the board with the search applied.
 */
export function SearchField({ className, autoFocus }: { className?: string; autoFocus?: boolean }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const params = useSearchParams()
  const urlQ = onBoard(pathname) ? (params.get("q") ?? "") : ""
  const [q, setQ] = useState(urlQ)
  const [prevUrlQ, setPrevUrlQ] = useState(urlQ)
  if (urlQ !== prevUrlQ) {
    // The URL changed under us (back button, "Clear filters"): follow it.
    setPrevUrlQ(urlQ)
    setQ(urlQ)
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!onBoard(pathname) || q === urlQ) return
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      router.replace(`${pathname}${boardSearch({ ...parseBoardFilters(params), q })}`, { scroll: false })
    }, 300)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [q, urlQ, pathname, params, router])

  return (
    <form
      role="search"
      className={cn("w-60", className)}
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!onBoard(pathname)) router.push(`/feedback${boardSearch({ ...parseBoardFilters(new URLSearchParams()), q })}`)
      }}
    >
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <IconSearch className="size-4" />
        </InputGroupAddon>
        <InputGroupInput
          type="search"
          placeholder="Search ideas"
          aria-label="Search ideas"
          value={q}
          autoFocus={autoFocus}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
        />
      </InputGroup>
    </form>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/board/profile-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { Field, FieldError, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { useUser, type User } from "@/lib/auth"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

/**
 * Name, avatar and sign out. The notification switches (It shipped, Changelog, Replies from the
 * team) join in Phase 3 and only when email is on.
 */
export function ProfileDialog({ open, onOpenChange }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void }) {
  const user = useUser()
  if (!user) return null
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[420px]">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Your profile</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>This is how you appear next to your ideas and comments.</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        {/* The popup unmounts when closed, so the form starts fresh each time it opens. */}
        <ProfileForm user={user} onDone={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function ProfileForm({ user, onDone }: { user: User; onDone: () => void }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [name, setName] = useState(user.name)
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState<File | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState("")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)

  const save = async () => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setError("Pick a name people will see next to your ideas.")
      return
    }
    setPending(true)
    setError("")
    try {
      // The SDK refreshes the saved session when you update your own record.
      await pb.collection("users").update(user.id, avatar ? { name: name.trim(), avatar } : { name: name.trim() })
      await qc.invalidateQueries()
      toast("Saved your profile.")
      onDone()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err))
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      className="flex flex-col gap-[18px]"
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        void save()
      }}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <PersonAvatar person={{ id: user.id, name: name || user.name, avatar: user.avatar }} size={56} keyline />
        <Field className="flex-1">
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="profile-avatar">Picture</FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id="profile-avatar"
            type="file"
            accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif"
            className="h-10 pt-1.5"
            onChange={(e) => setAvatar(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)}
          />
        </Field>
      </div>
      <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor="profile-name">Name</FieldLabel>
        <Input id="profile-name" value={name} maxLength={60} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
      </Field>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={async () => {
            pb.authStore.clear()
            await qc.invalidateQueries()
            onDone()
          }}
        >
          Sign out
        </Button>
        <span className="flex-1" />
        <Button type="submit" disabled={pending} aria-busy={pending}>
          Save
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}
```

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { useRequireUser } from "@/components/board/auth-gate"
import { ProfileDialog } from "@/components/board/profile-dialog"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"
import { useUser } from "@/lib/auth"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

/** The avatar at the right of the top bar, or Sign in. */
export function AccountMenu({ size = 38 }: { size?: number }) {
  const user = useUser()
  const requireUser = useRequireUser()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [profileOpen, setProfileOpen] = useState(false)

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => requireUser()}>
        Sign in
      </Button>
    )
  }

  return (
    <>
      <DropdownMenu>
        <DropdownMenuTrigger
          render={<button type="button" className="rounded-full" aria-label="Your account" />}
        >
          <PersonAvatar person={{ ...user, team: user.isAdmin }} size={size} keyline />
        </DropdownMenuTrigger>
        <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-56">
          <DropdownMenuLabel className="text-caption font-semibold text-foreground">
            {user.name}
            <span className="block font-normal text-muted-foreground">{user.email}</span>
          </DropdownMenuLabel>
          <DropdownMenuSeparator />
          <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setProfileOpen(true)}>Your profile</DropdownMenuItem>
          {user.isAdmin && <DropdownMenuItem render={<Link href="/dashboard/requests" />}>Dashboard</DropdownMenuItem>}
          <DropdownMenuItem
            onClick={async () => {
              pb.authStore.clear()
              await qc.invalidateQueries()
            }}
          >
            Sign out
          </DropdownMenuItem>
        </DropdownMenuContent>
      </DropdownMenu>
      <ProfileDialog open={profileOpen} onOpenChange={setProfileOpen} />
    </>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/board/board-shell.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation"
import { IconSearch } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { AccountMenu } from "@/components/board/account-menu"
import { AuthGateProvider } from "@/components/board/auth-gate"
import { BOARD_NAV, LORT_HOME } from "@/components/board/links"
import { SearchField } from "@/components/board/search-field"
import { LogoMark } from "@/components/lort/logo-mark"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

function isCurrent(pathname: string, href: string) {
  return href === "/feedback" ? pathname === "/" || pathname.startsWith("/feedback") : pathname.startsWith(href)
}

/** The public site's frame: top bar, the page, "Powered by Lort", and the optional "Get your own board" card. */
export function BoardShell({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const config = useConfig()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false)

  const nav = (
    <nav aria-label="Board" className="flex gap-1">
      {BOARD_NAV.map((item) => {
        const current = isCurrent(pathname, item.href)
        return (
          <Link
            key={item.href}
            href={item.href}
            aria-current={current ? "page" : undefined}
            className={cn(
              "rounded-full px-[13px] py-[7px] text-label text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground sm:px-3.5 sm:py-2 sm:text-[15px]",
              current && "bg-surface-2 text-foreground"
            )}
          >
            {item.label}
          </Link>
        )
      })}
    </nav>
  )

  return (
    <AuthGateProvider>
      <header className="mx-auto max-w-[1120px] px-4 pt-3.5 sm:flex sm:h-[76px] sm:items-center sm:gap-5 sm:px-8 sm:pt-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <Link href="/feedback" className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2.5 font-display text-lg font-extrabold sm:text-[19px]">
            <LogoMark appName={config.appName} logo={config.logo} size={32} />
            {config.appName}
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 sm:hidden">
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              aria-label="Search"
              aria-expanded={searchOpen}
              onClick={() => setSearchOpen((o) => !o)}
            >
              <IconSearch />
            </Button>
            <AccountMenu size={34} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 sm:mt-0 sm:ml-2.5">{nav}</div>
        {searchOpen && <SearchField autoFocus className="mt-2 w-full sm:hidden" />}
        <div className="hidden flex-1 sm:block" />
        <SearchField className="hidden sm:block" />
        <div className="hidden sm:block">
          <AccountMenu />
        </div>
      </header>
      {children}
      <footer className="flex justify-center pt-14 pb-[140px]">
        <a href={LORT_HOME} className="text-caption font-normal text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
          Powered by <b className="font-bold text-foreground">Lort</b>
        </a>
      </footer>
      {config.showLandingCard && (
        <a
          href={LORT_HOME}
          className="fixed bottom-[34px] left-6 z-20 hidden items-center gap-2.5 rounded-full border bg-card py-2 pr-4 pl-2 text-caption font-semibold inset-shadow-sm shadow-card-float xl:flex"
        >
          <span aria-hidden className="size-[26px] rotate-[8deg] rounded-full border-2 border-keyline bg-status-pending" />
          Get your own board like this
        </a>
      )}
    </AuthGateProvider>
  )
}
```
