Create `web/app/(board)/reset-password/page.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { Suspense, useState } from "react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Field, FieldError, FieldGroup, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

/** Where the team's "Copy password reset link" points. Works without email. */
export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  return (
    <main className="flex justify-center px-4 py-10">
      <Suspense>
        <ResetPassword />
      </Suspense>
    </main>
  )
}

function ResetPassword() {
  const token = useSearchParams().get("token") ?? ""
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("")
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState("")
  const [error, setError] = useState("")
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)

  const submit = async () => {
    if (password.length < 8) return setError("Use at least 8 characters.")
    if (password !== confirm) return setError("The two passwords don't match.")
    setPending(true)
    setError("")
    try {
      await pb.collection("users").confirmPasswordReset(token, password, confirm)
      setDone(true)
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err, "That link has expired. Ask the team for a new one."))
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className="flex w-[420px] max-w-full flex-col gap-[18px] rounded-xl border bg-card px-[30px] pt-8 pb-7">
      <div>
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] leading-[31px] font-bold tracking-[-0.01em]">
          {done ? "You're all set" : "Choose a new password"}
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-body text-ink-2">
          {done ? "Sign in with your new password from the top bar." : "Pick something you'll remember."}
        </p>
      </div>
      {done ? (
        <Button render={<Link href="/feedback" />} variant="secondary" className="w-full">
          Back to the board
        </Button>
      ) : (
        <form
          noValidate
          className="flex flex-col gap-[18px]"
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault()
            void submit()
          }}
        >
          <FieldGroup className="gap-[18px]">
            <Field>
              <FieldLabel htmlFor="reset-password">New password</FieldLabel>
              <Input id="reset-password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
            </Field>
            <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
              <FieldLabel htmlFor="reset-confirm">Type it again</FieldLabel>
              <Input id="reset-confirm" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" value={confirm} onChange={(e) => setConfirm(e.target.value)} />
              <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
            </Field>
          </FieldGroup>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={pending || !token} aria-busy={pending}>
            Save password
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}
```

### Task 11: Suggest something

**Files:**
- Create: `web/components/board/suggest-dialog.tsx`, `web/components/board/suggest-dialog.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's `Composer`, `Segmented`, `VoteSticker`, `StatusLabel`; Task 10's `AuthForm`, `useVote`, `useDebounced`; Task 8's `similar`, `reassign`, `keys`.
- Produces: `SuggestDialog({open, onOpenChange, team?, boards})`; `newRequestPayload(v: NewRequest)`; `NewRequest{board,title,body,tags,images,internal,website}`. The dashboard's "New request" (Task 15) reuses it with `team` (every board, plus "On behalf of").

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Requests are created through PocketBase's record API. Pass a plain object to `create()`: the SDK sends the files as multipart and every other field in `@jsonPayload`, so an admin's `internal: true` arrives as a boolean (Task 2's `TestAdminInternalRequestWithImage` guards the server side). A hand-built `FormData` would send the string `"true"`, which the server ignores.

Create `web/components/board/suggest-dialog.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { newRequestPayload } from "@/components/board/suggest-dialog"

describe("newRequestPayload", () => {
  it("is a plain object so the SDK keeps booleans in @jsonPayload", () => {
    const img = new File(["x"], "a.png", { type: "image/png" })
    const p = newRequestPayload({
      board: "b1",
      title: "  Dark mode  ",
      body: "Please",
      tags: ["t1"],
      images: [img],
      internal: true,
      website: "",
    })
    expect(p).toEqual({ board: "b1", title: "Dark mode", body: "Please", tags: ["t1"], images: [img], internal: true, website: "" })
    expect(p).not.toBeInstanceOf(FormData)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the dialog**

Layout follows `design/canvas/P-Suggest.dc.html`: the dialog sits 72px from the top over the dimmed, blurred board; the board picker is hidden with one board; similar requests appear after 3 characters (300 ms after the last key) with live stickers; the composer is `raised`. A signed-out visitor gets the account form inside the dialog and comes straight back to the form. After creating, invalidate the lists yourself (realtime refetches are debounced and cancelled by the navigation that follows).

Create `web/components/board/suggest-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { AuthForm } from "@/components/board/auth-form"
import { useVote } from "@/components/board/use-vote"
import { Composer, type ComposerValue } from "@/components/lort/composer"
import { Segmented } from "@/components/lort/segmented"
import { StatusLabel } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { VoteSticker } from "@/components/lort/vote-sticker"
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { Field, FieldLabel, FieldTitle } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Toggle } from "@/components/ui/toggle"
import { reassign, similar } from "@/lib/api"
import { useUser } from "@/lib/auth"
import type { Ref } from "@/lib/config"
import { pb, pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { useDebounced } from "@/lib/use-debounced"

export interface NewRequest {
  board: string
  title: string
  body: string
  tags: string[]
  images: File[]
  internal: boolean
  website: string
}

/**
 * The record-API payload for a new request. Pass it to create() as a plain object: the SDK
 * puts the files in the multipart body and everything else in "@jsonPayload", so booleans stay
 * booleans. Never build a FormData by hand. Pure, for tests.
 */
export function newRequestPayload(v: NewRequest) {
  return {
    board: v.board,
    title: v.title.trim(),
    body: v.body,
    tags: v.tags,
    images: v.images,
    internal: v.internal,
    website: v.website,
  }
}

const EMAIL = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/

export interface SuggestDialogProps {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  /** The dashboard's "New request": every board (private ones too) and "On behalf of". */
  team?: boolean
  /** Boards to offer; the first is the default. */
  boards: Ref[]
}

/** "Suggest something": board, title with similar requests, tags and details. Signed-out visitors sign in here first. */
export function SuggestDialog({ open, onOpenChange, team = false, boards }: SuggestDialogProps) {
  const user = useUser()
  const config = useConfig()
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="top-[72px] max-h-[calc(100dvh-96px)] translate-y-0 overflow-y-auto px-[26px] pt-6 pb-5 sm:max-w-[640px]">
        {user || team ? (
          <SuggestForm team={team} boards={boards} onDone={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <DialogTitle className="sr-only">Suggest something</DialogTitle>
            <AuthForm heading={`Sign in to suggest something for ${config.appName}`} onDone={() => {}} />
          </>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function SuggestForm({ team, boards, onDone }: { team: boolean; boards: Ref[]; onDone: () => void }) {
  const config = useConfig()
  const user = useUser()
  const router = useRouter()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const onVote = useVote()
  const [board, setBoard] = useState(boards[0]?.id ?? "")
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("")
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [onBehalf, setOnBehalf] = useState("")
  const [website, setWebsite] = useState("")

  const q = useDebounced(title.trim(), 300)
  const matches = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.similar(q), queryFn: () => similar(pb, q), enabled: q.length >= 3 })

  const submit = async ({ text, images, visibility }: ComposerValue) => {
    if (title.trim().length < 3) throw new Error("Give your idea a title of at least 3 characters.")
    if (!board) throw new Error("Pick a board for your idea.")
    if (team && onBehalf.trim() && !EMAIL.test(onBehalf.trim())) {
      throw new Error("Enter the email of the person you're posting for, like name@example.com.")
    }
    const rec = await pb
      .collection("requests")
      .create<{ id: string; awaitingApproval: boolean }>(
        newRequestPayload({ board, title, body: text, tags, images, internal: visibility === "internal", website })
      )
    if (team && onBehalf.trim()) await reassign(pbAdmin, rec.id, onBehalf.trim())
    await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })
    if (team) await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats })
    toast(rec.awaitingApproval ? "Thanks! The team will look at it before it goes on the board." : "Posted. Thanks for the idea!")
    onDone()
    router.push(team ? `/dashboard/requests/${rec.id}` : `/feedback/${rec.id}`)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <DialogHeader>
        <DialogTitle>{team ? "New request" : "Suggest something"}</DialogTitle>
        <DialogDescription>{team ? "Add one yourself, or for someone who asked." : `Got an idea for ${config.appName}?`}</DialogDescription>
      </DialogHeader>

      {boards.length > 1 && (
        <Field>
          <FieldTitle>Board</FieldTitle>
          <Segmented
            aria-label="Board"
            options={boards.map((b) => ({ value: b.id, label: b.name }))}
            value={board}
            onValueChange={setBoard}
          />
        </Field>
      )}

      <Field>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor="suggest-title">Title</FieldLabel>
        <Input
          id="suggest-title"
          value={title}
          maxLength={140}
          placeholder="Dark theme for writing at night"
          onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
          className="h-auto rounded-[14px] px-4 py-3 font-display text-lg font-bold"
        />
        {q.length >= 3 && (matches.data?.length ?? 0) > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-2.5 rounded-md bg-surface-2 px-3.5 py-3 [--si-ground:var(--surface-2)]">
            <p className="mb-2 text-caption font-semibold text-ink-2">Is it one of these? A dot counts the same as a new post.</p>
            <ul>
              {matches.data!.map((s) => (
                <li key={s.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-2">
                  <VoteSticker size="sm" count={s.votes} voted={s.voted} subject={s.title} onToggle={() => onVote(s.id)} />
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold">{s.title}</div>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-caption text-muted-foreground">
                      <StatusLabel status={s.status} size={14} className="font-medium text-muted-foreground" />· {s.board.name}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <Link
                    href={`/feedback/${s.id}`}
                    onClick={onDone}
                    className="text-caption font-semibold text-primary-text hover:underline"
                  >
                    Open
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        )}
      </Field>

      {config.tags.length > 0 && (
        <Field>
          <FieldTitle>
            Tags <span className="font-medium text-muted-foreground">(optional)</span>
          </FieldTitle>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {config.tags.map((t) => (
              <Toggle
                key={t.id}
                pressed={tags.includes(t.id)}
                onPressedChange={(on) => setTags(on ? [...tags, t.id] : tags.filter((x) => x !== t.id))}
              >
                {t.name}
              </Toggle>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Field>
      )}

      {team && (
        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="suggest-for">
            On behalf of <span className="font-medium text-muted-foreground">(optional)</span>
          </FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id="suggest-for"
            type="email"
            placeholder="name@example.com"
            value={onBehalf}
            onChange={(e) => setOnBehalf(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
      )}

      <input
        type="text"
        name="website"
        tabIndex={-1}
        autoComplete="off"
        aria-hidden
        value={website}
        onChange={(e) => setWebsite(e.target.value)}
        className="absolute -left-[9999px] size-px opacity-0"
      />

      <Field>
        <FieldTitle>Details</FieldTitle>
        <Composer
          raised
          label="Details"
          placeholder="What would it help you do? Screenshots welcome."
          canUseInternal={!!user?.isAdmin}
          onSubmit={submit}
        />
      </Field>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the card, the infinite scroll and the skeleton**

The card follows the design's RequestCard: the title link stretches over the whole card (`after:absolute after:inset-0`), the sticker sits above it (`relative z-10`), and the card shows the focus ring when its link is focused. Closed requests get a read-only sticker. A request that just arrived over realtime gets `animate-fresh` (primary-soft fading to the card surface).

The skeleton follows `design/canvas/S-Feedback.dc.html`, but only the list becomes bones: the headline doesn't depend on data, so it renders for real (spec 14.5: "only the content becomes bones").

Create `web/components/board/request-card.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import Link from "next/link"
import { IconMessageCircle } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import { StatusLabel } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { VoteSticker } from "@/components/lort/vote-sticker"
import type { RequestItem } from "@/lib/api"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface RequestCardProps {
  request: RequestItem
  onVote: (id: string) => void
  /** It just arrived over realtime: start tinted and fade. */
  fresh?: boolean
}

/** One request on /feedback: sticker left; title, two-line excerpt, meta row. The whole card opens the request. */
export function RequestCard({ request: r, onVote, fresh }: RequestCardProps) {
  const closed = r.status === "closed"
  return (
    <article
      className={cn(
        "group relative flex items-start gap-3.5 rounded-lg border bg-card p-4 transition-colors [--si-ground:var(--surface)] hover:bg-surface-2 hover:[--si-ground:var(--surface-2)] has-[a:focus-visible]:outline-3 has-[a:focus-visible]:outline-offset-3 has-[a:focus-visible]:outline-focus sm:gap-[18px] sm:px-[22px] sm:py-5",
        fresh && "animate-fresh"
      )}
    >
      <VoteSticker
        count={r.votes}
        voted={r.voted}
        readOnly={closed}
        subject={r.title}
        onToggle={() => onVote(r.id)}
        className="relative z-10 max-sm:size-10 max-sm:text-sm"
      />
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <h2 className="mt-0.5 font-display text-[17px] leading-[22px] font-bold tracking-[-0.01em] text-balance sm:text-title">
          <Link href={`/feedback/${r.id}`} className="outline-none after:absolute after:inset-0 after:rounded-[inherit]">
            {r.title}
          </Link>
        </h2>
        {r.body && <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-[14px] leading-[21px] text-ink-2 sm:text-body">{r.body}</p>}
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-2 text-caption text-muted-foreground sm:gap-x-3.5">
          <StatusLabel status={r.status} size={18} keyline className="font-medium" />
          <span className="rounded-full bg-surface-2 px-2.5 py-[3px] group-hover:bg-surface-3">{r.board.name}</span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <PersonAvatar person={r.author} size={20} />
            {r.author.name || "Someone"}
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5" aria-label={`${r.comments} comments`}>
            <IconMessageCircle className="size-[15px]" aria-hidden />
            {r.comments}
          </span>
          {r.awaitingApproval && <span>Waiting for approval</span>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/board/load-more.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react"
import { Spinner } from "@/components/ui/spinner"

/** Infinite scroll: calls onVisible when this sentinel nears the viewport, while there is more to load. */
export function LoadMore({ hasMore, loading, onVisible }: { hasMore: boolean; loading: boolean; onVisible: () => void }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const latest = useRef(onVisible)
  useEffect(() => {
    latest.current = onVisible
  })
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current
    if (!el || !hasMore) return
    const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => entries[0]?.isIntersecting && latest.current(), {
      rootMargin: "400px",
    })
    io.observe(el)
    return () => io.disconnect()
  }, [hasMore])
  if (!hasMore) return null
  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="flex justify-center py-6 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden={!loading}>
      {loading && <Spinner className="size-5" aria-label="Loading more" />}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Bone } from "@/components/lort/bone"

// Widths vary per card so the bones read like real titles and excerpts (design/canvas/S-Feedback).
const CARDS = [
  { t: "46%", b: "70%" },
  { t: "58%", b: "52%" },
  { t: "38%", b: "80%" },
  { t: "64%", b: "44%" },
  { t: "50%", b: "66%" },
]

/** The request list while it loads: card-shaped bones; the headline and floating bar render for real. */
export function FeedbackListSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div aria-busy="true" aria-label="Loading ideas" className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 sm:gap-3">
      {CARDS.map((c, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3.5 rounded-lg border bg-card p-4 sm:gap-[18px] sm:px-[22px] sm:py-5">
          <Bone w={58} h={58} className="max-sm:size-10" />
          <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2.5 pt-1">
            <Bone w={c.t} h={20} className="rounded-[8px]" />
            <Bone w="96%" h={13} />
            <Bone w={c.b} h={13} />
            <div className="mt-1.5 flex gap-2.5">
              <Bone w={92} h={20} />
              <Bone w={70} h={20} />
              <Bone w={84} h={20} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the phone filters and the board**

Sort, board and status filters live in the URL (`lib/filters.ts`): a refresh or a shared link keeps them. Infinite scroll loads pages of 20. Lists subscribe to realtime through `useLiveRefresh`. The floating bar is fixed at the bottom centre; on phones its filter button opens the bottom sheet.

Create `web/components/board/filters-sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { STATUS_OPTIONS } from "@/components/lort/floating-bar"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetHeader, SheetTitle } from "@/components/ui/sheet"
import { Toggle } from "@/components/ui/toggle"
import type { Ref } from "@/lib/config"
import type { Status } from "@/lib/status"

export interface FiltersSheetProps {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  boards: Ref[]
  selectedBoards: string[]
  onSelectedBoardsChange: (ids: string[]) => void
  selectedStatuses: Status[]
  onSelectedStatusesChange: (s: Status[]) => void
}

function toggle<T extends string>(list: T[], v: T, on: boolean): T[] {
  return on ? [...list, v] : list.filter((x) => x !== v)
}

/** Phones: the board's Board and Status filters in a bottom sheet, as toggle pills. */
export function FiltersSheet(p: FiltersSheetProps) {
  return (
    <Sheet open={p.open} onOpenChange={p.onOpenChange}>
      <SheetContent side="bottom" className="gap-5 px-5 pt-5">
        <SheetHeader className="p-0">
          <SheetTitle className="font-display text-title">Filters</SheetTitle>
        </SheetHeader>
        {p.boards.length > 1 && (
          <section aria-label="Boards" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {p.boards.map((b) => (
              <Toggle
                key={b.id}
                pressed={p.selectedBoards.includes(b.id)}
                onPressedChange={(on) => p.onSelectedBoardsChange(toggle(p.selectedBoards, b.id, on))}
              >
                {b.name}
              </Toggle>
            ))}
          </section>
        )}
        <section aria-label="Statuses" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
            <Toggle
              key={o.value}
              pressed={p.selectedStatuses.includes(o.value)}
              onPressedChange={(on) => p.onSelectedStatusesChange(toggle(p.selectedStatuses, o.value, on))}
            >
              {o.icon}
              {o.label}
            </Toggle>
          ))}
        </section>
        <div className="flex gap-2 pb-2">
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            onClick={() => {
              p.onSelectedBoardsChange([])
              p.onSelectedStatusesChange([])
            }}
          >
            Clear
          </Button>
          <span className="flex-1" />
          <Button onClick={() => p.onOpenChange(false)}>Show ideas</Button>
        </div>
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/board/feedback-board.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
import { useInfiniteQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { FeedbackListSkeleton } from "@/components/board/feedback-skeleton"
import { FiltersSheet } from "@/components/board/filters-sheet"
import { LoadMore } from "@/components/board/load-more"
import { RequestCard } from "@/components/board/request-card"
import { SuggestDialog } from "@/components/board/suggest-dialog"
import { useVote } from "@/components/board/use-vote"
import { FloatingBar } from "@/components/lort/floating-bar"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Empty, EmptyDescription, EmptyHeader, EmptyTitle } from "@/components/ui/empty"
import { listRequests } from "@/lib/api"
import { apiParams, boardSearch, parseBoardFilters, type BoardFilters } from "@/lib/filters"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { useLiveRefresh } from "@/lib/realtime"
import { OPEN_STATUSES } from "@/lib/status"

const FRESH_MS = 4000

/** /feedback (and /): the headline, the request list and the floating bar. Filters live in the URL. */
export function FeedbackBoard() {
  const config = useConfig()
  const router = useRouter()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const filters = parseBoardFilters(useSearchParams())
  const params = apiParams(filters)
  const onVote = useVote()
  const [suggestOpen, setSuggestOpen] = useState(false)
  const [filtersOpen, setFiltersOpen] = useState(false)
  const [fresh, setFresh] = useState<string[]>([])

  const setFilters = (next: Partial<BoardFilters>) =>
    router.replace(`${pathname}${boardSearch({ ...filters, ...next })}`, { scroll: false })

  const list = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: keys.requests("board", params),
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => listRequests(pb, params, pageParam),
    initialPageParam: 1,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => (last.page * last.perPage < last.totalItems ? last.page + 1 : undefined),
  })

  // useLiveRefresh keeps the latest callback in a ref, so this needn't be memoised.
  const onCreate = (id: string) => {
    setFresh((f) => [...f, id])
    setTimeout(() => setFresh((f) => f.filter((x) => x !== id)), FRESH_MS)
  }
  useLiveRefresh(pb, "requests", [["requests", "board"]], onCreate)

  const items = list.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? []
  const filtered =
    filters.q !== "" || filters.board.length > 0 || filters.status.join() !== OPEN_STATUSES.join()

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[860px] px-4 pt-3.5 sm:px-8 sm:pt-9">
      <h1 className="font-display text-[32px] leading-[34px] font-extrabold tracking-[-0.03em] text-balance sm:text-display-xl">
        Got an idea for {config.appName}?
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-2 mb-[18px] max-w-[60ch] text-body text-ink-2 sm:mt-3 sm:mb-[30px] sm:text-body-lg">
        Stick a dot on the ones you want most.
        <span className="max-sm:hidden"> The team builds what gets the most dots, and tells you when it ships.</span>
      </p>

      {list.isPending ? (
        <FeedbackListSkeleton />
      ) : list.isError ? (
        <Empty>
          <EmptyHeader>
            <EmptyTitle>The ideas didn&apos;t load</EmptyTitle>
            <EmptyDescription>Check your connection, then try again.</EmptyDescription>
          </EmptyHeader>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => list.refetch()}>
            Try again
          </Button>
        </Empty>
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <Empty>
          <EmptyHeader>
            <EmptyTitle>{filtered ? "No ideas match these filters" : "Nothing here yet"}</EmptyTitle>
            <EmptyDescription>
              {filtered ? "Try another search or clear the filters." : `Be the first to suggest something for ${config.appName}.`}
            </EmptyDescription>
          </EmptyHeader>
          {filtered && (
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => router.replace(pathname, { scroll: false })}>
              Clear filters
            </Button>
          )}
        </Empty>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 sm:gap-3">
          {items.map((r) => (
            <RequestCard key={r.id} request={r} onVote={onVote} fresh={fresh.includes(r.id)} />
          ))}
          <LoadMore hasMore={!!list.hasNextPage} loading={list.isFetchingNextPage} onVisible={() => list.fetchNextPage()} />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 bottom-5 z-20 flex justify-center px-4 sm:bottom-7">
        <div className="pointer-events-auto">
          <FloatingBar
            boards={config.boards}
            sort={filters.sort}
            onSortChange={(sort) => setFilters({ sort })}
            selectedBoards={filters.board}
            onSelectedBoardsChange={(board) => setFilters({ board })}
            selectedStatuses={filters.status}
            onSelectedStatusesChange={(status) => setFilters({ status })}
            onSuggest={() => setSuggestOpen(true)}
            onOpenFilters={() => setFiltersOpen(true)}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
      <FiltersSheet
        open={filtersOpen}
        onOpenChange={setFiltersOpen}
        boards={config.boards}
        selectedBoards={filters.board}
        onSelectedBoardsChange={(board) => setFilters({ board })}
        selectedStatuses={filters.status}
        onSelectedStatusesChange={(status) => setFilters({ status })}
      />
      <SuggestDialog open={suggestOpen} onOpenChange={setSuggestOpen} boards={config.boards} />
    </main>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write images, timeline and comments**

Images on a request that isn't public are protected files: get a file token first (`useFileToken`). The timeline merges status changes, merges, board moves and comments, newest first; replies stay under their comment, oldest first; internal notes use `InternalNote`. Public wording is "Luk from the team moved this to Reviewing"; the dashboard's is "Luk moved this to Reviewing" (`design/canvas/P-Request` and `D-Request`). `AlertDialogAction` doesn't close its dialog, so the delete confirmation closes itself once the delete is done.

Create `web/components/request/request-images.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { fileUrl } from "@/lib/files"
import { pbAdmin, type Client } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

/**
 * A file token for protected images, when the request isn't public. Tokens last a few minutes,
 * so it refreshes every 90 seconds while the page is open.
 */
export function useFileToken(client: Client, needed: boolean): string | undefined {
  const token = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["file-token", client === pbAdmin ? "team" : "board"],
    queryFn: () => client.files.getToken(),
    enabled: needed && client.authStore.isValid,
    staleTime: 90_000,
    refetchInterval: 90_000,
  })
  return token.data
}

export interface RequestImagesProps {
  collection: "requests" | "comments"
  recordId: string
  images: string[]
  /** A file token when the request isn't public (see useFileToken). */
  token?: string
  /** The request title, for alt text. */
  about: string
}

/** Up to three thumbnails; each opens the full image in a new tab. */
export function RequestImages({ collection, recordId, images, token, about }: RequestImagesProps) {
  if (images.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
      {images.map((name, i) => (
        <a
          key={name}
          href={fileUrl(collection, recordId, name, { token })}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noreferrer"
          className="block size-24 overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-surface-2"
        >
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- PocketBase serves the thumbnail */}
          <img
            src={fileUrl(collection, recordId, name, { thumb: "200x200", token })}
            alt={`Image ${i + 1} for ${about}`}
            className="size-full object-cover"
          />
        </a>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/request/timeline.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { IconArrowsExchange, IconGitMerge } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { InternalNote } from "@/components/lort/internal-note"
import { Composer, type ComposerValue } from "@/components/lort/composer"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import { StatusIcon } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { RequestImages } from "@/components/request/request-images"
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import type { Comment, Person, RequestDetail, TimelineEvent } from "@/lib/api"
import { STATUS_META, isStatus } from "@/lib/status"
import { relativeTime, shortDate } from "@/lib/time"
import { buildTimeline } from "@/lib/timeline"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface TimelineProps {
  request: RequestDetail
  /** The dashboard's wording ("Luk moved this…") instead of the public "Luk from the team moved this…". */
  team?: boolean
  /** File token for images on a request that isn't public. */
  token?: string
  /** Wraps an avatar, e.g. in the dashboard's person popover. */
  renderAvatar?: (person: Person, avatar: ReactNode) => ReactNode
  /** Signed in and not banned: shows Reply. */
  canReply: boolean
  onReply: (parentId: string, value: ComposerValue) => Promise<void>
  canDelete: (c: Comment) => boolean
  onDelete: (c: Comment) => Promise<void>
}

/** Status changes, merges and comments, newest first; replies sit under their comment. */
export function Timeline(p: TimelineProps) {
  const entries = buildTimeline(p.request)
  return (
    <section aria-label="Activity" className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-5">
      {entries.map((e) =>
        e.type === "event" ? (
          <EventRow key={e.event.id} event={e.event} request={p.request} team={p.team} />
        ) : (
          <CommentItem key={e.comment.id} comment={e.comment} {...p} />
        )
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

function Who({ person, team }: { person: Person; team?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <>
      <b className="font-bold text-foreground">{person.name || "Someone"}</b>
      {!team && person.team && " from the team"}
    </>
  )
}

function EventRow({ event: ev, request, team }: { event: TimelineEvent; request: RequestDetail; team?: boolean }) {
  const when = ev.kind === "created" ? shortDate(ev.created) : relativeTime(ev.created)
  let icon: ReactNode = <StatusIcon status="pending" keyline size={20} decorative />
  let text: ReactNode
  const refLink = (fallback: string) =>
    ev.ref ? (
      <Link href={team ? `/dashboard/requests/${ev.ref.id}` : `/feedback/${ev.ref.id}`} className="font-bold text-foreground underline-offset-4 hover:underline">
        {ev.ref.name}
      </Link>
    ) : (
      fallback
    )
  const roundIcon = (node: ReactNode) => (
    <span aria-hidden className="grid size-5 place-items-center rounded-full bg-surface-2 text-ink-2">
      {node}
    </span>
  )
  switch (ev.kind) {
    case "created":
      text = (
        <>
          <Who person={ev.actor} /> posted this in {request.board.name}
        </>
      )
      break
    case "status_changed":
      if (isStatus(ev.to)) icon = <StatusIcon status={ev.to} keyline size={20} decorative />
      text = (
        <>
          <Who person={ev.actor} team={team} /> moved this to{" "}
          <b className="font-bold text-foreground">{isStatus(ev.to) ? STATUS_META[ev.to].label : ev.to}</b>
        </>
      )
      break
    case "merged_in":
      icon = roundIcon(<IconGitMerge className="size-3" />)
      text = (
        <>
          <Who person={ev.actor} team={team} /> merged this into {refLink("another request")}
        </>
      )
      break
    case "merged_from":
      icon = roundIcon(<IconGitMerge className="size-3" />)
      text = (
        <>
          <Who person={ev.actor} team={team} /> merged {refLink("a duplicate")} into this
        </>
      )
      break
    case "board_changed":
      icon = roundIcon(<IconArrowsExchange className="size-3" />)
      text = (
        <>
          <Who person={ev.actor} team={team} /> moved this from {ev.from || "another board"} to {ev.to}
        </>
      )
      break
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pl-1 text-label font-normal text-muted-foreground">
      {icon}
      <span>
        {text} · {when}
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}

function CommentItem({ comment: c, ...p }: TimelineProps & { comment: Comment }) {
  const [replying, setReplying] = useState(false)
  if (c.internal) {
    return (
      <InternalNote
        author={c.author.name || "Someone"}
        time={relativeTime(c.created)}
        actions={p.canDelete(c) && <DeleteComment onDelete={() => p.onDelete(c)} />}
      >
        {c.body}
        <RequestImages collection="comments" recordId={c.id} images={c.images} token={p.token} about={p.request.title} />
      </InternalNote>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div className="px-1">
      <CommentBody comment={c} {...p} />
      <div className="mt-1 ml-[34px] flex gap-3">
        {p.canReply && (
          <Button variant="link" className="text-caption font-semibold text-muted-foreground" onClick={() => setReplying(true)}>
            Reply
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      {c.replies.map((r) => (
        <div key={r.id} className="mt-3 ml-[34px] rounded-md bg-card px-4 py-3.5">
          <CommentBody comment={r} {...p} />
        </div>
      ))}
      {replying && (
        <div className="mt-3 ml-[34px]">
          <Composer
            autoFocus
            label={`Reply to ${c.author.name || "this comment"}`}
            placeholder={`Reply to ${c.author.name || "this comment"}…`}
            onSubmit={async (v) => {
              await p.onReply(c.id, v)
              setReplying(false)
            }}
          />
          <Button variant="link" className="mt-1 text-caption text-muted-foreground" onClick={() => setReplying(false)}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function CommentBody({ comment: c, ...p }: TimelineProps & { comment: Comment }) {
  const avatar = <PersonAvatar person={c.author} size={26} />
  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-label font-normal">
        {p.renderAvatar ? p.renderAvatar(c.author, avatar) : avatar}
        <b className="font-bold">{c.author.name || "Someone"}</b>
        {c.author.team && <Badge>Team</Badge>}
        <span className="text-muted-foreground">· {relativeTime(c.created)}</span>
        {p.canDelete(c) && (
          <span className="ml-auto">
            <DeleteComment onDelete={() => p.onDelete(c)} />
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <p className={cn("mt-1.5 ml-[34px] text-body whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink-2")}>{c.body}</p>
      <div className="ml-[34px]">
        <RequestImages collection="comments" recordId={c.id} images={c.images} token={p.token} about={p.request.title} />
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

function DeleteComment({ onDelete }: { onDelete: () => Promise<void> }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  return (
    <AlertDialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <AlertDialogTrigger render={<Button variant="link" className="text-caption font-semibold text-muted-foreground" />}>
        Delete
      </AlertDialogTrigger>
      <AlertDialogContent>
        <AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogTitle>Delete this comment?</AlertDialogTitle>
          <AlertDialogDescription>It disappears for everyone, along with its replies.</AlertDialogDescription>
        </AlertDialogHeader>
        <AlertDialogFooter>
          <AlertDialogCancel>Keep it</AlertDialogCancel>
          {/* AlertDialogAction doesn't close the dialog by itself; close it once the delete is done. */}
          <AlertDialogAction
            variant="destructive"
            disabled={pending}
            onClick={async () => {
              setPending(true)
              try {
                await onDelete()
                setOpen(false)
              } finally {
                setPending(false)
              }
            }}
          >
            Delete
          </AlertDialogAction>
        </AlertDialogFooter>
      </AlertDialogContent>
    </AlertDialog>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/request/use-comments.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import type { ComposerValue } from "@/components/lort/composer"
import type { Comment } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pb, type Client } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

/**
 * Posting and deleting comments on one request. Posts always go through pb (the board user, or
 * an admin's team profile); `deleteWith` is pbAdmin in the dashboard, so the team can delete
 * anyone's comment. Both refresh the request page and the lists' comment counts.
 */
export function useComments(requestId: string, deleteWith: Client = pb) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const refresh = async () => {
    await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["request"] })
    await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })
  }
  return {
    post: async (v: ComposerValue, parent = "") => {
      // A plain object, so the SDK keeps `internal` a boolean in "@jsonPayload" when images are attached.
      await pb.collection("comments").create({
        request: requestId,
        parent,
        body: v.text,
        images: v.images,
        internal: v.visibility === "internal",
      })
      await refresh()
    },
    remove: async (c: Comment) => {
      try {
        await deleteWith.collection("comments").delete(c.id)
        toast("Deleted the comment.")
        await refresh()
      } catch (err) {
        toast(errorMessage(err))
      }
    },
  }
}
```

`RequestView` takes the team's extras as props instead of an `admin` flag sprinkled through it: the dashboard passes editable chips, the Edit/Delete menu and avatar popovers; the public page passes read-only chips. Closed and merged requests get a read-only sticker and, when merged, the "Merged into" banner. Next 16 route params are a promise.

Create `web/components/request/request-view.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { IconGitMerge } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import { VoteSticker } from "@/components/lort/vote-sticker"
import type { ComposerValue } from "@/components/lort/composer"
import { RequestImages } from "@/components/request/request-images"
import { Timeline } from "@/components/request/timeline"
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator"
import type { Comment, Person, RequestDetail } from "@/lib/api"
import { relativeTime } from "@/lib/time"

export interface RequestViewProps {
  request: RequestDetail
  /** The dashboard's view: no "Stick a dot" caption and no comment count heading, team wording in the timeline. */
  team?: boolean
  onVote: () => void
  /** The chips row: read-only on the public page, editable in the dashboard. */
  chips: ReactNode
  /** Next to the sticker, e.g. the dashboard's Edit/Delete menu. */
  titleAside?: ReactNode
  /** The composer, or the "sign in to comment" card. */
  composer: ReactNode
  /** File token for a request that isn't public (useFileToken). */
  token?: string
  renderAvatar?: (person: Person, avatar: ReactNode) => ReactNode
  canReply: boolean
  onReply: (parentId: string, value: ComposerValue) => Promise<void>
  canDelete: (c: Comment) => boolean
  onDelete: (c: Comment) => Promise<void>
}

/** One request: title, author, sticker, body, images, chips, composer and timeline. Shared by /feedback/[id] and the dashboard. */
export function RequestView(p: RequestViewProps) {
  const r = p.request
  const voteable = r.status !== "closed" && !r.mergedInto
  const author = <PersonAvatar person={r.author} size={32} keyline />
  return (
    <article>
      {r.mergedInto && (
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md bg-surface-2 px-4 py-3 text-label font-normal text-ink-2">
          <IconGitMerge className="size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
          <span>
            Merged into{" "}
            <Link
              href={p.team ? `/dashboard/requests/${r.mergedInto.id}` : `/feedback/${r.mergedInto.id}`}
              className="font-bold text-foreground underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
            >
              {r.mergedInto.name}
            </Link>
            . Its votes went there too.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="flex items-start gap-6">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h1 className="font-display text-[28px] leading-8 font-extrabold tracking-[-0.025em] text-balance sm:text-display-l">{r.title}</h1>
          <div className="mt-3.5 flex items-center gap-2.5 text-label font-normal">
            {p.renderAvatar ? p.renderAvatar(r.author, author) : author}
            <b className="font-bold">{r.author.name || "Someone"}</b>
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">· {relativeTime(r.created)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 pt-1">
          <VoteSticker count={r.votes} voted={r.voted} readOnly={!voteable} subject={r.title} onToggle={p.onVote} />
          {!p.team && voteable && <span className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground">Stick a dot</span>}
        </div>
        {p.titleAside}
      </div>

      {r.body && <p className="mt-5 max-w-[68ch] text-body whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink-2">{r.body}</p>}
      <RequestImages collection="requests" recordId={r.id} images={r.images} token={p.token} about={r.title} />

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">{p.chips}</div>

      <Separator className="mt-7 mb-6" />

      {!p.team && <h2 className="mb-3.5 font-display text-title">{r.comments === 1 ? "1 comment" : `${r.comments} comments`}</h2>}
      {p.composer}

      <Timeline
        request={r}
        team={p.team}
        token={p.token}
        renderAvatar={p.renderAvatar}
        canReply={p.canReply}
        onReply={p.onReply}
        canDelete={p.canDelete}
        onDelete={p.onDelete}
      />
    </article>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/board/public-request.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import Link from "next/link"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { ClientResponseError } from "pocketbase"
import { IconArrowLeft } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { useRequireUser } from "@/components/board/auth-gate"
import { useVote } from "@/components/board/use-vote"
import { Chip } from "@/components/lort/chip"
import { Composer } from "@/components/lort/composer"
import { StatusLabel } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { useFileToken } from "@/components/request/request-images"
import { RequestSkeleton } from "@/components/request/request-skeleton"
import { RequestView } from "@/components/request/request-view"
import { useComments } from "@/components/request/use-comments"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Empty, EmptyDescription, EmptyHeader, EmptyTitle } from "@/components/ui/empty"
import { getRequest } from "@/lib/api"
import { useUser } from "@/lib/auth"
import { pb } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"

/** /feedback/[id]: the shared request view without the team's controls. */
export function PublicRequest({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const config = useConfig()
  const user = useUser()
  const requireUser = useRequireUser()
  const onVote = useVote()
  const comments = useComments(id)
  const q = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.request("board", id), queryFn: () => getRequest(pb, id) })
  const token = useFileToken(pb, !!q.data && !q.data.public)

  const back = (
    <Link href="/feedback" className="mb-[22px] inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-label text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
      <IconArrowLeft className="size-4" aria-hidden />
      All ideas
    </Link>
  )

  if (q.isError) {
    const gone = q.error instanceof ClientResponseError && q.error.status === 404
    return (
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-[820px] px-4 pt-6 sm:px-8">
        {back}
        <Empty>
          <EmptyHeader>
            <EmptyTitle>{gone ? "That idea isn't here" : "This page didn't load"}</EmptyTitle>
            <EmptyDescription>
              {gone ? "It may have been removed, or it's waiting for the team to approve it." : "Check your connection, then try again."}
            </EmptyDescription>
          </EmptyHeader>
          {!gone && (
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => q.refetch()}>
              Try again
            </Button>
          )}
        </Empty>
      </main>
    )
  }

  const r = q.data
  const composer = user ? (
    <Composer
      placeholder={`Add a comment for ${r?.author.name || "them"} and the ${config.appName} team…`}
      onSubmit={(v) => comments.post(v)}
    />
  ) : (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-card px-5 py-4">
      <p className="flex-1 text-body text-ink-2">Sign in to join the conversation.</p>
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => requireUser()}>
        Sign in to comment
      </Button>
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[820px] px-4 pt-6 sm:px-8">
      {back}
      {!r ? (
        <RequestSkeleton composer={composer} />
      ) : (
        <RequestView
          request={r}
          onVote={() => onVote(r.id)}
          token={token}
          chips={
            <>
              <Chip>
                <StatusLabel status={r.status} size={18} keyline />
              </Chip>
              <Chip>{r.board.name}</Chip>
              {r.tags.map((t) => (
                <Chip key={t.id} className="text-ink-2">
                  {t.name}
                </Chip>
              ))}
            </>
          }
          composer={composer}
          canReply={!!user}
          onReply={(parent, v) => comments.post(v, parent)}
          canDelete={(c) => !!user && c.author.id === user.id}
          onDelete={comments.remove}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

From the known traps: a failed `/api/lort/team/me` ends the session only on 401 or 403.

Create `web/components/dashboard/session.test.ts`:

```ts
import { ClientResponseError } from "pocketbase"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { sessionAfterError } from "@/components/dashboard/session"

const err = (status: number, isAbort = false) => new ClientResponseError({ status, isAbort, response: {} })

describe("sessionAfterError", () => {
  it("signs out only when the server rejects the session", () => {
    expect(sessionAfterError(err(401))).toBe("sign-out")
    expect(sessionAfterError(err(403))).toBe("sign-out")
    expect(sessionAfterError(err(0, true))).toBe("keep") // the admin navigated away mid-fetch
    expect(sessionAfterError(err(0))).toBe("keep") // a network blip
    expect(sessionAfterError(err(500))).toBe("keep")
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the session**

Only superusers sign in (`_superusers`). On every dashboard load the admin's board profile is linked into `pb` (`linkProfile`), so their comments, votes and suggestions carry the Team badge. The shell renders straight away: the session lives in `localStorage`, which the server can't read, so branching on it during render would break hydration; pages show their skeletons until data arrives, and the guard redirects from an effect.

Create `web/components/dashboard/session.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useEffect } from "react"
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { teamMe } from "@/lib/api"
import { isAuthError } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pb, pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import type { RecordModel } from "pocketbase"

/**
 * What a failed /api/lort/team/me means for the admin's session. Only a 401 or 403 (the server
 * no longer accepts the token) ends it; an aborted request (status 0, e.g. the admin navigated
 * away mid-fetch) or a network blip keeps it. Pure, for tests.
 */
export function sessionAfterError(err: unknown): "sign-out" | "keep" {
  return isAuthError(err) ? "sign-out" : "keep"
}

/** Signs the admin out of the dashboard and of their board profile. */
export function signOutAdmin() {
  pbAdmin.authStore.clear()
  pb.authStore.clear()
}

/** Links the admin's board profile into pb: from now on they comment, vote and suggest as it. */
export async function linkProfile() {
  const me = await teamMe(pbAdmin)
  pb.authStore.save(me.token, me.profile as RecordModel)
}

/**
 * The dashboard's guard. Without a superuser session it sends the admin to the sign-in page.
 * With one, it renews the board-profile token once per load, signing out only on 401/403.
 * The shell renders straight away (the session lives in localStorage, which the server can't
 * see, so branching on it during render would break hydration); pages show their skeletons
 * until their data arrives.
 */
export function useAdminSession() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const qc = useQueryClient()

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pbAdmin.authStore.isValid) {
      router.replace(`/dashboard/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(pathname)}`)
      return
    }
    let cancelled = false
    linkProfile()
      .then(() => qc.invalidateQueries())
      .catch((err) => {
        if (sessionAfterError(err) === "sign-out") {
          signOutAdmin()
          if (!cancelled) router.replace(`/dashboard/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(pathname)}`)
        }
      })
    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
    // Once per dashboard load, not per page.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [])
}
```

Create `web/app/dashboard/login/page.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { Suspense, useState } from "react"
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { AuthCard } from "@/components/dashboard/auth-card"
import { linkProfile, signOutAdmin } from "@/components/dashboard/session"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Field, FieldError, FieldGroup, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

/** Superusers only. Board accounts can't sign in here. */
export default function LoginPage() {
  const config = useConfig()
  return (
    <AuthCard title={`${config.appName} dashboard`}>
      <Suspense>
        <LoginForm />
      </Suspense>
    </AuthCard>
  )
}

function LoginForm() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const next = useSearchParams().get("next")
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("")
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("")
  const [error, setError] = useState("")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)

  const submit = async () => {
    if (!email.trim() || !password) return setError("Enter your email and password.")
    setPending(true)
    setError("")
    try {
      await pbAdmin.collection("_superusers").authWithPassword(email.trim(), password)
      await linkProfile()
      await qc.invalidateQueries()
      router.replace(next?.startsWith("/dashboard") ? next : "/dashboard/requests")
    } catch (err) {
      signOutAdmin()
      setError(errorMessage(err, "That email and password don't match a team account."))
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      className="flex flex-col gap-[18px]"
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        void submit()
      }}
    >
      <FieldGroup className="gap-[18px]">
        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="login-email">Email</FieldLabel>
          <Input id="login-email" type="email" autoComplete="username" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
        </Field>
        <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="login-password">Password</FieldLabel>
          <Input
            id="login-password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="current-password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
        </Field>
      </FieldGroup>
      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={pending} aria-busy={pending}>
        Sign in
      </Button>
      <p className="mt-0.5 text-center text-caption leading-[19px] font-normal text-muted-foreground">
        Forgot it? On the server, run
        <br />
        <code className="rounded-[6px] bg-surface-2 px-[7px] py-0.5 font-mono text-[12px] text-foreground">./setup.sh --reset-admin</code>
      </p>
    </form>
  )
}
```

Create `web/app/dashboard/invite/page.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { Suspense, useState } from "react"
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { getTokenPayload } from "pocketbase"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { AuthCard } from "@/components/dashboard/auth-card"
import { linkProfile } from "@/components/dashboard/session"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Field, FieldError, FieldGroup, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"

/** Where an invite link lands: the new admin picks a password and is signed straight in. */
export default function InvitePage() {
  const config = useConfig()
  return (
    <AuthCard title={`Join the ${config.appName} team`}>
      <Suspense>
        <InviteForm />
      </Suspense>
    </AuthCard>
  )
}

function InviteForm() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const token = useSearchParams().get("token") ?? ""
  // The invite is a superuser password-reset token; its payload names the email.
  const email = String(getTokenPayload(token).email ?? "")
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("")
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState("")
  const [error, setError] = useState("")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)

  const submit = async () => {
    if (password.length < 8) return setError("Use at least 8 characters.")
    if (password !== confirm) return setError("The two passwords don't match.")
    setPending(true)
    setError("")
    try {
      await pbAdmin.collection("_superusers").confirmPasswordReset(token, password, confirm)
      await pbAdmin.collection("_superusers").authWithPassword(email, password)
      await linkProfile()
      await qc.invalidateQueries()
      router.replace("/dashboard/requests")
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err, "That invite has expired. Ask for a new one."))
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  if (!token || !email) {
    return <p className="text-center text-body text-ink-2">This invite link is incomplete. Ask whoever sent it for a new one.</p>
  }

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      className="flex flex-col gap-[18px]"
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        void submit()
      }}
    >
      <p className="text-center text-label font-normal text-ink-2">
        You&apos;re joining as <b className="font-bold text-foreground">{email}</b>.
      </p>
      <FieldGroup className="gap-[18px]">
        <Field>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="invite-password">Choose a password</FieldLabel>
          <Input id="invite-password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        </Field>
        <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
          <FieldLabel htmlFor="invite-confirm">Type it again</FieldLabel>
          <Input id="invite-confirm" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" value={confirm} onChange={(e) => setConfirm(e.target.value)} />
          <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
        </Field>
      </FieldGroup>
      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={pending} aria-busy={pending}>
        Join the team
      </Button>
    </form>
  )
}
```

```tsx
"use client"

import Link from "next/link"
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { IconAdjustments, IconInbox, IconLayoutSidebarLeftCollapse } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { signOutAdmin } from "@/components/dashboard/session"
import { LogoMark } from "@/components/lort/logo-mark"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"
import {
  Sidebar,
  SidebarContent,
  SidebarFooter,
  SidebarHeader,
  SidebarMenu,
  SidebarMenuBadge,
  SidebarMenuButton,
  SidebarMenuItem,
  useSidebar,
} from "@/components/ui/sidebar"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { stats } from "@/lib/api"
import { useUser } from "@/lib/auth"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"

// Roadmap and Changelog join this list in Phase 2, Docs in Phase 6.
const TOP = [{ href: "/dashboard/requests", label: "Requests", icon: IconInbox }]
const BOTTOM = [{ href: "/dashboard/settings", label: "Settings", icon: IconAdjustments }]

/** The dashboard sidebar: 244px, collapsing to a 76px rail (remembered in a cookie); a sheet on phones. */
export function AppSidebar() {
  const config = useConfig()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()
  const profile = useUser()
  const { toggleSidebar } = useSidebar()
  const counts = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.stats, queryFn: () => stats(pbAdmin) })

  const item = (i: (typeof TOP)[number], badge?: number) => (
    <SidebarMenuItem key={i.href}>
      <SidebarMenuButton isActive={pathname.startsWith(i.href)} tooltip={i.label} render={<Link href={i.href} />}>
        <i.icon />
        <span>{i.label}</span>
      </SidebarMenuButton>
      {!!badge && <SidebarMenuBadge>{badge} new</SidebarMenuBadge>}
    </SidebarMenuItem>
  )

  return (
    <Sidebar collapsible="icon" className="border-r-surface-2">
      <SidebarHeader className="flex-row items-center gap-2.5 px-5 pt-5 pb-[18px] group-data-[collapsible=icon]:flex-col group-data-[collapsible=icon]:px-2">
        <LogoMark appName={config.appName} logo={config.logo} size={30} />
        <span className="flex-1 truncate font-display text-[17px] font-extrabold group-data-[collapsible=icon]:hidden">
          {config.appName}
        </span>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label="Collapse sidebar" onClick={toggleSidebar} className="max-md:hidden">
          <IconLayoutSidebarLeftCollapse className="size-[18px] group-data-[collapsible=icon]:rotate-180" />
        </Button>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent className="px-3">
        <SidebarMenu className="gap-1">{TOP.map((i) => item(i, counts.data?.new))}</SidebarMenu>
        <div className="flex-1" />
        <SidebarMenu className="gap-1">{BOTTOM.map((i) => item(i))}</SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarContent>
      <SidebarFooter className="px-5 pt-3 pb-4 group-data-[collapsible=icon]:px-2">
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger
            render={
              <button
                type="button"
                className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-full text-left group-data-[collapsible=icon]:justify-center"
                aria-label="Your account"
              />
            }
          >
            <PersonAvatar person={{ id: profile?.id ?? "", name: profile?.name ?? "", avatar: profile?.avatar, team: true }} size={28} />
            <span className="flex-1 truncate text-caption font-semibold group-data-[collapsible=icon]:hidden">{profile?.name}</span>
            <span className="text-micro font-normal text-muted-foreground group-data-[collapsible=icon]:hidden">Lort {config.version}</span>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent side="top" align="start" className="min-w-52">
            <DropdownMenuItem render={<Link href="/feedback" />}>View the board</DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem
              onClick={() => {
                signOutAdmin()
                router.replace("/dashboard/login")
              }}
            >
              Sign out
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </SidebarFooter>
    </Sidebar>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/dashboard-shell.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { AppSidebar } from "@/components/dashboard/app-sidebar"
import { useAdminSession } from "@/components/dashboard/session"
import { LogoMark } from "@/components/lort/logo-mark"
import { SidebarInset, SidebarProvider, SidebarTrigger } from "@/components/ui/sidebar"

/** Guards the dashboard (superusers only) and lays out the sidebar and the page. */
export function DashboardShell({ defaultOpen, children }: { defaultOpen: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  useAdminSession()
  const config = useConfig()
  return (
    <SidebarProvider defaultOpen={defaultOpen}>
      <AppSidebar />
      <SidebarInset className="min-w-0 bg-background">
        {/* Phones: a top bar with the menu, which opens the sidebar as a sheet. */}
        <div className="flex h-14 items-center gap-2.5 border-b px-4 md:hidden">
          <SidebarTrigger aria-label="Open the menu" />
          <LogoMark appName={config.appName} logo={config.logo} size={26} />
          <span className="font-display text-base font-extrabold">{config.appName}</span>
        </div>
        {children}
      </SidebarInset>
    </SidebarProvider>
  )
}
```

Create `web/app/dashboard/(app)/layout.tsx`:

```tsx
import { cookies } from "next/headers"
import { DashboardShell } from "@/components/dashboard/dashboard-shell"

// The sidebar remembers whether it was collapsed in shadcn's sidebar_state cookie; reading it
// here renders the right width on the first paint.
export default async function DashboardLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const open = (await cookies()).get("sidebar_state")?.value !== "false"
  return <DashboardShell defaultOpen={open}>{children}</DashboardShell>
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the table and its skeleton**

Layout follows `design/canvas/D-Requests.dc.html` and `S-Requests.dc.html`. The status column is icon-only, so it has a tooltip and the icon's accessible name (the Status Label Rule). Unseen rows have the `primary-soft` tint. Each row's link carries the table's filters to the request page, which uses them for Skip and "3 of 12 in Reviewing". `table-fixed` keeps the title column from collapsing.

Create `web/components/dashboard/use-boards.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"

export interface Board {
  id: string
  name: string
  order: number
  isPublic: boolean
  requireApproval: boolean
}

export interface Tag {
  id: string
  name: string
  order: number
}

/** Every board, private ones too, in order (the team's view). */
export function useBoards() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.boards,
    queryFn: () => pbAdmin.collection("boards").getFullList<Board>({ sort: "order,created" }),
  })
}

/** Every tag, in order. */
export function useTags() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.tags,
    queryFn: () => pbAdmin.collection("tags").getFullList<Tag>({ sort: "order,created" }),
  })
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/requests-table.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import Link from "next/link"
import { IconArrowUp } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import { StatusIcon } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table"
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tooltip"
import type { RequestItem } from "@/lib/api"
import { STATUS_META } from "@/lib/status"
import { shortDate } from "@/lib/time"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface RequestsTableProps {
  items: RequestItem[]
  /** The table's filters as a query string, carried to the request page for Skip and "3 of 12". */
  search: string
  /** Ids that just arrived over realtime. */
  fresh: string[]
}

/** The dashboard's requests: status icon, title (New, Hidden), votes, tags, date, requested by. Rows open the request. */
export function RequestsTable({ items, search, fresh }: RequestsTableProps) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
      <Table className="table-fixed">
        <TableHeader>
          <TableRow>
            <TableHead className="w-[46px]">
              <span className="sr-only">Status</span>
            </TableHead>
            <TableHead>Request</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="w-[78px] text-right">Votes</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="w-[214px] max-lg:hidden">Tags</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="w-[78px] max-sm:hidden">Date</TableHead>
            <TableHead className="w-[62px]">
              <span className="sr-only">Requested by</span>
            </TableHead>
          </TableRow>
        </TableHeader>
        <TableBody>
          {items.map((r) => (
            <TableRow
              key={r.id}
              className={cn(
                "relative",
                r.new && "bg-primary-soft [--si-ground:var(--primary-soft)] hover:bg-primary-soft",
                fresh.includes(r.id) && "animate-fresh"
              )}
            >
              <TableCell>
                <Tooltip>
                  <TooltipTrigger render={<span className="relative z-10 inline-flex" />}>
                    <StatusIcon status={r.status} size={18} />
                  </TooltipTrigger>
                  <TooltipContent>{STATUS_META[r.status].label}</TooltipContent>
                </Tooltip>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="max-w-0">
                <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center">
                  <Link
                    href={`/dashboard/requests/${r.id}${search}`}
                    className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold outline-none after:absolute after:inset-0 focus-visible:after:outline-3 focus-visible:after:-outline-offset-3 focus-visible:after:outline-focus"
                  >
                    {r.title}
                  </Link>
                  {r.new && <Badge className="ml-2">New</Badge>}
                  {!r.visible && (
                    <Badge variant="secondary" className="ml-2">
                      Hidden
                    </Badge>
                  )}
                </div>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="text-right">
                <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-end gap-1 text-label font-bold tabular-nums">
                  <IconArrowUp className="size-3.5" stroke={2.6} aria-hidden />
                  {r.votes}
                </span>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="max-lg:hidden">
                <div className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-hidden">
                  {r.tags.slice(0, 2).map((t) => (
                    <span key={t.id} className="rounded-full bg-surface-2 px-[9px] py-0.5 text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink-2">
                      {t.name}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                  {r.tags.length > 2 && <span className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground">+{r.tags.length - 2}</span>}
                </div>
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell className="text-caption font-normal text-muted-foreground tabular-nums max-sm:hidden">
                {shortDate(r.created)}
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell>
                <PersonAvatar person={r.author} size={26} />
              </TableCell>
            </TableRow>
          ))}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>
    </div>
  )
}
```
