Filters (search, status, board, tag, visibility, sort) live in the URL. Realtime and infinite scroll as on the board.

Create `web/components/dashboard/requests-page.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
import { useInfiniteQuery, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { IconChevronDown, IconPlus, IconRotateClockwise, IconSearch } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { LoadMore } from "@/components/board/load-more"
import { SuggestDialog } from "@/components/board/suggest-dialog"
import { RequestsTableSkeleton } from "@/components/dashboard/requests-skeleton"
import { RequestsTable } from "@/components/dashboard/requests-table"
import { useBoards } from "@/components/dashboard/use-boards"
import { Bone } from "@/components/lort/bone"
import { FilterMenu } from "@/components/lort/filter-menu"
import { STATUS_OPTIONS } from "@/components/lort/floating-bar"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuRadioGroup,
  DropdownMenuRadioItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"
import { Empty, EmptyDescription, EmptyHeader, EmptyTitle } from "@/components/ui/empty"
import { InputGroup, InputGroupAddon, InputGroupInput } from "@/components/ui/input-group"
import { listRequests, stats } from "@/lib/api"
import { apiParams, parseTableFilters, tableSearch, type TableFilters, type TableSort } from "@/lib/filters"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { useLiveRefresh } from "@/lib/realtime"
import { OPEN_STATUSES, type Status } from "@/lib/status"
import { useCooldown } from "@/lib/use-cooldown"

const SORTS: { value: TableSort; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "newest", label: "Newest first" },
  { value: "oldest", label: "Oldest first" },
  { value: "top", label: "Most upvoted" },
  { value: "updated", label: "Recently updated" },
]

const openSummary = (v: string[]) =>
  v.length === OPEN_STATUSES.length && OPEN_STATUSES.every((s) => v.includes(s)) ? "Open" : undefined

/** /dashboard/requests: counts, filters (all in the URL), the table, realtime and a rate-limited refresh. */
export function RequestsPage() {
  const config = useConfig()
  const router = useRouter()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const filters = parseTableFilters(useSearchParams())
  const params = apiParams(filters)
  const search = tableSearch(filters)
  const boards = useBoards()
  const [newOpen, setNewOpen] = useState(false)
  const [fresh, setFresh] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [q, setQ] = useState(filters.q)
  const [ready, startCooldown] = useCooldown(10_000)

  const setFilters = (next: Partial<TableFilters>) =>
    router.replace(`${pathname}${tableSearch({ ...filters, ...next })}`, { scroll: false })
  // Search: the URL follows the field 300 ms after the last key.
  const urlQ = filters.q
  useEffect(() => {
    if (q === urlQ) return
    const t = setTimeout(() => router.replace(`${pathname}${tableSearch({ ...parseTableFilters(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search)), q })}`, { scroll: false }), 300)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [q, urlQ, pathname, router])

  const counts = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.stats, queryFn: () => stats(pbAdmin) })
  const list = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: keys.requests("team", params),
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => listRequests(pbAdmin, params, pageParam),
    initialPageParam: 1,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => (last.page * last.perPage < last.totalItems ? last.page + 1 : undefined),
  })

  const onCreate = (id: string) => {
    setFresh((f) => [...f, id])
    setTimeout(() => setFresh((f) => f.filter((x) => x !== id)), 4000)
  }
  const { live } = useLiveRefresh(pbAdmin, "requests", [["requests", "team"], keys.stats], onCreate)

  const items = list.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? []
  const total = list.data?.pages[0]?.totalItems ?? 0
  const visibility = filters.visibility ? [filters.visibility] : []

  return (
    <main className="px-4 pt-6 pb-14 sm:px-10 sm:pt-[30px]">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1060px]">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <h1 className="font-display text-[30px] font-extrabold tracking-[-0.025em]">Requests</h1>
          {counts.data ? (
            <span className="mt-1.5 text-label font-normal text-muted-foreground">
              {counts.data.total} total · {counts.data.waiting} waiting for you
            </span>
          ) : (
            <Bone w={190} h={14} className="mt-1.5" />
          )}
          <span className="flex-1" />
          {live && <span className="text-caption font-normal text-muted-foreground">Live</span>}
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            aria-label="Refresh"
            disabled={!ready}
            onClick={() => {
              startCooldown()
              void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests", "team"] })
              void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats })
            }}
          >
            <IconRotateClockwise />
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={() => setNewOpen(true)}>
            <IconPlus stroke={2.4} />
            New request
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-[22px] mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <InputGroup className="h-[42px] w-full sm:w-[280px]">
            <InputGroupAddon>
              <IconSearch className="size-4" />
            </InputGroupAddon>
            <InputGroupInput
              type="search"
              placeholder="Search titles and details"
              aria-label="Search requests"
              value={q}
              onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            />
          </InputGroup>
          <FilterMenu
            allLabel="Every status"
            noun="statuses"
            options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
            selected={filters.status}
            onSelectedChange={(v) => setFilters({ status: v as Status[] })}
            summarize={openSummary}
          />
          <FilterMenu
            allLabel="All boards"
            noun="boards"
            options={(boards.data ?? []).map((b) => ({ value: b.id, label: b.isPublic ? b.name : `${b.name} (private)` }))}
            selected={filters.board}
            onSelectedChange={(board) => setFilters({ board })}
          />
          {config.tags.length > 0 && (
            <FilterMenu
              allLabel="Any tag"
              noun="tags"
              options={config.tags.map((t) => ({ value: t.id, label: t.name }))}
              selected={filters.tag}
              onSelectedChange={(tag) => setFilters({ tag })}
            />
          )}
          <FilterMenu
            allLabel="Public and hidden"
            noun="kinds"
            options={[
              { value: "public", label: "On the board" },
              { value: "hidden", label: "Hidden" },
            ]}
            selected={visibility}
            onSelectedChange={(v) => setFilters({ visibility: v.length === 1 ? (v[0] as "public" | "hidden") : "" })}
          />
          <span className="flex-1" />
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<Button variant="secondary" />}>
              {SORTS.find((s) => s.value === filters.sort)?.label}
              <IconChevronDown className="size-[15px]" />
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-48">
              <DropdownMenuRadioGroup value={filters.sort} onValueChange={(v) => setFilters({ sort: v as TableSort })}>
                {SORTS.map((s) => (
                  <DropdownMenuRadioItem key={s.value} value={s.value}>
                    {s.label}
                  </DropdownMenuRadioItem>
                ))}
              </DropdownMenuRadioGroup>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        </div>

        {list.isPending ? (
          <RequestsTableSkeleton />
        ) : items.length === 0 ? (
          <Empty className="rounded-lg border bg-card">
            <EmptyHeader>
              <EmptyTitle>{search ? "Nothing matches these filters" : "No requests yet"}</EmptyTitle>
              <EmptyDescription>
                {search ? "Try another search or clear the filters." : "When people suggest something, it lands here first."}
              </EmptyDescription>
            </EmptyHeader>
            {search && (
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => router.replace(pathname, { scroll: false })}>
                Clear filters
              </Button>
            )}
          </Empty>
        ) : (
          <>
            <RequestsTable items={items} search={search} fresh={fresh} />
            <LoadMore hasMore={!!list.hasNextPage} loading={list.isFetchingNextPage} onVisible={() => list.fetchNextPage()} />
            <p className="mx-1 mt-3.5 text-caption font-normal text-muted-foreground">
              Showing {items.length} of {total}
              {list.hasNextPage ? ". Scroll for more." : "."}
            </p>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      <SuggestDialog open={newOpen} onOpenChange={setNewOpen} team boards={boards.data ?? []} />
    </main>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write triage**

Toasts from spec 4.2: moves onto or off the roadmap are named ("Added to the roadmap", "Taken off the roadmap"); Complete shows the 60-second Undo toast ("Marked as completed"; the number of people notified joins in Phase 3, when email exists). Undo calls `/undo` and toasts where it went back to. Every action refreshes the request, the lists, the queue and the counts.

Create `web/components/dashboard/use-triage.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { undoToast } from "@/components/lort/undo-toast"
import { setStatus, undoComplete, type StatusChange } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { STATUS_META, type TriageActionId } from "@/lib/status"

/** Seconds a completion can be undone; matches the server's UndoWindow. */
export const UNDO_SECONDS = 60

/** The toast for a status change: roadmap moves are named, a completion offers Undo. Pure, for tests. */
export function changeMessage(ch: StatusChange): string | null {
  if (ch.to === "completed") return "Marked as completed"
  if (ch.roadmap === "added") return "Added to the roadmap"
  if (ch.roadmap === "removed") return "Taken off the roadmap"
  return null
}

/** Applies status actions from the triage bar and the status chip, with the spec's toasts. */
export function useTriage(requestId: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const config = useConfig()
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<TriageActionId | null>(null)

  const refresh = async () => {
    await Promise.all([
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["request"] }),
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] }),
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["queue"] }),
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats }),
    ])
  }

  const undo = async () => {
    try {
      const back = await undoComplete(pbAdmin, requestId)
      toast(`Moved back to ${STATUS_META[back.to].label}`)
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
    await refresh()
  }

  const apply = async (action: TriageActionId) => {
    setBusy(action)
    try {
      const ch = await setStatus(pbAdmin, requestId, action)
      await refresh()
      const message = changeMessage(ch)
      if (ch.to === "completed") {
        // The shipped emails (Phase 3) wait out the same window; the count joins the message then.
        undoToast({ message: config.features.email ? `${message}. People will hear about it in a minute.` : message!, seconds: UNDO_SECONDS, onUndo: undo })
      } else if (message) {
        toast(message)
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    } finally {
      setBusy(null)
    }
  }

  return { apply, busy }
}
```


- [ ] **Step 3: Write the chips, the menu, the person popover and merge**

The status chip offers the same moves as the triage bar (minus Merge and Skip), so the server's transition rules are the only rules. The board chip writes a `board_changed` timeline entry (Task 2). Tags toggle from a Command list. "On the board" is a shadcn Switch inside a chip. The avatar popover has Copy password reset link (works without email) and Ban/Unban (admins can't be banned). Merge searches by title, then confirms.

Create `web/components/dashboard/admin-chips.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { IconCheck, IconPlus, IconWorld } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useConfig } from "@/app/providers"
import { useBoards } from "@/components/dashboard/use-boards"
import { ChipButton, chipClass } from "@/components/lort/chip"
import { StatusIcon, StatusLabel } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { Command, CommandEmpty, CommandGroup, CommandInput, CommandItem, CommandList } from "@/components/ui/command"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuRadioGroup,
  DropdownMenuRadioItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover"
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch"
import { moveBoard, type RequestDetail } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { STATUS_META, statusMoves, type TriageActionId } from "@/lib/status"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface AdminChipsProps {
  request: RequestDetail
  /** Status moves go through the same handler as the triage bar. */
  onStatusAction: (action: TriageActionId) => void
}

/** The dashboard's editable chips: status, board, tags, add tag, and "On the board". */
export function AdminChips({ request: r, onStatusAction }: AdminChipsProps) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const config = useConfig()
  const boards = useBoards()

  const refresh = () =>
    Promise.all([qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["request"] }), qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })])

  const update = async (data: Record<string, unknown>, done: string) => {
    try {
      await pbAdmin.collection("requests").update(r.id, data)
      await refresh()
      toast(done)
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
  }

  const tagIds = r.tags.map((t) => t.id)
  const toggleTag = (id: string) =>
    update({ tags: tagIds.includes(id) ? tagIds.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...tagIds, id] }, "Updated the tags.")

  return (
    <>
      <DropdownMenu>
        <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<ChipButton aria-label={`Status: ${STATUS_META[r.status].label}`} />}>
          <StatusLabel status={r.status} size={18} keyline />
        </DropdownMenuTrigger>
        <DropdownMenuContent className="min-w-52">
          {statusMoves(r.status).map((a) => (
            <DropdownMenuItem key={a.id} onClick={() => onStatusAction(a.id)}>
              <StatusIcon status={a.next!} decorative />
              {a.label}
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          ))}
        </DropdownMenuContent>
      </DropdownMenu>

      <DropdownMenu>
        <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<ChipButton aria-label={`Board: ${r.board.name}`} />}>{r.board.name}</DropdownMenuTrigger>
        <DropdownMenuContent className="min-w-52">
          <DropdownMenuRadioGroup
            value={r.board.id}
            onValueChange={async (id: string) => {
              try {
                await moveBoard(pbAdmin, r.id, id)
                await refresh()
                toast("Moved to another board.")
              } catch (err) {
                toast(errorMessage(err))
              }
            }}
          >
            {(boards.data ?? []).map((b) => (
              <DropdownMenuRadioItem key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                {b.isPublic ? b.name : `${b.name} (private)`}
              </DropdownMenuRadioItem>
            ))}
          </DropdownMenuRadioGroup>
        </DropdownMenuContent>
      </DropdownMenu>

      {r.tags.map((t) => (
        <ChipButton key={t.id} className="text-ink-2" aria-label={`Remove tag ${t.name}`} onClick={() => toggleTag(t.id)}>
          {t.name}
        </ChipButton>
      ))}

      {config.tags.length > 0 && (
        <Popover>
          <PopoverTrigger render={<ChipButton className="w-[34px] justify-center px-0" aria-label="Add tag" />}>
            <IconPlus className="size-4" />
          </PopoverTrigger>
          <PopoverContent align="start" className="w-60 p-1.5">
            <Command>
              <CommandInput placeholder="Find a tag" />
              <CommandList>
                <CommandEmpty>No tag by that name.</CommandEmpty>
                <CommandGroup>
                  {config.tags.map((t) => (
                    <CommandItem key={t.id} value={t.name} onSelect={() => toggleTag(t.id)}>
                      {t.name}
                      <IconCheck className={cn("ml-auto size-4", !tagIds.includes(t.id) && "invisible")} />
                    </CommandItem>
                  ))}
                </CommandGroup>
              </CommandList>
            </Command>
          </PopoverContent>
        </Popover>
      )}

      <label className={cn(chipClass, "cursor-pointer gap-2 hover:bg-surface-3")}>
        <IconWorld className="size-4" aria-hidden />
        On the board
        <Switch
          checked={r.visible}
          onCheckedChange={(on) => update({ visible: on }, on ? "It's on the board now." : "Hidden from the board.")}
        />
      </label>
    </>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/request-menu.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { IconDots } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { DropdownMenu, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuItem, DropdownMenuTrigger } from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"
import { Field, FieldError, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import type { RequestDetail } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"

/** The request page's overflow menu: Edit (title, body) and Delete. */
export function RequestMenu({ request: r }: { request: RequestDetail }) {
  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = useState(false)
  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = useState(false)
  return (
    <>
      <DropdownMenu>
        <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="More actions" className="mt-2" />}>
          <IconDots />
        </DropdownMenuTrigger>
        <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-40">
          <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setEditOpen(true)}>Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
          <DropdownMenuItem variant="destructive" onClick={() => setDeleteOpen(true)}>
            Delete
          </DropdownMenuItem>
        </DropdownMenuContent>
      </DropdownMenu>
      <Dialog open={editOpen} onOpenChange={setEditOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[560px]">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Edit the request</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <EditForm request={r} onDone={() => setEditOpen(false)} />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
      <DeleteDialog request={r} open={deleteOpen} onOpenChange={setDeleteOpen} />
    </>
  )
}

function EditForm({ request: r, onDone }: { request: RequestDetail; onDone: () => void }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(r.title)
  const [body, setBody] = useState(r.body)
  const [error, setError] = useState("")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const save = async () => {
    if (title.trim().length < 3) return setError("Give it a title of at least 3 characters.")
    setPending(true)
    try {
      await pbAdmin.collection("requests").update(r.id, { title: title.trim(), body })
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["request"] })
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })
      toast("Saved your changes.")
      onDone()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(errorMessage(err))
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }
  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      className="flex flex-col gap-[18px]"
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        void save()
      }}
    >
      <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor="edit-title">Title</FieldLabel>
        <Input id="edit-title" value={title} maxLength={140} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} />
        <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
      </Field>
      <Field>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor="edit-body">Details</FieldLabel>
        <Textarea id="edit-body" value={body} rows={6} maxLength={10000} onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)} />
      </Field>
      <DialogFooter>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onDone}>
          Cancel
        </Button>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={pending} aria-busy={pending}>
          Save
        </Button>
      </DialogFooter>
    </form>
  )
}

function DeleteDialog({ request: r, open, onOpenChange }: { request: RequestDetail; open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  return (
    <AlertDialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <AlertDialogContent>
        <AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogTitle>Delete “{r.title}”?</AlertDialogTitle>
          <AlertDialogDescription>Its votes, comments and timeline go with it. This can&apos;t be undone.</AlertDialogDescription>
        </AlertDialogHeader>
        <AlertDialogFooter>
          <AlertDialogCancel>Keep it</AlertDialogCancel>
          <AlertDialogAction
            variant="destructive"
            disabled={pending}
            onClick={async () => {
              setPending(true)
              try {
                await pbAdmin.collection("requests").delete(r.id)
                await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })
                await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats })
                toast("Deleted the request.")
                router.replace("/dashboard/requests")
              } catch (err) {
                toast(errorMessage(err))
                setPending(false)
              }
            }}
          >
            Delete
          </AlertDialogAction>
        </AlertDialogFooter>
      </AlertDialogContent>
    </AlertDialog>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/person-popover.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover"
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator"
import { resetLink, setBanned, userSummary, type Person } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"

const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"]

/**
 * Wraps a person's avatar in the dashboard: a popover with their email, join date, request and
 * vote counts and verification, plus Copy password reset link (works without email) and Ban.
 */
export function PersonPopover({ person, children }: { person: Person; children: ReactNode }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const summary = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.user(person.id),
    queryFn: () => userSummary(pbAdmin, person.id),
    enabled: open && !!person.id,
  })
  if (!person.id) return <>{children}</>
  const s = summary.data
  const joined = s ? new Date(s.joined) : null

  return (
    <Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <PopoverTrigger
        render={<button type="button" className="rounded-full" aria-label={`${person.name || "Someone"}, open member menu`} />}
      >
        {children}
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent align="start" className="w-[270px]">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-1 pb-2.5">
          <PersonAvatar person={person} size={36} />
          <div className="min-w-0 text-[12px] text-muted-foreground">
            <div className="text-label font-bold text-foreground">{person.name || "Someone"}</div>
            {s ? (
              <>
                <div className="truncate">
                  {s.email} · joined {MONTHS[joined!.getMonth()]} {joined!.getFullYear()}
                </div>
                <div>
                  {s.requests} {s.requests === 1 ? "request" : "requests"} · {s.votes} {s.votes === 1 ? "vote" : "votes"} ·{" "}
                  {s.verified ? "verified email" : "email not verified"}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div>Loading…</div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        <Separator />
        <div className="flex flex-col pt-1.5">
          <Button
            variant="neutral"
            className="justify-start rounded-xl px-2.5"
            onClick={async () => {
              try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(await resetLink(pbAdmin, person.id))
                toast("Copied the reset link. Send it to them; it works for 30 minutes.")
              } catch (err) {
                toast(errorMessage(err))
              }
            }}
          >
            Copy password reset link
          </Button>
          {s && !s.team && (
            <Button
              variant="destructive"
              className="justify-start rounded-xl px-2.5"
              onClick={async () => {
                try {
                  await setBanned(pbAdmin, person.id, !s.banned)
                  await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.user(person.id) })
                  toast(s.banned ? `Unbanned ${person.name}.` : `Banned ${person.name}. They can read but not post, vote or comment.`)
                } catch (err) {
                  toast(errorMessage(err))
                }
              }}
            >
              {s.banned ? `Unban ${person.name}` : `Ban ${person.name}`}
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/merge-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { StatusIcon } from "@/components/lort/status-icon"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Command, CommandEmpty, CommandInput, CommandItem, CommandList } from "@/components/ui/command"
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { listRequests, merge, type RequestDetail, type RequestItem } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { useDebounced } from "@/lib/use-debounced"

/**
 * Merge: pick the request this one duplicates (search by title), then confirm. The source closes,
 * points at the target, and its voters' dots move there.
 */
export function MergeDialog({ request: r, open, onOpenChange }: { request: RequestDetail; open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void }) {
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[560px]">
        <MergeForm request={r} onDone={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function MergeForm({ request: r, onDone }: { request: RequestDetail; onDone: () => void }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [q, setQ] = useState("")
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<RequestItem | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const search = useDebounced(q.trim(), 250)
  const params = { q: search, sort: "top", status: "pending,reviewing,planned,progress,completed" }
  const results = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.requests("team", { ...params, merge: r.id }),
    queryFn: () => listRequests(pbAdmin, params),
  })
  const options = (results.data?.items ?? []).filter((x) => x.id !== r.id)

  if (target) {
    return (
      <>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Merge into “{target.title}”?</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            “{r.title}” closes and points there. Everyone who voted on it gets a dot on “{target.title}”.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setTarget(null)}>
            Pick another
          </Button>
          <Button
            disabled={pending}
            aria-busy={pending}
            onClick={async () => {
              setPending(true)
              try {
                await merge(pbAdmin, r.id, target.id)
                await Promise.all([
                  qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["request"] }),
                  qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] }),
                  qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats }),
                ])
                toast(`Merged into “${target.title}”.`)
                onDone()
                router.push(`/dashboard/requests/${target.id}`)
              } catch (err) {
                toast(errorMessage(err))
                setPending(false)
              }
            }}
          >
            Merge
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </>
    )
  }

  return (
    <>
      <DialogHeader>
        <DialogTitle>Merge into…</DialogTitle>
        <DialogDescription>Find the request this one duplicates.</DialogDescription>
      </DialogHeader>
      <Command shouldFilter={false}>
        <CommandInput placeholder="Search by title" value={q} onValueChange={setQ} />
        <CommandList className="mt-2 max-h-72">
          <CommandEmpty>{results.isPending ? "Searching…" : "Nothing matches."}</CommandEmpty>
          {options.map((o) => (
            <CommandItem key={o.id} value={o.id} onSelect={() => setTarget(o)}>
              <StatusIcon status={o.status} />
              <span className="truncate">{o.title}</span>
              <span className="ml-auto text-caption text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{o.votes}</span>
            </CommandItem>
          ))}
        </CommandList>
      </Command>
    </>
  )
}
```

Layout follows `design/canvas/D-Request.dc.html` and `S-Request.dc.html`: breadcrumb and queue position, the shared view, the composer with Public/Internal, and the TriageBar floating at the bottom centre of the content area (its left edge follows the sidebar's width; while loading it's a bone, because its actions depend on the status). Opening a request clears its "New" marker once. Skip opens the next request in the table's filtered list.

Create `web/components/dashboard/team-request.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"
import Link from "next/link"
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { ClientResponseError } from "pocketbase"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useVote } from "@/components/board/use-vote"
import { AdminChips } from "@/components/dashboard/admin-chips"
import { MergeDialog } from "@/components/dashboard/merge-dialog"
import { PersonPopover } from "@/components/dashboard/person-popover"
import { RequestMenu } from "@/components/dashboard/request-menu"
import { useTriage } from "@/components/dashboard/use-triage"
import { Bone } from "@/components/lort/bone"
import { Composer } from "@/components/lort/composer"
import { TriageBar } from "@/components/lort/triage-bar"
import { useFileToken } from "@/components/request/request-images"
import { RequestSkeleton } from "@/components/request/request-skeleton"
import { RequestView } from "@/components/request/request-view"
import { useComments } from "@/components/request/use-comments"
import { Empty, EmptyDescription, EmptyHeader, EmptyTitle } from "@/components/ui/empty"
import { useSidebar } from "@/components/ui/sidebar"
import { getRequest, markSeen, queue } from "@/lib/api"
import { apiParams, parseTableFilters, tableSearch } from "@/lib/filters"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { OPEN_STATUSES, STATUS_META } from "@/lib/status"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** "3 of 12 in Reviewing", "3 of 40 open", or "3 of 12" for any other filter. Pure, for tests. */
export function queueLabel(position: number, total: number, statuses: string[]): string {
  if (!position) return "Not in this list any more"
  const base = `${position} of ${total}`
  if (statuses.length === 1 && statuses[0] in STATUS_META) return `${base} in ${STATUS_META[statuses[0] as keyof typeof STATUS_META].label}`
  if (statuses.length === OPEN_STATUSES.length && OPEN_STATUSES.every((s) => statuses.includes(s))) return `${base} open`
  return base
}

/** /dashboard/requests/[id]: the shared request view with the team's controls and the triage bar. */
export function TeamRequest({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const { state } = useSidebar()
  const filters = parseTableFilters(useSearchParams())
  const params = apiParams(filters)
  const search = tableSearch(filters)
  const onVote = useVote()
  const comments = useComments(id, pbAdmin)
  const triage = useTriage(id)
  const [mergeOpen, setMergeOpen] = useState(false)

  const detail = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.request("team", id), queryFn: () => getRequest(pbAdmin, id) })
  const position = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.queue(params, id), queryFn: () => queue(pbAdmin, params, id) })
  const token = useFileToken(pbAdmin, !!detail.data && !detail.data.public)

  // Opening a request clears its "New" marker, once per visit.
  const seen = useRef("")
  const r = detail.data
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!r || !r.new || seen.current === r.id) return
    seen.current = r.id
    markSeen(pbAdmin, r.id)
      .then(() => Promise.all([qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.stats }), qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests", "team"] })]))
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [r, qc])

  const skip = () => {
    const next = position.data?.next
    if (next) router.push(`/dashboard/requests/${next}${search}`)
    else toast("That's the end of this list.")
  }

  if (detail.isError) {
    const gone = detail.error instanceof ClientResponseError && detail.error.status === 404
    return (
      <main className="px-4 pt-6 sm:px-10 sm:pt-[30px]">
        <Empty className="mx-auto max-w-[820px]">
          <EmptyHeader>
            <EmptyTitle>{gone ? "That request is gone" : "This page didn't load"}</EmptyTitle>
            <EmptyDescription>
              <Link href={`/dashboard/requests${search}`}>Back to the requests</Link>
            </EmptyDescription>
          </EmptyHeader>
        </Empty>
      </main>
    )
  }

  const composer = (
    <Composer
      canUseInternal
      label="Comment"
      placeholder={r ? `Reply to ${r.author.name || "them"}, or leave a note for the team…` : "Write a reply or a note for the team…"}
      onSubmit={(v) => comments.post(v)}
    />
  )

  return (
    <main className="px-4 pt-6 pb-[170px] sm:px-10 sm:pt-[30px]">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[820px]">
        <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="mb-[22px] flex items-center gap-2 text-label font-normal text-muted-foreground">
          <Link href={`/dashboard/requests${search}`} className="hover:text-foreground">
            Requests
          </Link>
          <span aria-hidden>/</span>
          {r ? <span>{r.board.name}</span> : <Bone w={48} h={12} />}
          <span className="flex-1" />
          {position.data ? (
            <span className="text-caption font-normal">{queueLabel(position.data.position, position.data.total, filters.status)}</span>
          ) : (
            <Bone w={120} h={12} />
          )}
        </nav>
        {!r ? (
          <RequestSkeleton composer={composer} />
        ) : (
          <RequestView
            request={r}
            team
            onVote={() => onVote(r.id)}
            token={token}
            titleAside={<RequestMenu request={r} />}
            chips={<AdminChips request={r} onStatusAction={triage.apply} />}
            renderAvatar={(person, avatar) => <PersonPopover person={person}>{avatar}</PersonPopover>}
            composer={composer}
            canReply
            onReply={(parent, v) => comments.post(v, parent)}
            canDelete={() => true}
            onDelete={comments.remove}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* The triage bar floats at the bottom centre of the content area; a bone while its status is unknown. */}
      <div
        className={cn(
          "pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 bottom-7 z-20 flex justify-center px-4 md:left-(--sidebar-width)",
          state === "collapsed" && "md:left-(--sidebar-width-icon)"
        )}
      >
        <div className="pointer-events-auto">
          {r ? (
            <TriageBar
              status={r.status}
              busy={triage.busy}
              onAction={(a) => {
                if (a.id === "merge") setMergeOpen(true)
                else if (a.id === "skip") skip()
                else void triage.apply(a.id)
              }}
            />
          ) : (
            <Bone w={470} h={56} className="max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] inset-shadow-sm" />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      {r && <MergeDialog request={r} open={mergeOpen} onOpenChange={setMergeOpen} />}
    </main>
  )
}
```

After a save, `router.refresh()` re-renders the server layout, which reads the new brand from `X-Lort-Config` (the Go cache drops it on every settings, board or tag change, Task 3). ```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** One Settings section: a card with a title and an optional lede. */
export function SettingsSection({ id, title, lede, children }: { id: string; title: string; lede?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section id={id} aria-labelledby={`${id}-title`} className="scroll-mt-6 rounded-lg border bg-card px-6 py-[22px]">
      <h2 id={`${id}-title`} className="font-display text-title">
        {title}
      </h2>
      {lede && <p className="mt-1 mb-[18px] max-w-[62ch] text-label font-normal text-muted-foreground">{lede}</p>}
      {children}
    </section>
  )
}

/** A setting: what it is and what it does on the left, the control on the right. */
export function SettingRow({
  title,
  description,
  children,
  className,
  htmlFor,
}: {
  title: string
  description?: string
  children: ReactNode
  className?: string
  /** The control's id, so the title labels it. */
  htmlFor?: string
}) {
  const Title = htmlFor ? "label" : "b"
  return (
    <div className={cn("flex items-center gap-4 border-t py-3.5 first-of-type:border-t-0", className)}>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <Title htmlFor={htmlFor} className="block text-[15px] font-semibold">
          {title}
        </Title>
        {description && <span className="mt-0.5 block text-caption font-normal text-muted-foreground">{description}</span>}
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

Create `web/components/dashboard/settings/use-settings.ts`:

```ts
"use client"

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation"
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"

export interface Settings {
  id: string
  appName: string
  logo: string
  primaryColor: string
  showLandingCard: boolean
  roadmapPublic: boolean
}

/** The settings record and a save that refreshes everything showing the brand. */
export function useSettings() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const router = useRouter()
  const settings = useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.settings,
    queryFn: () => pbAdmin.collection("settings").getFirstListItem<Settings>(""),
  })

  /** Saves on change with a toast. router.refresh() re-renders the server layout, which reads the new brand from X-Lort-Config. */
  const save = async (data: Partial<Settings> | { logo: File | null }, done = "Saved.") => {
    if (!settings.data) return
    try {
      await pbAdmin.collection("settings").update(settings.data.id, data)
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.settings })
      router.refresh()
      toast(done)
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
  }

  return { settings, save }
}

/** After a board or tag change: the dashboard's lists and the brand header (public boards, tags) both refresh. */
export function useCatalogRefresh() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const router = useRouter()
  return async () => {
    await Promise.all([qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.boards }), qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.tags })])
    router.refresh()
  }
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/settings/branding-section.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useRef, useState } from "react"
import { SettingRow, SettingsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/section"
import type { Settings } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/use-settings"
import { LogoMark } from "@/components/lort/logo-mark"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch"
import { Toggle } from "@/components/ui/toggle"
import { ToggleGroup } from "@/components/ui/toggle-group"
import { fileUrl } from "@/lib/files"

/** The 8 presets from the spec (14.2). */
export const PRESETS = [
  { name: "Pink", hex: "#cc3a6d" },
  { name: "Coral", hex: "#d4553a" },
  { name: "Amber", hex: "#b7791f" },
  { name: "Leaf", hex: "#2f855a" },
  { name: "Teal", hex: "#0f8a8a" },
  { name: "Ocean", hex: "#2563eb" },
  { name: "Grape", hex: "#7c4dcc" },
  { name: "Graphite", hex: "#4b5563" },
]

const HEX = /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i

export function BrandingSection({ settings: s, save }: { settings: Settings; save: (d: object, done?: string) => Promise<void> }) {
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const [name, setName] = useState(s.appName)
  const [hex, setHex] = useState(s.primaryColor)
  const [hexError, setHexError] = useState("")
  const logo = s.logo ? fileUrl("settings", s.id, s.logo) : ""
  const color = s.primaryColor.toLowerCase()

  return (
    <SettingsSection id="branding" title="Branding" lede="How your board looks to your users. Lort stays in the background.">
      <SettingRow title="App logo" description="Square PNG, SVG or WebP, at least 128 px.">
        <LogoMark appName={s.appName} logo={logo} size={56} className="rounded-[14px]" />
        <input
          ref={fileRef}
          type="file"
          accept="image/png,image/svg+xml,image/webp"
          hidden
          onChange={(e) => {
            const f = e.target.files?.[0]
            if (f) void save({ logo: f }, "Saved the new logo.")
            e.target.value = ""
          }}
        />
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}>
          {s.logo ? "Replace" : "Upload"}
        </Button>
        {s.logo && (
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => void save({ logo: null }, "Removed the logo.")}>
            Remove
          </Button>
        )}
      </SettingRow>
      <SettingRow title="App name" description="Shown in the top bar, emails and the browser tab." htmlFor="app-name">
        <Input
          id="app-name"
          className="h-10 w-[260px] max-w-full"
          value={name}
          maxLength={60}
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          onBlur={() => {
            if (name.trim() && name.trim() !== s.appName) void save({ appName: name.trim() }, "Saved the app name.")
          }}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && e.currentTarget.blur()}
        />
      </SettingRow>
      <SettingRow
        className="items-start max-sm:flex-col"
        title="Primary colour"
        description="Buttons, voted stickers and everything that's on. Lort adjusts custom colours so text on them stays readable in light and dark."
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-3 max-sm:items-start">
          <ToggleGroup
            aria-label="Primary colour presets"
            value={PRESETS.some((p) => p.hex === color) ? [color] : []}
            onValueChange={(v) => {
              if (!v[0]) return
              setHex(v[0])
              void save({ primaryColor: v[0] }, "Saved the colour.")
            }}
            className="flex-wrap gap-2.5"
          >
            {PRESETS.map((p) => (
              <Toggle
                key={p.hex}
                value={p.hex}
                aria-label={p.name}
                style={{ backgroundColor: p.hex }}
                className="size-[30px] rounded-full p-0 hover:opacity-90 data-pressed:shadow-[0_0_0_3px_var(--card),0_0_0_5px_var(--foreground)]"
              />
            ))}
          </ToggleGroup>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-caption font-normal text-muted-foreground">
            Custom
            <Input
              aria-label="Custom hex colour"
              aria-invalid={!!hexError}
              className="h-10 w-[120px] font-mono text-caption"
              value={hex}
              onChange={(e) => setHex(e.target.value)}
              onBlur={() => {
                const v = hex.trim()
                if (!HEX.test(v)) return setHexError("Use a hex colour like #2563eb.")
                setHexError("")
                if (v.toLowerCase() !== color) void save({ primaryColor: v.toLowerCase() }, "Saved the colour.")
              }}
            />
          </label>
          {hexError && (
            <p role="alert" className="text-caption font-medium text-danger">
              {hexError}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </SettingRow>
      <SettingRow
        title="Show “Get your own board” card"
        description="A small card on public pages that links to Lort. “Powered by Lort” in the footer stays either way."
        htmlFor="landing-card"
      >
        <Switch
          id="landing-card"
          checked={s.showLandingCard}
          onCheckedChange={(on) => void save({ showLandingCard: on }, on ? "The card is back." : "Hid the card.")}
        />
      </SettingRow>
    </SettingsSection>
  )
}
```


- [ ] **Step 3: Write Boards and Tags**

Reordering uses dnd-kit with its keyboard sensor (focus the grip, Space to lift, arrows to move, Space to drop) and saves through `POST /api/lort/order/{boards|tags}`. Dragged rows and chips keep an opaque `bg-card` and a `shadow-card-float`, so text never overlaps what's underneath. Deleting a board asks where its requests go (`DELETE /api/lort/boards/{id}?moveTo=`); the last board can't be deleted. Deleting a tag removes it from every request (Task 2's test covers PocketBase doing that).

Create `web/components/dashboard/settings/boards-section.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import {
  closestCenter,
  DndContext,
  KeyboardSensor,
  PointerSensor,
  useSensor,
  useSensors,
  type DragEndEvent,
} from "@dnd-kit/core"
import { arrayMove, SortableContext, sortableKeyboardCoordinates, useSortable, verticalListSortingStrategy } from "@dnd-kit/sortable"
import { CSS } from "@dnd-kit/utilities"
import { IconGripVertical, IconPlus, IconX } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { SettingsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/section"
import { useCatalogRefresh } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/use-settings"
import { useBoards, type Board } from "@/components/dashboard/use-boards"
import { Bone } from "@/components/lort/bone"
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Field, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select"
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch"
import { deleteBoard, setOrder } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const GRID = "grid grid-cols-[32px_minmax(0,1fr)_88px_120px_36px] items-center gap-3"

/** Boards: drag to reorder (the top one is the default), Public and Needs approval switches, rename, add, delete. */
export function BoardsSection() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const boards = useBoards()
  const refresh = useCatalogRefresh()
  const [newId, setNewId] = useState("")
  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(PointerSensor, { activationConstraint: { distance: 4 } }),
    useSensor(KeyboardSensor, { coordinateGetter: sortableKeyboardCoordinates })
  )
  const list = boards.data ?? []

  const onDragEnd = async ({ active, over }: DragEndEvent) => {
    if (!over || active.id === over.id) return
    const next = arrayMove(
      list,
      list.findIndex((b) => b.id === active.id),
      list.findIndex((b) => b.id === over.id)
    )
    qc.setQueryData(keys.boards, next) // show the new order straight away
    try {
      await setOrder(pbAdmin, "boards", next.map((b) => b.id))
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
    await refresh()
  }

  const add = async () => {
    try {
      const b = await pbAdmin
        .collection("boards")
        .create<Board>({ name: "New board", order: list.length, isPublic: true, requireApproval: false })
      setNewId(b.id)
      await refresh()
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
  }

  return (
    <SettingsSection
      id="boards"
      title="Boards"
      lede="Drag to reorder. The top board is the default for new suggestions. Private boards are only visible to your team."
    >
      <div className={cn(GRID, "pb-1.5 text-[12px] font-bold text-muted-foreground")}>
        <span />
        <span>Board</span>
        <span>Public</span>
        <span>Needs approval</span>
        <span />
      </div>
      {boards.isPending ? (
        [0, 1].map((i) => (
          <div key={i} className={cn(GRID, "border-t py-2.5")}>
            <Bone w={16} h={16} />
            <Bone w="40%" h={14} />
            <Bone w={30} h={18} />
            <Bone w={30} h={18} />
            <span />
          </div>
        ))
      ) : (
        <DndContext sensors={sensors} collisionDetection={closestCenter} onDragEnd={onDragEnd}>
          <SortableContext items={list.map((b) => b.id)} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>
            {list.map((b, i) => (
              <BoardRow key={b.id} board={b} isDefault={i === 0} others={list.filter((o) => o.id !== b.id)} autoFocus={b.id === newId} />
            ))}
          </SortableContext>
        </DndContext>
      )}
      <div className="border-t pt-3">
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={add}>
          <IconPlus />
          Add board
        </Button>
      </div>
    </SettingsSection>
  )
}

function BoardRow({ board: b, isDefault, others, autoFocus }: { board: Board; isDefault: boolean; others: Board[]; autoFocus: boolean }) {
  const refresh = useCatalogRefresh()
  const { attributes, listeners, setNodeRef, transform, transition, isDragging } = useSortable({ id: b.id })
  const [name, setName] = useState(b.name)

  const update = async (data: Partial<Board>, done: string) => {
    try {
      await pbAdmin.collection("boards").update(b.id, data)
      await refresh()
      toast(done)
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
  }

  return (
    <div
      ref={setNodeRef}
      style={{ transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform), transition }}
      className={cn(GRID, "relative border-t bg-card py-2.5", isDragging && "z-10 rounded-md shadow-card-float")}
    >
      <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" className="cursor-grab touch-none" aria-label={`Reorder ${b.name}`} {...attributes} {...listeners}>
        <IconGripVertical />
      </Button>
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
        <Input
          aria-label={`Name of ${b.name}`}
          autoFocus={autoFocus}
          value={name}
          maxLength={60}
          className="h-9 border-transparent bg-transparent px-2.5 font-semibold hover:border-border focus-visible:border-border"
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          onBlur={() => {
            if (name.trim() && name.trim() !== b.name) void update({ name: name.trim() }, "Renamed the board.")
            else setName(b.name)
          }}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && e.currentTarget.blur()}
        />
        {isDefault && <Badge variant="secondary">Default</Badge>}
      </div>
      <Switch
        aria-label={`${b.name} is public`}
        checked={b.isPublic}
        onCheckedChange={(on) => void update({ isPublic: on }, on ? `${b.name} is public now.` : `${b.name} is private now.`)}
      />
      <Switch
        aria-label={`${b.name} needs approval`}
        checked={b.requireApproval}
        onCheckedChange={(on) =>
          void update({ requireApproval: on }, on ? "New ideas there wait for your approval." : "New ideas there go straight on the board.")
        }
      />
      <DeleteBoard board={b} others={others} />
    </div>
  )
}

function DeleteBoard({ board: b, others }: { board: Board; others: Board[] }) {
  const refresh = useCatalogRefresh()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [moveTo, setMoveTo] = useState(others[0]?.id ?? "")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label={`Delete ${b.name}`} disabled={others.length === 0} onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        <IconX />
      </Button>
      <AlertDialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Delete {b.name}?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>Any requests on it move to the board you pick, with a note on their timeline.</AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <Field>
            <FieldLabel>Move its requests to</FieldLabel>
            <Select value={moveTo} onValueChange={(v) => setMoveTo(String(v))} items={others.map((o) => ({ value: o.id, label: o.name }))}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-full">
                <SelectValue />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                {others.map((o) => (
                  <SelectItem key={o.id} value={o.id}>
                    {o.name}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </Field>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Keep it</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              variant="destructive"
              disabled={pending}
              onClick={async () => {
                setPending(true)
                try {
                  await deleteBoard(pbAdmin, b.id, moveTo)
                  await refresh()
                  await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })
                  toast(`Deleted ${b.name}.`)
                  setOpen(false)
                } catch (err) {
                  toast(errorMessage(err))
                } finally {
                  setPending(false)
                }
              }}
            >
              Delete board
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/settings/tags-section.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { closestCenter, DndContext, KeyboardSensor, PointerSensor, useSensor, useSensors, type DragEndEvent } from "@dnd-kit/core"
import { arrayMove, rectSortingStrategy, SortableContext, sortableKeyboardCoordinates, useSortable } from "@dnd-kit/sortable"
import { CSS } from "@dnd-kit/utilities"
import { IconGripVertical, IconPlus } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { SettingsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/section"
import { useCatalogRefresh } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/use-settings"
import { useTags, type Tag } from "@/components/dashboard/use-boards"
import { Bone } from "@/components/lort/bone"
import { chipClass } from "@/components/lort/chip"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover"
import { setOrder } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** Tags: add, rename, reorder (drag the grip, or focus it and use the arrow keys), delete. */
export function TagsSection() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const tags = useTags()
  const refresh = useCatalogRefresh()
  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(PointerSensor, { activationConstraint: { distance: 4 } }),
    useSensor(KeyboardSensor, { coordinateGetter: sortableKeyboardCoordinates })
  )
  const list = tags.data ?? []

  const onDragEnd = async ({ active, over }: DragEndEvent) => {
    if (!over || active.id === over.id) return
    const next = arrayMove(
      list,
      list.findIndex((t) => t.id === active.id),
      list.findIndex((t) => t.id === over.id)
    )
    qc.setQueryData(keys.tags, next)
    try {
      await setOrder(pbAdmin, "tags", next.map((t) => t.id))
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
    await refresh()
  }

  return (
    <SettingsSection id="tags" title="Tags" lede="Labels you can put on requests. People can pick from these when they suggest something.">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {tags.isPending ? (
          [64, 48, 72].map((w) => <Bone key={w} w={w} h={34} />)
        ) : (
          <DndContext sensors={sensors} collisionDetection={closestCenter} onDragEnd={onDragEnd}>
            <SortableContext items={list.map((t) => t.id)} strategy={rectSortingStrategy}>
              {list.map((t) => (
                <TagChip key={t.id} tag={t} />
              ))}
            </SortableContext>
          </DndContext>
        )}
        <AddTag count={list.length} />
      </div>
    </SettingsSection>
  )
}

function TagChip({ tag: t }: { tag: Tag }) {
  const refresh = useCatalogRefresh()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const { attributes, listeners, setNodeRef, transform, transition, isDragging } = useSortable({ id: t.id })
  const [name, setName] = useState(t.name)

  return (
    <div
      ref={setNodeRef}
      style={{ transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform), transition }}
      className={cn(chipClass, "gap-0.5 pr-0 pl-1 text-ink-2", isDragging && "z-10 shadow-card-float")}
    >
      <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-xs" className="cursor-grab touch-none" aria-label={`Reorder ${t.name}`} {...attributes} {...listeners}>
        <IconGripVertical />
      </Button>
      <Popover>
        <PopoverTrigger render={<button type="button" className="h-full rounded-full pr-3.5 pl-1 hover:text-foreground" />}>{t.name}</PopoverTrigger>
        <PopoverContent align="start" className="w-64">
          <form
            className="flex flex-col gap-3"
            onSubmit={async (e) => {
              e.preventDefault()
              if (!name.trim() || name.trim() === t.name) return
              try {
                await pbAdmin.collection("tags").update(t.id, { name: name.trim() })
                await refresh()
                toast("Renamed the tag.")
              } catch (err) {
                toast(errorMessage(err))
              }
            }}
          >
            <Input aria-label="Tag name" className="h-10" value={name} maxLength={40} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="destructive"
                size="sm"
                onClick={async () => {
                  try {
                    await pbAdmin.collection("tags").delete(t.id)
                    await refresh()
                    await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["requests"] })
                    toast(`Deleted “${t.name}” and took it off every request.`)
                  } catch (err) {
                    toast(errorMessage(err))
                  }
                }}
              >
                Delete
              </Button>
              <span className="flex-1" />
              <Button type="submit" size="sm">
                Save
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </PopoverContent>
      </Popover>
    </div>
  )
}

function AddTag({ count }: { count: number }) {
  const refresh = useCatalogRefresh()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [name, setName] = useState("")
  return (
    <Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <PopoverTrigger render={<button type="button" className={cn(chipClass, "w-[34px] justify-center px-0 hover:bg-surface-3")} aria-label="Add a tag" />}>
        <IconPlus className="size-4" />
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent align="start" className="w-64">
        <form
          className="flex gap-2"
          onSubmit={async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault()
            if (!name.trim()) return
            try {
              await pbAdmin.collection("tags").create({ name: name.trim(), order: count })
              await refresh()
              setName("")
              setOpen(false)
              toast("Added the tag.")
            } catch (err) {
              toast(errorMessage(err))
            }
          }}
        >
          <Input aria-label="New tag" placeholder="ios" className="h-10" value={name} maxLength={40} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          <Button type="submit" size="sm" className="h-10">
            Add
          </Button>
        </form>
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  )
}
```


- [ ] **Step 4: Write Team and the page**

Invite creates the superuser and shows a 7-day link to copy (emailed as well once email is on, in Phase 3). "Copy invite link" makes a fresh one for someone who lost theirs. The first admin reads "set up Lort"; the others "invited 2 Oct".

Create `web/components/dashboard/settings/team-section.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useState } from "react"
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { IconPlus } from "@tabler/icons-react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { SettingsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/section"
import { Bone } from "@/components/lort/bone"
import { PersonAvatar } from "@/components/lort/person-avatar"
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { Field, FieldError, FieldLabel } from "@/components/ui/field"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { invite, removeAdmin, teamList, type TeamMember } from "@/lib/api"
import { errorMessage } from "@/lib/errors"
import { pbAdmin } from "@/lib/pocketbase"
import { keys } from "@/lib/query"
import { shortDate } from "@/lib/time"

/** Team: the admins, "Invite an admin" (a 7-day link to copy; emailed too once email is on) and Remove. */
export function TeamSection() {
  const team = useQuery({ queryKey: keys.team, queryFn: () => teamList(pbAdmin) })
  const [inviteOpen, setInviteOpen] = useState(false)
  const members = team.data?.items ?? []
  return (
    <SettingsSection id="team" title="Team" lede="Admins can sign in to this dashboard. Their comments show a Team badge.">
      {team.isPending
        ? [0, 1].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-4 border-t py-3.5 first:border-t-0">
              <Bone w={34} h={34} />
              <Bone w="40%" h={14} />
            </div>
          ))
        : members.map((m, i) => <MemberRow key={m.id} member={m} you={m.id === team.data?.you} founder={i === 0} />)}
      <div className="border-t pt-3">
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setInviteOpen(true)}>
          <IconPlus />
          Invite an admin
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Dialog open={inviteOpen} onOpenChange={setInviteOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[460px]">
          <InviteForm onDone={() => setInviteOpen(false)} />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </SettingsSection>
  )
}

function MemberRow({ member: m, you, founder }: { member: TeamMember; you: boolean; founder: boolean }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const name = m.name || m.email.split("@")[0]
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-t py-3.5 first:border-t-0">
      <PersonAvatar person={{ id: "", name, team: true }} size={34} />
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <b className="block text-[15px] font-semibold">
          {name}
          {you && (
            <Badge variant="secondary" className="ml-1.5">
              You
            </Badge>
          )}
        </b>
        <span className="block truncate text-caption font-normal text-muted-foreground">
          {m.email} · {founder ? "set up Lort" : `invited ${shortDate(m.created)}`}
        </span>
      </div>
      {!you && (
        <>
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            onClick={async () => {
              try {
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(await invite(pbAdmin, m.email))
                toast("Copied a fresh invite link. It works for 7 days.")
              } catch (err) {
                toast(errorMessage(err))
              }
            }}
          >
            Copy invite link
          </Button>
          <AlertDialog>
            <AlertDialogTrigger render={<Button variant="destructive" size="sm" />}>Remove</AlertDialogTrigger>
            <AlertDialogContent>
              <AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogTitle>Remove {name} from the team?</AlertDialogTitle>
                <AlertDialogDescription>They can&apos;t sign in to the dashboard any more. Their comments stay.</AlertDialogDescription>
              </AlertDialogHeader>
              <AlertDialogFooter>
                <AlertDialogCancel>Keep them</AlertDialogCancel>
                <AlertDialogAction
                  variant="destructive"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    try {
                      await removeAdmin(pbAdmin, m.id)
                      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.team })
                      toast(`Removed ${name}.`)
                    } catch (err) {
                      toast(errorMessage(err))
                    }
                  }}
                >
                  Remove
                </AlertDialogAction>
              </AlertDialogFooter>
            </AlertDialogContent>
          </AlertDialog>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function InviteForm({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("")
  const [link, setLink] = useState("")
  const [error, setError] = useState("")
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)

  if (link) {
    return (
      <>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Send them this link</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>It works for 7 days. They pick a password and land in the dashboard.</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <Input readOnly aria-label="Invite link" value={link} className="h-10 font-mono text-caption" onFocus={(e) => e.currentTarget.select()} />
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onDone}>
            Done
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={async () => {
              await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link)
              toast("Copied the invite link.")
            }}
          >
            Copy link
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </>
    )
  }

  return (
    <form
      noValidate
      className="flex flex-col gap-5"
      onSubmit={async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())) return setError("Enter an email address like name@example.com.")
        setPending(true)
        setError("")
        try {
          setLink(await invite(pbAdmin, email.trim()))
          await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: keys.team })
        } catch (err) {
          setError(errorMessage(err))
        } finally {
          setPending(false)
        }
      }}
    >
      <DialogHeader>
        <DialogTitle>Invite an admin</DialogTitle>
        <DialogDescription>They&apos;ll be able to triage, reply and change settings.</DialogDescription>
      </DialogHeader>
      <Field data-invalid={!!error}>
        <FieldLabel htmlFor="invite-email">Their email</FieldLabel>
        <Input id="invite-email" type="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
        <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
      </Field>
      <DialogFooter>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onDone}>
          Cancel
        </Button>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={pending} aria-busy={pending}>
          Create invite link
        </Button>
      </DialogFooter>
    </form>
  )
}
```

Create `web/components/dashboard/settings/settings-page.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { BoardsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/boards-section"
import { BrandingSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/branding-section"
import { SettingsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/section"
import { TagsSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/tags-section"
import { TeamSection } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/team-section"
import { useSettings } from "@/components/dashboard/settings/use-settings"
import { Bone } from "@/components/lort/bone"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// Services (Email, Voice, Spam protection), Connect (Widget, MCP, Webhooks), Server (Backups,
// Updates) and the Roadmap switch join this list in later phases.
const TOC = [
  {
    group: "Your board",
    items: [
      { id: "branding", label: "Branding" },
      { id: "boards", label: "Boards" },
      { id: "tags", label: "Tags" },
      { id: "team", label: "Team" },
    ],
  },
]

/** /dashboard/settings: one page, an in-page table of contents, each section saving on change. */
export function SettingsPage() {
  const { settings, save } = useSettings()
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState("branding")

  // Highlight the section nearest the top of the viewport.
  useEffect(() => {
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        const top = entries.filter((e) => e.isIntersecting).sort((a, b) => a.boundingClientRect.top - b.boundingClientRect.top)[0]
        if (top) setCurrent(top.target.id)
      },
      { rootMargin: "-10% 0px -70% 0px" }
    )
    TOC.flatMap((g) => g.items).forEach((i) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(i.id)
      if (el) io.observe(el)
    })
    return () => io.disconnect()
  }, [])

  return (
    <main className="px-4 pt-6 pb-20 sm:px-10 sm:pt-[30px]">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-[1040px] items-start gap-8 md:grid-cols-[190px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <nav aria-label="Settings sections" className="md:sticky md:top-6">
          <h1 className="mb-3.5 ml-3 font-display text-[30px] font-extrabold tracking-[-0.025em]">Settings</h1>
          {TOC.map((g) => (
            <div key={g.group} className="max-md:hidden">
              <div className="px-3 pt-3.5 pb-1 text-[12px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">{g.group}</div>
              {g.items.map((i) => (
                <a
                  key={i.id}
                  href={`#${i.id}`}
                  aria-current={current === i.id ? "location" : undefined}
                  className={cn(
                    "block rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-label text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground",
                    current === i.id && "bg-surface-2 text-foreground"
                  )}
                >
                  {i.label}
                </a>
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          {settings.data ? (
            <BrandingSection settings={settings.data} save={save} />
          ) : (
            <SettingsSection id="branding" title="Branding" lede="How your board looks to your users. Lort stays in the background.">
              <div aria-busy="true" className="flex flex-col gap-5">
                {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2">
                      <Bone w={120} h={14} />
                      <Bone w="60%" h={12} />
                    </div>
                    <Bone w={i === 2 ? 300 : 120} h={30} />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </SettingsSection>
          )}
          <BoardsSection />
          <TagsSection />
          <TeamSection />
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}
```
