### Task 18: End-to-end tests, the full check and the commit

**Files:**
- Create: `web/playwright.config.ts`, `web/e2e/{server.mjs,helpers.ts,global-setup.ts,board.spec.ts,dashboard.spec.ts,settings.spec.ts,a11y.spec.ts}`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above, plus the Go server.
- Produces: `pnpm e2e`: the suite runs against a fresh database in both themes, with axe checks on every page.

- [ ] **Step 1: Install a browser for Playwright**

```bash
cd web && pnpm exec playwright install chromium
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the config, the server launcher and the global setup**

Playwright starts Next (`pnpm build && pnpm start` on :3000) and the Go server (`e2e/server.mjs`, which wipes `e2e/.data` first). The global setup turns Lort's rate limits off (sign-up allows 3 per minute per IP, which the suite exceeds) and seeds the boards (Features, and Bugs which needs approval), the tags and the app name. Every test runs in both themes.

Create `web/playwright.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from "@playwright/test"

// One Lort server (Go on :4052, proxying to Next on :3000) with a fresh database per run.
// Tests share it, so they run one at a time and name what they create uniquely.
export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "e2e",
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  reporter: [["list"]],
  globalSetup: "./e2e/global-setup.ts",
  use: { baseURL: "http://localhost:4052", trace: "retain-on-failure" },
  // Every test runs in both themes (WCAG AA in light and dark, spec section 12).
  projects: [
    { name: "dark", use: { ...devices["Desktop Chrome"], colorScheme: "dark" } },
    { name: "light", use: { ...devices["Desktop Chrome"], colorScheme: "light" } },
  ],
  webServer: [
    {
      command: "pnpm build && pnpm start",
      url: "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
      reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
      timeout: 300_000,
    },
    {
      command: "node e2e/server.mjs",
      url: "http://127.0.0.1:4052/api/lort/health",
      reuseExistingServer: false,
      timeout: 300_000,
    },
  ],
})
```

Create `web/e2e/server.mjs`:

```js
// Starts the Go server for Playwright with a fresh database in e2e/.data.
import { spawn } from "node:child_process"
import { rmSync } from "node:fs"
import { join } from "node:path"

const data = join(import.meta.dirname, ".data")
rmSync(data, { recursive: true, force: true })

const go = spawn("go", ["run", "."], {
  cwd: join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "server"),
  stdio: "inherit",
  shell: process.platform === "win32",
  env: {
    ...process.env,
    LORT_DATA: data,
    LORT_URL: "http://localhost:4052",
    LORT_ADMIN_EMAIL: "admin@lort.test",
    LORT_ADMIN_PASSWORD: "correcthorse-e2e",
  },
})

for (const sig of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"]) process.on(sig, () => go.kill(sig))
go.on("exit", (code) => process.exit(code ?? 0))
```

Create `web/e2e/helpers.ts`:

```ts
import AxeBuilder from "@axe-core/playwright"
import { expect, type APIRequestContext, type Page } from "@playwright/test"

export const API = "http://127.0.0.1:4052"
export const ADMIN = { email: "admin@lort.test", password: "correcthorse-e2e" }

/** A suffix that keeps names unique across tests and both theme projects. */
export const unique = () => `${Date.now().toString(36)}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`

export async function adminToken(request: APIRequestContext): Promise<string> {
  const res = await request.post(`${API}/api/collections/_superusers/auth-with-password`, {
    data: { identity: ADMIN.email, password: ADMIN.password },
  })
  expect(res.ok()).toBeTruthy()
  return (await res.json()).token
}

export async function boardIds(request: APIRequestContext): Promise<{ features: string; bugs: string }> {
  const token = await adminToken(request)
  const res = await request.get(`${API}/api/collections/boards/records?sort=order`, { headers: { Authorization: token } })
  const items: Array<{ id: string; name: string }> = (await res.json()).items
  return { features: items.find((b) => b.name === "Features")!.id, bugs: items.find((b) => b.name === "Bugs")!.id }
}

/** A board user and a request of theirs, made through the API (faster than the UI where the UI isn't under test). */
export async function seedRequest(request: APIRequestContext, title: string, board?: string): Promise<{ id: string; token: string }> {
  const email = `seed-${unique()}@example.com`
  await request.post(`${API}/api/collections/users/records`, {
    data: { email, password: "correcthorse", passwordConfirm: "correcthorse" },
  })
  const auth = await (
    await request.post(`${API}/api/collections/users/auth-with-password`, { data: { identity: email, password: "correcthorse" } })
  ).json()
  const res = await request.post(`${API}/api/collections/requests/records`, {
    headers: { Authorization: auth.token },
    data: { board: board ?? (await boardIds(request)).features, title, body: `Details for ${title}.` },
  })
  expect(res.ok()).toBeTruthy()
  return { id: (await res.json()).id, token: auth.token }
}

export async function signInAdmin(page: Page) {
  await page.goto("/dashboard/login")
  await page.getByLabel("Email").fill(ADMIN.email)
  await page.getByLabel("Password").fill(ADMIN.password)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests/)
}

/** axe on the current page: no WCAG 2.2 A/AA violations. */
export async function expectAccessible(page: Page) {
  const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).withTags(["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21a", "wcag21aa", "wcag22aa"]).analyze()
  expect(results.violations.map((v) => `${v.id}: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(" ")).join(", ")}`)).toEqual([])
}
```

Create `web/e2e/global-setup.ts`:

```ts
// Runs once after both servers are up: turns Lort's rate limits off (sign-up allows 3 per minute
// per IP, which the suite exceeds) and seeds the boards, tags and app name the specs expect.
// schema.Ensure turns the limits back on at every boot, so a normal run is unaffected.
import { ADMIN, API } from "./helpers"

async function call(path: string, init: RequestInit & { token?: string } = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(`${API}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(init.token ? { Authorization: init.token } : {}) },
  })
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${init.method ?? "GET"} ${path}: ${res.status} ${await res.text()}`)
  return res.status === 204 ? null : res.json()
}

export default async function globalSetup() {
  const { token } = await call("/api/collections/_superusers/auth-with-password", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({ identity: ADMIN.email, password: ADMIN.password }),
  })
  await call("/api/settings", { method: "PATCH", token, body: JSON.stringify({ rateLimits: { enabled: false } }) })

  const boards = await call("/api/collections/boards/records", { token })
  if (boards.totalItems === 0) {
    for (const b of [
      { name: "Features", order: 0, isPublic: true, requireApproval: false },
      { name: "Bugs", order: 1, isPublic: true, requireApproval: true },
    ]) {
      await call("/api/collections/boards/records", { method: "POST", token, body: JSON.stringify(b) })
    }
    for (const [i, name] of ["ios", "ui"].entries()) {
      await call("/api/collections/tags/records", { method: "POST", token, body: JSON.stringify({ name, order: i }) })
    }
  }
  const settings = await call("/api/collections/settings/records", { token })
  await call(`/api/collections/settings/records/${settings.items[0].id}`, {
    method: "PATCH",
    token,
    body: JSON.stringify({ appName: "Driftnote", showLandingCard: true }),
  })
}
```

After a click that navigates, always `await page.waitForURL(…)` before reading `page.url()` or asserting on the new page.

Create `web/e2e/board.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { seedRequest, unique } from "./helpers"

test("a visitor signs up inside Suggest something, posts, votes and comments", async ({ page }) => {
  const id = unique()
  const title = `Dark mode ${id}`
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "Got an idea for Driftnote?" })).toBeVisible()

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Suggest something" }).click()
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog")
  await dialog.getByLabel("Email").fill(`visitor-${id}@example.com`)
  await dialog.getByLabel("Password").fill("correcthorse")
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click()

  await dialog.getByLabel("Title").fill(title)
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "ui" }).click()
  await dialog.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Details" }).fill("Writing at night is rough on the eyes.")
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Send" }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/feedback\/[a-z0-9]+$/)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: title })).toBeVisible()

  // The author's dot is already on it; peel it off and stick it back.
  const own = page.getByRole("button", { name: `Remove your upvote from ${title}, 1 vote` })
  await expect(own).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true")
  await own.click()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: `Upvote ${title}, 0 votes` }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: `Remove your upvote from ${title}, 1 vote` })).toBeVisible()

  await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Comment" }).fill("Same on iOS.")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Same on iOS.")).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Reply" }).click()
  await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: /Reply to/ }).fill("Thanks for checking.")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send" }).last().click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Thanks for checking.")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "1 comment" })).toBeVisible()
})

test("similar requests show up while typing a title", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const id = unique()
  await seedRequest(request, `Export entries as markdown ${id}`)
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click()
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByLabel("Email").fill(`similar-${id}@example.com`)
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByLabel("Password").fill("correcthorse")
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toBeHidden()

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Suggest something" }).click()
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByLabel("Title").fill(`markdown ${id}`)
  await expect(page.getByText("Is it one of these? A dot counts the same as a new post.")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog").getByText(`Export entries as markdown ${id}`)).toBeVisible()
})

test("an idea on a board that needs approval waits, visible only to its author", async ({ page, browser }) => {
  const id = unique()
  const title = `Crash on paste ${id}`
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Suggest something" }).click()
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog")
  await dialog.getByLabel("Email").fill(`approval-${id}@example.com`)
  await dialog.getByLabel("Password").fill("correcthorse")
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Create account" }).click()
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Bugs" }).click()
  await dialog.getByLabel("Title").fill(title)
  await dialog.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Details" }).fill("It crashes when I paste a long screenshot.")
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Send" }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/feedback\/[a-z0-9]+$/)

  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await expect(page.getByRole("article").filter({ hasText: title }).getByText("Waiting for approval")).toBeVisible()

  const stranger = await browser.newPage()
  await stranger.goto("/feedback")
  await expect(stranger.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(stranger.getByText(title)).toHaveCount(0)
  await stranger.close()
})

test("filters live in the URL", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Newest" }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/sort=newest/)
  await page.reload()
  await expect(page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Newest" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true")
})
```

Create `web/e2e/dashboard.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test"
import { seedRequest, signInAdmin, unique } from "./helpers"

async function openRequest(page: Page, title: string) {
  await page.goto("/dashboard/requests?status=all")
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: title }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests\/[a-z0-9]+/)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: title })).toBeVisible()
}

const triage = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("toolbar", { name: "Triage" })

test("the team moves a request through every status, with undo", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const title = `Calendar heatmap ${unique()}`
  await seedRequest(request, title)
  await signInAdmin(page)
  await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: title })).toBeVisible()
  await openRequest(page, title)

  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Accept" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: Reviewing" })).toBeVisible()

  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Add to roadmap" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Added to the roadmap")).toBeVisible()

  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Re-review" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Taken off the roadmap")).toBeVisible()
  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Add to roadmap" }).click()
  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Start working" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: In progress" })).toBeVisible()

  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Complete" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Marked as completed")).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Undo" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Moved back to In progress")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: In progress" })).toBeVisible()

  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Complete" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: Completed" })).toBeVisible()
  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Reopen" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: In progress" })).toBeVisible()
})

test("closing and reopening, and the status chip menu", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const title = `Share as image ${unique()}`
  await seedRequest(request, title)
  await signInAdmin(page)
  await openRequest(page, title)
  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Close" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: Closed" })).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: Closed" }).click()
  await page.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Reopen" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Status: Pending" })).toBeVisible()
})

test("merging a duplicate moves it and leaves a pointer", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const id = unique()
  const target = `Dark theme ${id}`
  const source = `Night mode ${id}`
  await seedRequest(request, target)
  const src = await seedRequest(request, source)
  await signInAdmin(page)
  await openRequest(page, source)
  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Merge" }).click()
  await page.getByPlaceholder("Search by title").fill(`Dark theme ${id}`)
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: new RegExp(`Dark theme ${id}`) }).click()
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("button", { name: "Merge", exact: true }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests\/[a-z0-9]+/)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: target })).toBeVisible()

  await page.goto(`/feedback/${src.id}`)
  await expect(page.getByText(`Merged into ${target}`)).toBeVisible()
})

test("internal notes stay inside the team, and Skip opens the next request", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const id = unique()
  const first = await seedRequest(request, `Widget ${id} one`)
  await seedRequest(request, `Widget ${id} two`)
  await signInAdmin(page)
  await page.goto(`/dashboard/requests?q=${encodeURIComponent(`Widget ${id}`)}&sort=oldest`)
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: `Widget ${id} one` }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests\/[a-z0-9]+/)
  await expect(page.getByText("1 of 2 open")).toBeVisible()

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Visibility: Public/ }).click()
  await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Internal note" }).fill(`Reproduced ${id}`)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Send" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Only the team sees this")).toBeVisible()

  await triage(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Skip" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: `Widget ${id} two` })).toBeVisible()

  await page.goto(`/feedback/${first.id}`)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: `Widget ${id} one` })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText(`Reproduced ${id}`)).toHaveCount(0)
})

test("a request posted for someone else carries their name", async ({ page }) => {
  const id = unique()
  await signInAdmin(page)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "New request" }).click()
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog")
  await dialog.getByLabel("Title").fill(`Offline mode ${id}`)
  await dialog.getByLabel("On behalf of (optional)").fill(`priya-${id}@example.com`)
  await dialog.getByRole("textbox", { name: "Details" }).fill("Asked for this on a call.")
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "Send" }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests\/[a-z0-9]+/)
  await expect(page.getByText(`priya-${id} posted this in Features`)).toBeVisible()
})
```

Create `web/e2e/settings.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { signInAdmin, unique } from "./helpers"

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await signInAdmin(page)
  await page.goto("/dashboard/settings")
})

test("renaming the app changes the board", async ({ page }) => {
  const name = `Driftnote ${unique()}`
  const field = page.getByLabel("App name")
  await field.fill(name)
  await field.press("Enter")
  await expect(page.getByText("Saved the app name.")).toBeVisible()
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: `Got an idea for ${name}?` })).toBeVisible()

  await page.goto("/dashboard/settings")
  await page.getByLabel("App name").fill("Driftnote")
  await page.getByLabel("App name").press("Enter")
  await expect(page.getByText("Saved the app name.")).toBeVisible()
})

test("boards can be added, renamed, reordered with the keyboard and deleted", async ({ page }) => {
  const name = `Ideas ${unique()}`
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add board" }).click()
  const field = page.getByLabel("Name of New board")
  await field.fill(name)
  await field.press("Enter")
  await expect(page.getByText("Renamed the board.")).toBeVisible()

  // Space lifts, arrows move, Space drops (dnd-kit's keyboard sensor).
  const grip = page.getByRole("button", { name: `Reorder ${name}` })
  await grip.focus()
  await page.keyboard.press("Space")
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) await page.keyboard.press("ArrowUp")
  await page.keyboard.press("Space")
  await expect(page.locator("#boards").getByText("Default")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Name of Features")).toBeVisible()
  const rows = page.locator("#boards input[aria-label^='Name of']")
  await expect(rows.first()).toHaveValue(name)

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: `Delete ${name}` }).click()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete board" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText(`Deleted ${name}.`)).toBeVisible()
  await expect(rows.first()).toHaveValue("Features")
})

test("tags can be added and deleted", async ({ page }) => {
  const tag = `android${unique()}`
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add a tag" }).click()
  await page.getByLabel("New tag").fill(tag)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Added the tag.")).toBeVisible()
  await page.locator("#tags").getByRole("button", { name: tag, exact: true }).click()
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.locator("#tags").getByRole("button", { name: tag, exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
})

test("the landing card can be switched off", async ({ page }) => {
  const toggle = page.getByLabel("Show “Get your own board” card")
  await toggle.click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Hid the card.")).toBeVisible()
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("Get your own board like this")).toHaveCount(0)

  await page.goto("/dashboard/settings")
  await page.getByLabel("Show “Get your own board” card").click()
  await expect(page.getByText("The card is back.")).toBeVisible()
})

test("an invited admin sets a password and lands in the dashboard", async ({ page, browser }) => {
  const email = `sofia-${unique()}@example.com`
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Invite an admin" }).click()
  await page.getByLabel("Their email").fill(email)
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create invite link" }).click()
  const link = await page.getByLabel("Invite link").inputValue()
  expect(link).toContain("/dashboard/invite?token=")

  const invitee = await browser.newPage()
  await invitee.goto(link.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+/, ""))
  await expect(invitee.getByText(`You're joining as ${email}.`)).toBeVisible()
  await invitee.getByLabel("Choose a password").fill("correcthorse-sofia")
  await invitee.getByLabel("Type it again").fill("correcthorse-sofia")
  await invitee.getByRole("button", { name: "Join the team" }).click()
  await invitee.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests/)
  await expect(invitee.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "Requests" })).toBeVisible()
  await invitee.close()
})
```

Create `web/e2e/a11y.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { expectAccessible, seedRequest, signInAdmin, unique } from "./helpers"

// axe on every 1B page, in both themes (the projects in playwright.config.ts).

test("public pages", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const { id } = await seedRequest(request, `Accessible idea ${unique()}`)
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await expect(page.getByRole("article").first()).toBeVisible()
  await expectAccessible(page)
  await page.goto(`/feedback/${id}`)
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible()
  await expectAccessible(page)
  await page.goto("/feedback")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Suggest something" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toBeVisible()
  await expectAccessible(page)
})

test("dashboard pages", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const title = `Accessible request ${unique()}`
  await seedRequest(request, title)
  await page.goto("/dashboard/login")
  await expectAccessible(page)
  await signInAdmin(page)
  await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: title })).toBeVisible()
  await expectAccessible(page)
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: title }).click()
  await page.waitForURL(/\/dashboard\/requests\/[a-z0-9]+/)
  await expect(page.getByRole("toolbar", { name: "Triage" })).toBeVisible()
  await expectAccessible(page)
  await page.goto("/dashboard/settings")
  await expect(page.getByLabel("App name")).toBeVisible()
  await expectAccessible(page)
})
```


- [ ] **Step 4: Run the suite twice over**

```bash
cd web && pnpm e2e --repeat-each=2
```

Expected: every test passes in both projects. `--repeat-each=2` flushes out waits that only pass by luck. If axe reports a contrast problem, fix the token or the class, not the test.

- [ ] **Step 5: Run everything**

```bash
cd server && gofmt -l . && go vet ./... && go test ./...
```

```bash
cd web && pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test && pnpm build
```

Expected: no gofmt output; every Go package `ok`; the web checks pass and the build lists `/`, `/feedback`, `/feedback/[id]`, `/reset-password`, `/dashboard`, `/dashboard/login`, `/dashboard/invite`, `/dashboard/requests`, `/dashboard/requests/[id]` and `/dashboard/settings`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

One commit for the phase:

```bash
git add server web
git commit -m "Add the Lort web app: public board, request pages, accounts and the dashboard (Phase 1B)"
```

Then open the pull request. The owner's two review rounds and the docs commit follow, as `CLAUDE.md` describes.

---

## Self-review notes

Checked against the spec's Phase 1 web scope (sections 5, 6.1, 6.2, 6.6 and 14–15):

- **Public board** (5.1): headline and intro, one column of cards, top-bar search (FTS), floating bar with sort tabs, board drop-up (hidden with one board), status drop-up ("Open ideas"), Suggest something; filters in the URL; infinite scroll of 20; "Waiting for approval" for the author: Tasks 9, 10, 12.
- **Request page** (5.1): title, author, sticker ("Stick a dot"), body, images, status as a timeline entry, one level of replies, Team badge, composer for signed-in users, no voter avatars, "Merged into" banner: Task 13.
- **Accounts** (5.1): email and password sign-up, no verify step while email is off, profile dialog with name, avatar and sign out; reset page for the team's reset links: Task 10.
- **Suggest something** (5.2): board pills, title with similar requests (3+ characters, 300 ms, up to 3), tags, details composer with images, admin Public/Internal, approval boards, author's vote, sign-in inside the modal: Task 11 (server: Phase 1A plus Task 2's test).
- **Votes** (5.3): optimistic with rollback, sign-in first, read-only on closed or merged: Tasks 10, 12, 13.
- **Freshness** (5.6): realtime with a 300 ms debounce, new-item tint, 10-second refresh limit, 3-minute polling while disconnected: Tasks 8, 12, 15.
- **Anti-spam** (5.7): honeypot on sign-up and suggest; 429s become a friendly toast: Tasks 8, 10, 11.
- **Dashboard requests** (6.1): counts, Live, refresh, New request (with "on behalf of"), URL filters, icon-only status with tooltip, New and Hidden markers, unseen tint, sidebar badge: Tasks 2, 14, 15.
- **Dashboard request** (6.2): breadcrumb and queue position, editable chips (status, board, tags, add tag, visibility), Public/Internal composer, internal notes, avatar popovers (reset link, ban), Edit and Delete, the triage bar with Merge, Skip, roadmap toasts and the 60-second Undo: Tasks 1, 2, 16.
- **Settings** (6.6): Branding (logo, name, 8 presets plus custom hex, landing card), Boards (reorder, public, needs approval, add, rename, delete with move), Tags (add, rename, reorder, delete), Team (invite link, remove): Tasks 2, 3, 17.
- **Visual system** (14): tokens, fonts, owner's colour with contrast adjustment, the components, skeletons per page, reduced motion, focus rings: Tasks 4–9 and every page task.
- **Testing** (12): Go unit and integration tests for the new endpoints; Vitest for filters, optimistic votes, colour contrast, composer limits and the auth render loop; Playwright for sign-up, suggest with similar, vote, comment, triage through every status with undo, merge, settings, in both themes with axe: Tasks 1–3, 5–17, 18.

Deliberately not in 1B (per spec section 13): roadmap and changelog (Phase 2); email, verification and notification settings (Phase 3); webhooks, MCP and keys (Phase 4); voice, widget, Turnstile, backups and updates (Phase 5); `/lort-landing` and docs (Phase 6); the Dockerfile, setup scripts and CI (plan 1C).

The plan's code was checked before writing: the Go additions were built and `go test ./...` passed against PocketBase v0.40.5; the web code was scaffolded with the exact `shadcn init` command above, then type-checked, linted, unit-tested (43 tests) and built with Next 16.4; the Go server and the built app were run together and driven in a browser (sign-up inside Suggest something, posting, the dashboard table, Accept → Add to roadmap → Start working → Complete → Undo, renaming the app in Settings). The Playwright specs type-check but were not run during planning.
